===== M0011 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.getGreatestSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 600-606
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the greatest subtype of {@code this} and {@code that}.
   * The greatest subtype is the meet (&#8743;) or infimum of both types in the
   * type lattice.<p>
   * Examples
   * <ul>
   * <li>{@code Number &#8743; Any} = {@code Any}</li>
   * <li>{@code number &#8743; Object} = {@code Any}</li>
   * <li>{@code Number &#8743; Object} = {@code Number}</li>
   * </ul>
   * @return {@code this &#8744; that}
   */
Method code:
600 |   public JSType getGreatestSubtype(JSType that) {
601 |      if (that.isRecordType()) {
602 |       // Record types have their own implementation of getGreatestSubtype.
603 |       return that.getGreatestSubtype(this);
604 |     }
605 |     return getGreatestSubtype(this, that);
606 |   }

===== M0019 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.resolveStubDeclarations()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1325-1355
Documentation:
/**
     * Resolve any stub delcarations to unknown types if we could not
     * find types for them during traversal.
     */
Method code:
1325 |     void resolveStubDeclarations() {
1326 |       for (StubDeclaration stub : stubDeclarations) {
1327 |         Node n = stub.node;
1328 |         Node parent = n.getParent();
1329 |         String qName = n.getQualifiedName();
1330 |         String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
1331 |         String ownerName = stub.ownerName;
1332 |         boolean isExtern = stub.isExtern;
1333 | 
1334 |         if (scope.isDeclared(qName, false)) {
1335 |           continue;
1336 |         }
1337 | 
1338 |         // If we see a stub property, make sure to register this property
1339 |         // in the type registry.
1340 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1341 |         ObjectType unknownType = typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1342 |         defineSlot(n, parent, unknownType, true);
1343 | 
1344 |         if (ownerType != null &&
1345 |             (isExtern || ownerType.isFunctionPrototypeType())) {
1346 |           // If this is a stub for a prototype, just declare it
1347 |           // as an unknown type. These are seen often in externs.
1348 |           ownerType.defineInferredProperty(
1349 |               propName, unknownType, isExtern);
1350 |         } else {
1351 |           typeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(
1352 |               propName, ownerType == null ? unknownType : ownerType);
1353 |         }
1354 |       }
1355 |     }

===== M0020 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeValidator.expectUndeclaredVariable(String,Node,Node,Var,String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeValidator.java
Original range: 463-518
Documentation:
/**
   * Expect that the given variable has not been declared with a type.
   *
   * @param sourceName The name of the source file we're in.
   * @param n The node where warnings should point to.
   * @param parent The parent of {@code n}.
   * @param var The variable that we're checking.
   * @param variableName The name of the variable.
   * @param newType The type being applied to the variable. Mostly just here
   *     for the benefit of the warning.
   */
Method code:
463 |   void expectUndeclaredVariable(String sourceName, Node n, Node parent, Var var,
464 |       String variableName, JSType newType) {
465 |     boolean allowDupe = false;
466 |     if (n.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
467 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
468 |       if (info == null) {
469 |         info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
470 |       }
471 |       allowDupe =
472 |           info != null && info.getSuppressions().contains("duplicate");
473 |     }
474 | 
475 |     JSType varType = var.getType();
476 | 
477 |     // Only report duplicate declarations that have types. Other duplicates
478 |     // will be reported by the syntactic scope creator later in the
479 |     // compilation process.
480 |     if (varType != null &&
481 |         varType != typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) &&
482 |         newType != null &&
483 |         newType != typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE)) {
484 |       // If there are two typed declarations of the same variable, that
485 |       // is an error and the second declaration is ignored, except in the
486 |       // case of native types. A null input type means that the declaration
487 |       // was made in TypedScopeCreator#createInitialScope and is a
488 |       // native type.
489 |       if (var.input == null) {
490 |         n.setJSType(varType);
491 |         if (parent.getType() == Token.VAR) {
492 |           if (n.getFirstChild() != null) {
493 |             n.getFirstChild().setJSType(varType);
494 |           }
495 |         } else {
496 |           Preconditions.checkState(parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
497 |           parent.setJSType(varType);
498 |         }
499 |       } else {
500 |         // Always warn about duplicates if the overridden type does not
501 |         // match the original type.
502 |         //
503 |         // If the types match, suppress the warning iff there was a @suppress
504 |         // tag, or if the original declaration was a stub.
505 |         if (!(allowDupe ||
506 |               var.getParentNode().getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT) ||
507 |             !newType.equals(varType)) {
508 |           if (shouldReport) {
509 |             compiler.report(
510 |                 JSError.make(sourceName, n, DUP_VAR_DECLARATION,
511 |                     variableName, newType.toString(), var.getInputName(),
512 |                     String.valueOf(var.nameNode.getLineno()),
513 |                     varType.toString()));
514 |           }
515 |         }
516 |       }
517 |     }
518 |   }

===== M0037 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.functionCallHasSideEffects(Node,AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 734-790
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if calls to this function have side effects.
   *
   * @param callNode The call node to inspected.
   * @param compiler A compiler object to provide program state changing
   *     context information. Can be null.
   */
Method code:
734 |   static boolean functionCallHasSideEffects(
735 |       Node callNode, @Nullable AbstractCompiler compiler) {
736 |     if (callNode.getType() != Token.CALL) {
737 |       throw new IllegalStateException(
738 |           "Expected CALL node, got " + Token.name(callNode.getType()));
739 |     }
740 | 
741 |     if (callNode.isNoSideEffectsCall()) {
742 |       return false;
743 |     }
744 | 
745 |     Node nameNode = callNode.getFirstChild();
746 | 
747 |     // Built-in functions with no side effects.
748 |     if (nameNode.getType() == Token.NAME) {
749 |       String name = nameNode.getString();
750 |       if (BUILTIN_FUNCTIONS_WITHOUT_SIDEEFFECTS.contains(name)) {
751 |         return false;
752 |       }
753 |     } else if (nameNode.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
754 |       if (callNode.hasOneChild()
755 |           && OBJECT_METHODS_WITHOUT_SIDEEFFECTS.contains(
756 |                 nameNode.getLastChild().getString())) {
757 |         return false;
758 |       }
759 | 
760 |       if (callNode.isOnlyModifiesThisCall()
761 |           && evaluatesToLocalValue(nameNode.getFirstChild())) {
762 |         return false;
763 |       }
764 | 
765 |       // Functions in the "Math" namespace have no side effects.
766 |       if (nameNode.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NAME) {
767 |         String namespaceName = nameNode.getFirstChild().getString();
768 |         if (namespaceName.equals("Math")) {
769 |           return false;
770 |         }
771 |       }
772 | 
773 |       if (compiler != null && !compiler.hasRegExpGlobalReferences()) {
774 |         if (nameNode.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.REGEXP
775 |             && REGEXP_METHODS.contains(nameNode.getLastChild().getString())) {
776 |           return false;
777 |         } else if (nameNode.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.STRING
778 |             && STRING_REGEXP_METHODS.contains(
779 |                 nameNode.getLastChild().getString())) {
780 |           Node param = nameNode.getNext();
781 |           if (param != null &&
782 |               (param.getType() == Token.STRING
783 |                   || param.getType() == Token.REGEXP))
784 |           return false;
785 |         }
786 |       }
787 |     }
788 | 
789 |     return true;
790 |   }

Method code:
123 |   public static Node transformTree(AstRoot node,

134 |           irFactory.handlePossibleFileOverviewJsDoc(comment);

===== M0048 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverseName(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 632-671
Documentation:

Method code:
632 |   private FlowScope traverseName(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
633 |     String varName = n.getString();
634 |     Node value = n.getFirstChild();
635 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
636 |     if (value != null) {
637 |       scope = traverse(value, scope);
638 |       updateScopeForTypeChange(scope, n, n.getJSType() /* could be null */,
639 |           getJSType(value));
640 |       return scope;
641 |     } else {
642 |       StaticSlot<JSType> var = scope.getSlot(varName);
643 |       if (var != null) {
644 |         // There are two situations where we don't want to use type information
645 |         // from the scope, even if we have it.
646 | 
647 |         // 1) The var is escaped in a weird way, e.g.,
648 |         // function f() { var x = 3; function g() { x = null } (x); }
649 |         boolean isInferred = var.isTypeInferred();
650 |         boolean unflowable =
651 |             isInferred && unflowableVarNames.contains(varName);
652 | 
653 |         // 2) We're reading type information from another scope for an
654 |         // inferred variable.
655 |         // var t = null; function f() { (t); }
656 |         boolean nonLocalInferredSlot =
657 |             isInferred &&
658 |             syntacticScope.getParent() != null &&
659 |             var == syntacticScope.getParent().getSlot(varName);
660 | 
661 |         if (!unflowable && !nonLocalInferredSlot) {
662 |           type = var.getType();
663 |           if (type == null) {
664 |             type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
665 |           }
666 |         }
667 |       }
668 |     }
669 |     n.setJSType(type);
670 |     return scope;
671 |   }

Documentation:

Method code:
559 |   public Node getChildBefore(Node child) {
560 |     if (child == first) {
561 |       return null;
562 |     }
563 |     Node n = first;
564 |     while (n.next != child) {
565 |       n = n.next;
566 |       if (n == null) {
567 |         throw new RuntimeException("node is not a child");
568 |       }
569 |     }
570 |     return n;
571 |   }

   * Create the annotation names from the user-specified
   * annotation whitelist.
   */
Method code:
75 |   private static Map<String, Annotation> buildAnnotationNames(
76 |       Set<String> annotationWhitelist) {
77 |     ImmutableMap.Builder<String, Annotation> annotationBuilder =
78 |         ImmutableMap.builder();
79 |     annotationBuilder.putAll(Annotation.recognizedAnnotations);

   * state to {@link State#SEARCHING_ANNOTATION}.
   */

2085 |   private JsDocToken eatTokensUntilEOL(JsDocToken token) {
2086 |     do {

2093 |     } while (true);

412 |     @Override
413 |     public final boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal nodeTraversal, Node n,
414 |         Node parent) {
415 |       if (n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION ||
416 |           n.getType() == Token.SCRIPT) {

===== M0105 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitVar(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1248-1281
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a VAR node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1248 |   private void visitVar(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1249 |     // TODO(nicksantos): Fix this so that the doc info always shows up
1250 |     // on the NAME node. We probably want to wait for the parser
1251 |     // merge to fix this.
1252 |     JSDocInfo varInfo = n.hasOneChild() ? n.getJSDocInfo() : null;
1253 |     for (Node name : n.children()) {
1254 |       Node value = name.getFirstChild();
1255 |       // A null var would indicate a bug in the scope creation logic.
1256 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(name.getString());
1257 | 
1258 |       if (value != null) {
1259 |         JSType valueType = getJSType(value);
1260 |         JSType nameType = var.getType();
1261 |         nameType = (nameType == null) ? getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : nameType;
1262 | 
1263 |         JSDocInfo info = name.getJSDocInfo();
1264 |         if (info == null) {
1265 |           info = varInfo;
1266 |         }
1267 |         if (info != null && info.hasEnumParameterType()) {
1268 |           // var.getType() can never be null, this would indicate a bug in the
1269 |           // scope creation logic.
1270 |           checkEnumInitializer(
1271 |               t, value,
1272 |               info.getEnumParameterType().evaluate(t.getScope(), typeRegistry));
1273 |         } else if (var.isTypeInferred()) {
1274 |           ensureTyped(t, name, valueType);
1275 |         } else {
1276 |           validator.expectCanAssignTo(
1277 |               t, value, valueType, nameType, "initializing variable");
1278 |         }
1279 |       }
1280 |     }
1281 |   }

/** Gets the minimum number of arguments that this function requires. */
Method code:
210 |   public int getMinArguments() {
211 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): There are some native functions that have optional
212 |     // parameters before required parameters. This algorithm finds the position
213 |     // of the last required parameter.
214 |     int i = 0;
215 |     int min = 0;

220 |       }
221 |     }

===== M0111 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PureFunctionIdentifier.markPureFunctionCalls()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PureFunctionIdentifier.java
Original range: 307-365
Documentation:
/**
   * Set no side effect property at pure-function call sites.
   */
Method code:
307 |   private void markPureFunctionCalls() {
308 |     for (Node callNode : allFunctionCalls) {
309 |       Node name = callNode.getFirstChild();
310 |       Collection<Definition> defs =
311 |           getCallableDefinitions(definitionProvider, name);
312 |       // Default to side effects, non-local results
313 |       Node.SideEffectFlags flags = new Node.SideEffectFlags();
314 |       if (defs == null) {
315 |         flags.setMutatesGlobalState();
316 |         flags.setThrows();
317 |         flags.setReturnsTainted();
318 |       } else {
319 |         flags.clearAllFlags();
320 |         for (Definition def : defs) {
321 |           FunctionInformation functionInfo =
322 |               functionSideEffectMap.get(def.getRValue());
323 |           Preconditions.checkNotNull(functionInfo);
324 |           // TODO(johnlenz): set the arguments separately from the
325 |           // global state flag.
326 |           if (functionInfo.mutatesGlobalState()) {
327 |             flags.setMutatesGlobalState();
328 |           }
329 | 
330 |           if (functionInfo.functionThrows) {
331 |             flags.setThrows();
332 |           }
333 | 
334 |           if (!NodeUtil.isNew(callNode)) {
335 |             if (functionInfo.taintsThis) {
336 |               flags.setMutatesThis();
337 |             }
338 |           }
339 | 
340 |           if (functionInfo.taintsReturn) {
341 |             flags.setReturnsTainted();
342 |           }
343 | 
344 |           if (flags.areAllFlagsSet()) {
345 |             break;
346 |           }
347 |         }
348 |       }
349 | 
350 |       // Handle special cases (Math, RegEx)
351 |       if (NodeUtil.isCall(callNode)) {
352 |         Preconditions.checkState(compiler != null);
353 |         if (!NodeUtil.functionCallHasSideEffects(callNode, compiler)) {
354 |           flags.clearSideEffectFlags();
355 |         }
356 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isNew(callNode)) {
357 |         // Handle known cases now (Object, Date, RegExp, etc)
358 |         if (!NodeUtil.constructorCallHasSideEffects(callNode)) {
359 |           flags.clearSideEffectFlags();
360 |         }
361 |       }
362 | 
363 |       callNode.setSideEffectFlags(flags.valueOf());
364 |     }
365 |   }

===== M0112 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitAssign(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 822-927
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits an assignment <code>lvalue = rvalue</code>. If the
   * <code>lvalue</code> is a prototype modification, we change the schema
   * of the object type it is referring to.
   * @param t the traversal
   * @param assign the assign node
   * (<code>assign.getType() == Token.ASSIGN</code> is an implicit invariant)
   */
Method code:
822 |   private void visitAssign(NodeTraversal t, Node assign) {
823 |     JSDocInfo info = assign.getJSDocInfo();
824 |     Node lvalue = assign.getFirstChild();
825 |     Node rvalue = assign.getLastChild();
826 | 
827 |     if (lvalue.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
828 |       Node object = lvalue.getFirstChild();
829 |       JSType objectJsType = getJSType(object);
830 |       String property = lvalue.getLastChild().getString();
831 | 
832 |       // the first name in this getprop refers to an interface
833 |       // we perform checks in addition to the ones below
834 |       if (object.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
835 |         JSType jsType = getJSType(object.getFirstChild());
836 |         if (jsType.isInterface() &&
837 |             object.getLastChild().getString().equals("prototype")) {
838 |           visitInterfaceGetprop(t, assign, object, property, lvalue, rvalue);
839 |         }
840 |       }
841 | 
842 |       // /** @type ... */object.name = ...;
843 |       if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
844 |         visitAnnotatedAssignGetprop(t, assign,
845 |             info.getType().evaluate(t.getScope(), typeRegistry), object,
846 |             property, rvalue);
847 |         return;
848 |       }
849 | 
850 |       // /** @enum ... */object.name = ...;
851 |       if (info != null && info.hasEnumParameterType()) {
852 |         checkEnumInitializer(
853 |             t, rvalue, info.getEnumParameterType().evaluate(
854 |                 t.getScope(), typeRegistry));
855 |         return;
856 |       }
857 | 
858 |       // object.prototype = ...;
859 |       if (property.equals("prototype")) {
860 |         if (objectJsType instanceof FunctionType) {
861 |           FunctionType functionType = (FunctionType) objectJsType;
862 |           if (functionType.isConstructor()) {
863 |             JSType rvalueType = rvalue.getJSType();
864 |             validator.expectObject(t, rvalue, rvalueType,
865 |                 OVERRIDING_PROTOTYPE_WITH_NON_OBJECT);
866 |           }
867 |         } else {
868 |           // TODO(user): might want to flag that
869 |         }
870 |         return;
871 |       }
872 | 
873 |       // object.prototype.property = ...;
874 |       if (object.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
875 |         Node object2 = object.getFirstChild();
876 |         String property2 = NodeUtil.getStringValue(object.getLastChild());
877 | 
878 |         if ("prototype".equals(property2)) {
879 |           JSType jsType = object2.getJSType();
880 |           if (jsType instanceof FunctionType) {
881 |             FunctionType functionType = (FunctionType) jsType;
882 |             if (functionType.isConstructor() || functionType.isInterface()) {
883 |               checkDeclaredPropertyInheritance(
884 |                   t, assign, functionType, property, info, getJSType(rvalue));
885 |             }
886 |           } else {
887 |             // TODO(user): might want to flag that
888 |           }
889 |           return;
890 |         }
891 |       }
892 | 
893 |       // object.property = ...;
894 |       ObjectType type = ObjectType.cast(
895 |           objectJsType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
896 |       if (type != null) {
897 |         if (type.hasProperty(property) &&
898 |             !type.isPropertyTypeInferred(property) &&
899 |             !propertyIsImplicitCast(type, property)) {
900 |           validator.expectCanAssignToPropertyOf(
901 |               t, assign, getJSType(rvalue),
902 |               type.getPropertyType(property), object, property);
903 |         }
904 |         return;
905 |       }
906 |     } else if (lvalue.getType() == Token.NAME) {
907 |       // variable with inferred type case
908 |       JSType rvalueType = getJSType(assign.getLastChild());
909 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(lvalue.getString());
910 |       if (var != null) {
911 |         if (var.isTypeInferred()) {
912 |           return;
913 |         }
914 |       }
915 |     }
916 | 
917 |     // fall through case
918 |     JSType leftType = getJSType(lvalue);
919 |     Node rightChild = assign.getLastChild();
920 |     JSType rightType = getJSType(rightChild);
921 |     if (validator.expectCanAssignTo(
922 |             t, assign, rightType, leftType, "assignment")) {
923 |       ensureTyped(t, assign, rightType);
924 |     } else {
925 |       ensureTyped(t, assign);
926 |     }
927 |   }

/**
   * Parses a {@link JSDocInfo} object. This parsing method reads all tokens
   * returned by the {@link JsDocTokenStream#getJsDocToken()} method until the
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOC} is returned.
   *
   * @return {@code true} if JSDoc information was correctly parsed,
   *     {@code false} otherwise
   */

773 |                       case DEFINE:
774 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDefineType(type)) {
775 |                           parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.define",
776 |                               lineno, charno);
777 |                         }
778 |                         break;
779 | 
780 |                       case RETURN:
781 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordReturnType(type)) {
782 |                           parser.addWarning(
783 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
784 |                           break;
785 |                         }
786 | 
787 |                         // *Update* the token to that after the type annotation.
788 |                         token = current();
789 | 
790 |                         // Find the return's description (if applicable).
791 |                         if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
792 |                           ExtractionInfo returnDescriptionInfo =
793 |                               extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
794 | 
795 |                           String returnDescription =
796 |                               returnDescriptionInfo.string;
797 | 
798 |                           if (returnDescription.length() > 0) {
799 |                             jsdocBuilder.recordReturnDescription(
800 |                                 returnDescription);
801 |                           }
802 | 
803 |                           token = returnDescriptionInfo.token;
804 |                         } else {
805 |                           token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
806 |                         }
807 |                         continue retry;
808 | 
809 |                       case THIS:
810 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordThisType(type)) {
811 |                           parser.addWarning(
812 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
813 |                         }
814 |                         break;
815 | 
816 |                       case TYPE:
817 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordType(type)) {
818 |                           parser.addWarning(
819 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
820 |                         }
821 |                         break;
822 | 
823 |                       case TYPEDEF:
824 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTypedef(type)) {
825 |                           parser.addWarning(
826 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
827 |                         }
828 |                         break;
829 |                     }
830 | 
831 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
832 |                   }
833 |                   continue retry;
834 |               }
835 |             }
836 |           }
837 |           break;
838 | 
839 |         case EOC:
840 |           if (hasParsedFileOverviewDocInfo()) {
841 |             fileOverviewJSDocInfo = retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
842 |           }
843 | 
844 |           return true;
845 | 
846 |         case EOF:
847 |           // discard any accumulated information
848 |           jsdocBuilder.build(null);
849 |           parser.addWarning("msg.unexpected.eof",
850 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
851 |           return false;
852 | 
853 |         case EOL:
854 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE) {
855 |             state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
856 |           }
857 |           token = next();
858 |           continue retry;
859 | 
860 |         default:
861 |           if (token == JsDocToken.STAR && state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
862 |             token = next();
863 |             continue retry;
864 |           } else {
865 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
866 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
867 |             continue retry;
868 |           }
869 |       }
870 | 
871 |       // next token
872 |       token = next();
873 |     }
874 |   }

===== M0116 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFollowNode(Node,Node,ControlFlowAnalysis)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 675-772
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the follow() node of a given node and its parent. There is a side
   * effect when calling this function. If this function computed an edge that
   * exists a FINALLY, it'll attempt to connect the fromNode to the outer
   * FINALLY according to the finallyMap.
   *
   * @param fromNode The original source node since {@code node} is changed
   *        during recursion.
   * @param node The node that follow() should compute.
   */
Method code:
675 |   private static Node computeFollowNode(
676 |       Node fromNode, Node node, ControlFlowAnalysis cfa) {
677 |     /*
678 |      * This is the case where:
679 |      *
680 |      * 1. Parent is null implies that we are transferring control to the end of
681 |      * the script.
682 |      *
683 |      * 2. Parent is a function implies that we are transferring control back to
684 |      * the caller of the function.
685 |      *
686 |      * 3. If the node is a return statement, we should also transfer control
687 |      * back to the caller of the function.
688 |      *
689 |      * 4. If the node is root then we have reached the end of what we have been
690 |      * asked to traverse.
691 |      *
692 |      * In all cases we should transfer control to a "symbolic return" node.
693 |      * This will make life easier for DFAs.
694 |      */
695 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
696 |     if (parent == null || parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION ||
697 |         (cfa != null && node == cfa.root)) {
698 |       return null;
699 |     }
700 | 
701 |     // If we are just before a IF/WHILE/DO/FOR:
702 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
703 |       // The follow() of any of the path from IF would be what follows IF.
704 |       case Token.IF:
705 |         return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
706 |       case Token.CASE:
707 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
708 |         // After the body of a CASE, the control goes to the body of the next
709 |         // case, without having to go to the case condition.
710 |         if (parent.getNext() != null) {
711 |           if (parent.getNext().getType() == Token.CASE) {
712 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild().getNext();
713 |           } else if (parent.getNext().getType() == Token.DEFAULT) {
714 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild();
715 |           } else {
716 |             Preconditions.checkState(false, "Not reachable");
717 |           }
718 |         } else {
719 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
720 |         }
721 |         break;
722 |       case Token.FOR:
723 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
724 |           return parent;
725 |         } else {
726 |           return parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
727 |         }
728 |       case Token.WHILE:
729 |       case Token.DO:
730 |         return parent;
731 |       case Token.TRY:
732 |         // If we are coming out of the TRY block...
733 |         if (parent.getFirstChild() == node) {
734 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
735 |             return computeFallThrough(parent.getLastChild());
736 |           } else { // and have no FINALLY.
737 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
738 |           }
739 |         // CATCH block.
740 |         } else if (NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node){
741 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
742 |             return computeFallThrough(node.getNext());
743 |           } else {
744 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
745 |           }
746 |         // If we are coming out of the FINALLY block...
747 |         } else if (parent.getLastChild() == node){
748 |           if (cfa != null) {
749 |             for (Node finallyNode : cfa.finallyMap.get(parent)) {
750 |               cfa.createEdge(fromNode, Branch.UNCOND, finallyNode);
751 |             }
752 |           }
753 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
754 |         }
755 |     }
756 | 
757 |     // Now that we are done with the special cases follow should be its
758 |     // immediate sibling, unless its sibling is a function
759 |     Node nextSibling = node.getNext();
760 | 
761 |     // Skip function declarations because control doesn't get pass into it.
762 |     while (nextSibling != null && nextSibling.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
763 |       nextSibling = nextSibling.getNext();
764 |     }
765 | 
766 |     if (nextSibling != null) {
767 |       return computeFallThrough(nextSibling);
768 |     } else {
769 |       // If there are no more siblings, control is transfered up the AST.
770 |       return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
771 |     }
772 |   }

83 |         new LinkedDirectedGraphEdge<N, E>(src, edgeValue, dest);

===== M0126 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DefinitionsRemover.getDefinition(Node,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DefinitionsRemover.java
Original range: 36-64
Documentation:
/**
   * @return an {@link Definition} object if the node contains a definition or
   *     {@code null} otherwise.
   */
Method code:
36 |   static Definition getDefinition(Node n, boolean isExtern) {
37 |     // TODO(user): Since we have parent pointers handy. A lot of constructors
38 |     // can be simplified.
39 | 
40 |     // This logic must match #isDefinitionNode
41 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
42 |     if (parent == null) {
43 |       return null;
44 |     }
45 | 
46 |     if (NodeUtil.isVarDeclaration(n) && n.hasChildren()) {
47 |       return new VarDefinition(n, isExtern);
48 |     } else if (NodeUtil.isFunction(parent) && parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
49 |       if (!NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(parent)) {
50 |         return new NamedFunctionDefinition(parent, isExtern);
51 |       } else if (!n.getString().equals("")) {
52 |         return new FunctionExpressionDefinition(parent, isExtern);
53 |       }
54 |     } else if (NodeUtil.isAssign(parent) && parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
55 |       return new AssignmentDefinition(parent, isExtern);
56 |     } else if (NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, parent)) {
57 |       return new ObjectLiteralPropertyDefinition(parent, n, n.getFirstChild(),
58 |           isExtern);
59 |     } else if (parent.getType() == Token.LP) {
60 |       Node function = parent.getParent();
61 |       return new FunctionArgumentDefinition(function, n, isExtern);
62 |     }
63 |     return null;
64 |   }

===== M0146 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isControlStructure(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1241-1258
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether the given node is a FOR, DO, WHILE, WITH, or IF node.
   */
Method code:
1241 |   static boolean isControlStructure(Node n) {
1242 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1243 |       case Token.FOR:
1244 |       case Token.DO:
1245 |       case Token.WHILE:
1246 |       case Token.WITH:
1247 |       case Token.IF:

775 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {

===== M0168 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InferJSDocInfo.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InferJSDocInfo.java
Original range: 82-178
Documentation:

Method code:
82 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
83 |     JSDocInfo docInfo;
84 | 
85 |     switch (n.getType()) {
86 |       // Infer JSDocInfo on types of all type declarations on variables.
87 |       case Token.NAME:
88 |         if (parent == null) {
89 |           return;
90 |         }
91 | 
92 |         // Only allow JSDoc on VARs, function declarations, and assigns.
93 |         if (parent.getType() != Token.VAR &&
94 |             !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(parent) &&
95 |             !(parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN &&
96 |               n == parent.getFirstChild())) {
97 |           return;
98 |         }
99 | 
100 |         // There are four places the doc info could live.
101 |         // 1) A FUNCTION node.
102 |         // /** ... */ function f() { ... }
103 |         // 2) An ASSIGN parent.
104 |         // /** ... */ x = function () { ... }
105 |         // 3) A NAME parent.
106 |         // var x, /** ... */ y = function() { ... }
107 |         // 4) A VAR gramps.
108 |         // /** ... */ var x = function() { ... }
109 |         docInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
110 |         if (docInfo == null &&
111 |             !(parent.getType() == Token.VAR &&
112 |                 !parent.hasOneChild())) {
113 |           docInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
114 |         }
115 | 
116 |         // Try to find the type of the NAME.
117 |         JSType varType = n.getJSType();
118 |         if (varType == null && parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
119 |           varType = parent.getJSType();
120 |         }
121 | 
122 |         // If we have no type to attach JSDocInfo to, then there's nothing
123 |         // we can do.
124 |         if (varType == null || docInfo == null) {
125 |           return;
126 |         }
127 | 
128 |         // Dereference the type. If the result is not an object, or already
129 |         // has docs attached, then do nothing.
130 |         ObjectType objType = dereferenceToObject(varType);
131 |         if (objType == null || objType.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
132 |           return;
133 |         }
134 | 
135 |         attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(objType, docInfo, n.getString());
136 |         break;
137 | 
138 |       case Token.GETPROP:
139 |         // Infer JSDocInfo on properties.
140 |         // There are two ways to write doc comments on a property.
141 |         //
142 |         // 1)
143 |         // /** @deprecated */
144 |         // obj.prop = ...
145 |         //
146 |         // 2)
147 |         // /** @deprecated */
148 |         // obj.prop;
149 |         if (NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(parent) ||
150 |             (parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN &&
151 |              parent.getFirstChild() == n)) {
152 |           docInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
153 |           if (docInfo == null) {
154 |             docInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
155 |           }
156 |           if (docInfo != null) {
157 |             ObjectType lhsType =
158 |                 dereferenceToObject(n.getFirstChild().getJSType());
159 |             if (lhsType != null) {
160 |               // Put the JSDoc in the property slot, if there is one.
161 |               String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
162 |               if (lhsType.hasOwnProperty(propName)) {
163 |                 lhsType.setPropertyJSDocInfo(propName, docInfo, inExterns);
164 |               }
165 | 
166 |               // Put the JSDoc in any constructors or function shapes as well.
167 |               ObjectType propType =
168 |                   dereferenceToObject(lhsType.getPropertyType(propName));
169 |               if (propType != null) {
170 |                 attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(
171 |                     propType, docInfo, n.getQualifiedName());
172 |               }
173 |             }
174 |           }
175 |         }
176 |         break;
177 |     }
178 |   }

===== M0173 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionNames.getFunctionName(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionNames.java
Original range: 83-109
Documentation:

Method code:
83 |   public String getFunctionName(Node f) {
84 |     FunctionRecord record = functionMap.get(f);
85 |     if (record == null) {
86 |       // Function node was added during compilation and has no name.
87 |       return null;
88 |     }
89 | 
90 |     String str = record.name;
91 |     if (str.isEmpty()) {
92 |       str = "<anonymous>";
93 |     }
94 | 
95 |     Node parent = record.parent;
96 |     if (parent != null) {
97 |       str = getFunctionName(parent) + "::" + str;
98 |     }
99 | 
100 |     // this.foo -> foo
101 |     str = str.replaceAll("::this\\.", ".");
102 |     // foo.prototype.bar -> foo.bar
103 |     // AnonymousFunctionNamingCallback already replaces ".prototype."
104 |     // with "..", just remove the extra dot.
105 |     str = str.replaceAll("\\.\\.", ".");
106 |     // remove toplevel anonymous blocks, if they exists.
107 |     str = str.replaceFirst("^(<anonymous>::)*", "");
108 |     return str;
109 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.getFunctionType(String,Node,JSDocInfo,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 755-833
Documentation:
/**
     * Gets the function type from the function node and its attached
     * {@link JSDocInfo}.
     * @param name the function's name
     * @param rValue the function node. It must be a {@link Token#FUNCTION}.
     * @param info the {@link JSDocInfo} attached to the function definition
     * @param lvalueNode The node where this function is being
     *     assigned. For example, {@code A.prototype.foo = ...} would be used to
     *     determine that this function is a method of A.prototype. May be
     *     null to indicate that this is not being assigned to a qualified name.
     */
Method code:
755 |     private FunctionType getFunctionType(String name,
756 |         Node rValue, JSDocInfo info, @Nullable Node lvalueNode) {
757 |       FunctionType functionType = null;
758 | 
759 |       // Global function aliases should be registered with the type registry.
760 |       if (rValue != null && rValue.isQualifiedName() && scope.isGlobal()) {
761 |         Var var = scope.getVar(rValue.getQualifiedName());
762 |         if (var != null && var.getType() instanceof FunctionType) {
763 |           functionType = (FunctionType) var.getType();
764 |           if (functionType != null &&
765 |               (functionType.isConstructor() || functionType.isInterface())) {
766 |             typeRegistry.declareType(name, functionType.getInstanceType());
767 |           }
768 |         }
769 |         return functionType;
770 |       }
771 | 
772 |       Node owner = null;
773 |       if (lvalueNode != null) {
774 |         owner = getPrototypePropertyOwner(lvalueNode);
775 |       }
776 | 
777 |       Node errorRoot = rValue == null ? lvalueNode : rValue;
778 |       boolean isFnLiteral =
779 |           rValue != null && rValue.getType() == Token.FUNCTION;
780 |       Node fnRoot = isFnLiteral ? rValue : null;
781 |       Node parametersNode = isFnLiteral ?
782 |           rValue.getFirstChild().getNext() : null;
783 |       Node fnBlock = isFnLiteral ? parametersNode.getNext() : null;
784 | 
785 |       if (functionType == null && info != null && info.hasType()) {
786 |         JSType type = info.getType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
787 | 
788 |         // Known to be not null since we have the FUNCTION token there.
789 |         type = type.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
790 |         if (type.isFunctionType()) {
791 |           functionType = (FunctionType) type;
792 |           functionType.setJSDocInfo(info);
793 |         }
794 |       }
795 | 
796 |       if (functionType == null) {
797 |         // Find the type of any overridden function.
798 |         FunctionType overriddenPropType = null;
799 |         if (lvalueNode != null && lvalueNode.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
800 |             lvalueNode.isQualifiedName()) {
801 |           Var var = scope.getVar(
802 |               lvalueNode.getFirstChild().getQualifiedName());
803 |           if (var != null) {
804 |             ObjectType ownerType = ObjectType.cast(var.getType());
805 |             if (ownerType != null) {
806 |               String propName = lvalueNode.getLastChild().getString();
807 |               overriddenPropType = findOverriddenFunction(ownerType, propName);
808 |             }
809 |           }
810 |         }
811 | 
812 |         functionType =
813 |             new FunctionTypeBuilder(name, compiler, errorRoot, sourceName,
814 |                 scope)
815 |             .setSourceNode(fnRoot)
816 |             .inferFromOverriddenFunction(overriddenPropType, parametersNode)
817 |             .inferTemplateTypeName(info)
818 |             .inferReturnType(info)
819 |             .inferInheritance(info)
820 |             .inferThisType(info, owner)
821 |             .inferParameterTypes(parametersNode, info)
822 |             .inferReturnStatementsAsLastResort(fnBlock)
823 |             .buildAndRegister();
824 |       }
825 | 
826 |       // assigning the function type to the function node
827 |       if (rValue != null) {
828 |         setDeferredType(rValue, functionType);
829 |       }
830 | 
831 |       // all done
832 |       return functionType;
833 |     }

===== M0189 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.evaluatesToLocalValue(Node,Predicate<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2424-2489
Documentation:
/**
   * @param locals A predicate to apply to unknown local values.
   * @return Whether the node is known to be a value that is not a reference
   *     outside the expression scope.
   */
Method code:
2424 |   static boolean evaluatesToLocalValue(Node value, Predicate<Node> locals) {
2425 |     switch (value.getType()) {
2426 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
2427 |         // A result that is aliased by a non-local name, is the effectively the
2428 |         // same as returning a non-local name, but this doesn't matter if the
2429 |         // value is immutable.
2430 |         return NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(value.getLastChild())
2431 |             || (locals.apply(value)
2432 |                 && evaluatesToLocalValue(value.getLastChild(), locals));
2433 |       case Token.COMMA:
2434 |         return evaluatesToLocalValue(value.getLastChild(), locals);
2435 |       case Token.AND:
2436 |       case Token.OR:
2437 |         return evaluatesToLocalValue(value.getFirstChild(), locals)
2438 |            && evaluatesToLocalValue(value.getLastChild(), locals);
2439 |       case Token.HOOK:
2440 |         return evaluatesToLocalValue(value.getFirstChild().getNext(), locals)
2441 |            && evaluatesToLocalValue(value.getLastChild(), locals);
2442 |       case Token.INC:
2443 |       case Token.DEC:
2444 |         if (value.getBooleanProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP)) {
2445 |           return evaluatesToLocalValue(value.getFirstChild(), locals);
2446 |         } else {
2447 |           return true;
2448 |         }
2449 |       case Token.THIS:
2450 |         return locals.apply(value);
2451 |       case Token.NAME:
2452 |         return isImmutableValue(value) || locals.apply(value);
2453 |       case Token.GETELEM:
2454 |       case Token.GETPROP:
2455 |         // There is no information about the locality of object properties.
2456 |         return locals.apply(value);
2457 |       case Token.CALL:
2458 |         return callHasLocalResult(value)
2459 |             || isToStringMethodCall(value)
2460 |             || locals.apply(value);
2461 |       case Token.NEW:
2462 |         // TODO(nicksantos): This needs to be changed so that it
2463 |         // returns true iff we're sure the value was never aliased from inside
2464 |         // the constructor (similar to callHasLocalResult)
2465 |         return true;
2466 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
2467 |       case Token.REGEXP:
2468 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
2469 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
2470 |         // Literals objects with non-literal children are allowed.
2471 |         return true;
2472 |       case Token.IN:
2473 |         // TODO(johnlenz): should IN operator be included in #isSimpleOperator?
2474 |         return true;
2475 |       default:
2476 |         // Other op force a local value:
2477 |         //  x = '' + g (x is now an local string)
2478 |         //  x -= g (x is now an local number)
2479 |         if (isAssignmentOp(value)
2480 |             || isSimpleOperator(value)
2481 |             || isImmutableValue(value)) {
2482 |           return true;
2483 |         }
2484 | 
2485 |         throw new IllegalStateException(
2486 |             "Unexpected expression node" + value +
2487 |             "\n parent:" + value.getParent());
2488 |     }
2489 |   }

/**
   * Add a parameter to the param list.
   * @param builder A builder.
   * @param paramType The parameter type.
   * @param warnedAboutArgList Whether we've already warned about arg ordering
   *     issues (like if optional args appeared before required ones).
   * @param isOptional Is this an optional parameter?
   * @param isVarArgs Is this a var args parameter?
   * @return Whether a warning was emitted.
   */
Method code:
578 |   private boolean addParameter(FunctionParamBuilder builder,
579 |       JSType paramType, boolean warnedAboutArgList,
580 |       boolean isOptional, boolean isVarArgs) {
581 |     boolean emittedWarning = false;
582 |     if (isOptional) {
583 |       // Remembering that an optional parameter has been encountered
584 |       // so that if a non optional param is encountered later, an
585 |       // error can be reported.
586 |       if (!builder.addOptionalParams(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
587 |         reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
588 |         emittedWarning = true;
589 |       }
590 |     } else if (isVarArgs) {
591 |       if (!builder.addVarArgs(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
592 |         reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
593 |         emittedWarning = true;
594 |       }
595 |     } else {
596 |       if (!builder.addRequiredParams(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
597 |         // An optional parameter was seen and this argument is not an optional
598 |         // or var arg so it is an error.
599 |         if (builder.hasVarArgs()) {
600 |           reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
601 |         } else {
602 |           reportWarning(OPTIONAL_ARG_AT_END);
603 |         }
604 |         emittedWarning = true;
605 |       }
606 |     }
607 |     return emittedWarning;
608 |   }

337 |   protected int getChar() {
338 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
339 |       cursor++;
340 |       --ungetCursor;
341 |       if (charno == -1) {
342 |         charno = getOffset();
343 |       }
344 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
345 |     }
346 | 
347 |     for(;;) {
348 |       int c;
349 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
350 |         hitEOF = true;
351 |         if (charno == -1) {
352 |           charno = getOffset();
353 |         }
354 |         return EOF_CHAR;
355 |       }
356 |       cursor++;
357 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
358 | 
359 | 
360 |       if (lineEndChar >= 0) {
361 |         if (lineEndChar == '\r' && c == '\n') {
362 |           lineEndChar = '\n';
363 |           continue;
364 |         }
365 |         lineEndChar = -1;
366 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
367 |         lineno++;
368 |       }
369 | 
370 |       if (c <= 127) {
371 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
372 |           lineEndChar = c;
373 |           c = '\n';
374 |         }
375 |       } else {
376 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
377 |           continue;
378 |         }
379 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
380 |           lineEndChar = c;
381 |           c = '\n';
382 |         }
383 |       }
384 | 
385 |       if (charno == -1) {
386 |         charno = getOffset();
387 |       }
388 | 
389 |       return c;
390 |     }
391 |   }

/**
   * Analyze a call site and extract the node that will be act as
   * "this" inside the call, which is either the object part of the
   * qualified function name, the first argument to the call in the
   * case of ".call" and ".apply" or null if object is not specified

/**
   * Given a constructor or an interface type, find out whether the unknown
   * type is a supertype of the current type.
   */
Method code:
609 |   public boolean hasUnknownSupertype() {
610 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(isConstructor() || isInterface());
611 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(!this.isUnknownType());
612 |     // Potential infinite loop if our type system messes up or someone defines
613 |     // a bad type. Otherwise the loop should always end.
614 |     FunctionType ctor = this;
615 |     while (true) {
616 |       ObjectType maybeSuperInstanceType =
617 |           ctor.getPrototype().getImplicitPrototype();
618 |       if (maybeSuperInstanceType == null) {
619 |         return false;
620 |       }
621 |       if (maybeSuperInstanceType.isUnknownType()) {
622 |         return true;
623 |       }
624 |       ctor = maybeSuperInstanceType.getConstructor();
625 |       if (ctor == null) {
626 |         return false;
627 |       }
628 |       Preconditions.checkState(ctor.isConstructor() || ctor.isInterface());
629 |     }

===== M0204 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeNameExtractor.getName(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeNameExtractor.java
Original range: 47-94
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns a qualified name of the specified node. Dots and brackets
   * are changed to the delimiter passed in when constructing the
   * NodeNameExtractor object.  We also replace ".prototype" with the
   * delimiter to keep names short, while still differentiating them
   * from static properties.  (Prototype properties will end up
   * looking like "a$b$$c" if this.delimiter = '$'.)
   */
Method code:
47 |   String getName(Node node) {
48 |     switch (node.getType()) {
49 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
50 |         Node functionNameNode = node.getFirstChild();
51 |         return functionNameNode.getString();
52 |       case Token.GETPROP:
53 |         Node lhsOfDot = node.getFirstChild();
54 |         Node rhsOfDot = lhsOfDot.getNext();
55 |         String lhsOfDotName = getName(lhsOfDot);
56 |         String rhsOfDotName = getName(rhsOfDot);
57 |         if ("prototype".equals(rhsOfDotName)) {
58 |           return lhsOfDotName + delimiter;
59 |         } else {
60 |           return lhsOfDotName + delimiter + rhsOfDotName;
61 |         }
62 |       case Token.GETELEM:
63 |         Node outsideBrackets = node.getFirstChild();
64 |         Node insideBrackets = outsideBrackets.getNext();
65 |         String nameOutsideBrackets = getName(outsideBrackets);
66 |         String nameInsideBrackets = getName(insideBrackets);
67 |         if ("prototype".equals(nameInsideBrackets)) {
68 |           return nameOutsideBrackets + delimiter;
69 |         } else {
70 |           return nameOutsideBrackets + delimiter + nameInsideBrackets;
71 |         }
72 |       case Token.NAME:
73 |         return node.getString();
74 |       case Token.STRING:
75 |         return TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(node.getString()) ?
76 |             node.getString() : ("__" + nextUniqueInt++);
77 |       case Token.NUMBER:
78 |         return NodeUtil.getStringValue(node);
79 |       case Token.THIS:
80 |         return "this";
81 |       case Token.CALL:
82 |         return getName(node.getFirstChild());
83 |       default:
84 |         StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
85 |         for (Node child = node.getFirstChild(); child != null;
86 |              child = child.getNext()) {
87 |           if (sb.length() > 0) {

888 |     //
889 |     // TODO(nicksantos): In ES3, the runtime coerces "null" to the global
890 |     // activation object. In ES5, it leaves it as null. Just punt on this
891 |     // issue for now by coercing out null. This is complicated by the
892 |     // fact that when most people write @this {Foo}, they really don't
893 |     // mean "nullable Foo". For certain tags (like @extends) we de-nullify
894 |     // the name for them.

906 |     for (ObjectType iface : implementedInterfaces) {
907 |       ObjectType resolvedIface = (ObjectType) iface.resolve(t, scope);
908 |       resolvedInterfaces.add(resolvedIface);
909 |       changed |= (resolvedIface != iface);
910 |     }
911 |     if (changed) {
912 |       implementedInterfaces = resolvedInterfaces.build();
913 |     }
914 | 
915 |     if (subTypes != null) {
916 |       for (int i = 0; i < subTypes.size(); i++) {
917 |         subTypes.set(i, (FunctionType) subTypes.get(i).resolve(t, scope));

113 |     if (functionScope.isLocal()) {

===== M0216 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.TypeInference(AbstractCompiler,ControlFlowGraph<Node>,ReverseAbstractInterpreter,Scope,Map<String, AssertionFunctionSpec>,Collection<Var>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 119-159
Documentation:
// TODO(nicksantos): Create a builder for this class.
Method code:
119 |   TypeInference(AbstractCompiler compiler, ControlFlowGraph<Node> cfg,
120 |                 ReverseAbstractInterpreter reverseInterpreter,
121 |                 Scope functionScope,
122 |                 Map<String, AssertionFunctionSpec> assertionFunctionsMap,
123 |                 Collection<Var> unflowableVars) {
124 |     super(cfg, new LinkedFlowScope.FlowScopeJoinOp());
125 |     this.compiler = compiler;
126 |     this.registry = compiler.getTypeRegistry();
127 |     this.reverseInterpreter = reverseInterpreter;
128 |     this.syntacticScope = functionScope;
129 |     this.functionScope = LinkedFlowScope.createEntryLattice(functionScope);
130 |     this.assertionFunctionsMap = assertionFunctionsMap;
131 | 
132 |     for (Var unflowableVar : unflowableVars) {
133 |       String name = unflowableVar.getName();
134 |       if (functionScope.getVar(name) == unflowableVar) {
135 |         this.unflowableVarNames.add(name);
136 |       }
137 |     }
138 | 
139 |     Iterator<Var> varIt = functionScope.getVars();
140 |     while (varIt.hasNext()) {
141 |       Var var = varIt.next();
142 |       if (this.unflowableVarNames.contains(var.getName())) {
143 |         continue;
144 |       }
145 | 
146 |       // For each local variable declared with the VAR keyword, the entry
147 |       // type is VOID.
148 |       if (var.getParentNode() != null &&
149 |           var.getType() == null && // no declared type
150 |           var.getParentNode().getType() == Token.VAR &&
151 |           !var.isExtern()) {
152 |         this.functionScope.inferSlotType(
153 |             var.getName(), getNativeType(VOID_TYPE));
154 |       }
155 |     }
156 | 
157 |     this.bottomScope = LinkedFlowScope.createEntryLattice(
158 |         new Scope(functionScope.getRootNode(), functionScope.getTypeOfThis()));
159 |   }

===== M0219 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.transform(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 204-228
Documentation:

Method code:
204 |   private Node transform(AstNode node) {
205 |     JSDocInfo jsDocInfo = handleJsDoc(node);
206 |     Node irNode = justTransform(node);
207 |     if (jsDocInfo != null) {
208 |       irNode.setJSDocInfo(jsDocInfo);
209 |     }
210 | 
211 |     // If we have a named function, set the position to that of the name.
212 |     if (irNode.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
213 |         irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno() != -1) {
214 |       irNode.setLineno(irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno());
215 |       irNode.setCharno(irNode.getFirstChild().getCharno());
216 |     } else {
217 |       if (irNode.getLineno() == -1) {
218 |         // If we didn't already set the line, then set it now.  This avoids
219 |         // cases like ParenthesizedExpression where we just return a previous
220 |         // node, but don't want the new node to get its parent's line number.
221 |         int lineno = node.getLineno();
222 |         irNode.setLineno(lineno);
223 |         int charno = position2charno(node.getAbsolutePosition());
224 |         irNode.setCharno(charno);
225 |       }
226 |     }
227 |     return irNode;
228 |   }

295 |         int length = s.length();

297 |         if (length == 0 || !Character.isJavaIdentifierStart(s.charAt(0)))
298 |             return false;
299 | 
300 |         for (int i=1; i<length; i++) {
301 |             char c = s.charAt(i);
302 |             if (!Character.isJavaIdentifierPart(c)) {
303 |                 if (c == '\\') {
304 |                     if (! ((i + 5) < length)
305 |                         && (s.charAt(i + 1) == 'u')
306 |                         && 0 <= Kit.xDigitToInt(s.charAt(i + 2), 0)
307 |                         && 0 <= Kit.xDigitToInt(s.charAt(i + 3), 0)
308 |                         && 0 <= Kit.xDigitToInt(s.charAt(i + 4), 0)
309 |                         && 0 <= Kit.xDigitToInt(s.charAt(i + 5), 0)) {
310 |                         return true;
311 |                      }
312 |                 }
313 | 
314 |                 return false;
315 |             }
316 |         }
317 | 
318 |         return true;
319 |     }

===== M0243 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.AnonymousFunctionNamingCallback.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/AnonymousFunctionNamingCallback.java
Original range: 67-107
Documentation:

Method code:
67 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
68 |     switch (n.getType()) {
69 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
70 |         // this handles functions that are assigned to variables or
71 |         // properties
72 |         // e.g. goog.string.htmlEscape = function(str) {
73 |         // }
74 | 
75 |         // get the function name and see if it's empty
76 |         Node functionNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
77 |         String functionName = functionNameNode.getString();
78 |         if (functionName.length() == 0) {
79 |           if (parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN) {
80 |             // this is an assignment to a property, generally either a
81 |             // static function or a prototype function
82 |             // e.g. goog.string.htmlEscape = function() { } or
83 |             //      goog.structs.Map.prototype.getCount = function() { }
84 |             Node lhs = parent.getFirstChild();
85 |             String name = namer.getName(lhs);
86 |             namer.setFunctionName(name, n);
87 |           } else if (parent.getType() == Token.NAME) {
88 |             // this is an assignment to a variable
89 |             // e.g. var handler = function() {}
90 |             String name = namer.getName(parent);
91 |             namer.setFunctionName(name, n);
92 |           }
93 |         }
94 |         break;
95 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
96 |         // this handles functions that are assigned to a prototype through
97 |         // an object literal
98 |         // e.g. BuzzApp.prototype = {
99 |         //        Start : function() { }
100 |         //      }
101 |         Node lhs = n.getFirstChild();
102 |         Node rhs = lhs.getNext();
103 |         if (rhs.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) {
104 |           nameObjectLiteralMethods(rhs, namer.getName(lhs));
105 |         }
106 |     }
107 |   }

===== M0246 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PureFunctionIdentifier.FunctionAnalyzer.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PureFunctionIdentifier.java
Original range: 396-467
Documentation:

Method code:
396 |     @Override
397 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal traversal, Node node, Node parent) {
398 | 
399 |       if (inExterns) {
400 |         return;
401 |       }
402 | 
403 |       if (!NodeUtil.nodeTypeMayHaveSideEffects(node)
404 |           && node.getType() != Token.RETURN) {
405 |         return;
406 |       }
407 | 
408 |       if (NodeUtil.isCall(node) || NodeUtil.isNew(node)) {
409 |         allFunctionCalls.add(node);
410 |       }
411 | 
412 |       Node enclosingFunction = traversal.getEnclosingFunction();
413 |       if (enclosingFunction != null) {
414 |         FunctionInformation sideEffectInfo =
415 |             functionSideEffectMap.get(enclosingFunction);
416 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(sideEffectInfo);
417 | 
418 |         if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(node)) {
419 |           visitAssignmentOrUnaryOperator(
420 |               sideEffectInfo, traversal.getScope(),
421 |               node, node.getFirstChild(), node.getLastChild());
422 |         } else {
423 |           switch(node.getType()) {
424 |             case Token.CALL:
425 |             case Token.NEW:
426 |               visitCall(sideEffectInfo, node);
427 |               break;
428 |             case Token.DELPROP:
429 |             case Token.DEC:
430 |             case Token.INC:
431 |               visitAssignmentOrUnaryOperator(
432 |                   sideEffectInfo, traversal.getScope(),
433 |                   node, node.getFirstChild(), null);
434 |               break;
435 |             case Token.NAME:
436 |               // Variable definition are not side effects.
437 |               // Just check that the name appears in the context of a
438 |               // variable declaration.
439 |               Preconditions.checkArgument(
440 |                   NodeUtil.isVarDeclaration(node));
441 |               Node value = node.getFirstChild();
442 |               // Assignment to local, if the value isn't a safe local value,
443 |               // new object creation or literal or known primitive result
444 |               // value, add it to the local blacklist.
445 |               if (value != null && !NodeUtil.evaluatesToLocalValue(value)) {
446 |                 Scope scope = traversal.getScope();
447 |                 Var var = scope.getVar(node.getString());
448 |                 sideEffectInfo.blacklistLocal(var);
449 |               }
450 |               break;
451 |             case Token.THROW:
452 |               visitThrow(sideEffectInfo);
453 |               break;
454 |             case Token.RETURN:
455 |               if (node.hasChildren()
456 |                   && !NodeUtil.evaluatesToLocalValue(node.getFirstChild())) {
457 |                 sideEffectInfo.setTaintsReturn();
458 |               }
459 |               break;
460 |             default:
461 |               throw new IllegalArgumentException(
462 |                   "Unhandled side effect node type " +
463 |                   Token.name(node.getType()));
464 |           }
465 |         }
466 |       }
467 |     }

===== M0257 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 408-452
Documentation:

Method code:
408 |   @Override
409 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
410 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
411 |       return true;
412 |     }
413 | 
414 |     // Union types
415 |     if (that instanceof UnionType) {
416 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
417 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
418 |       return false;
419 |     }
420 | 
421 |     // record types
422 |     if (that instanceof RecordType) {
423 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, (RecordType) that);
424 |     }
425 | 
426 |     // Interfaces
427 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
428 |     // to the interface instance.
429 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
430 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
431 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
432 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
433 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
434 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
435 |           return true;
436 |         }
437 |       }
438 |     }
439 | 
440 |     // other prototype based objects
441 |     if (that != null) {
442 |       if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
443 |         // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
444 |         // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
445 |         // to avoid guessing.
446 |         return true;
447 |       }
448 |       return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
449 |     }
450 | 
451 |     return false;
452 |   }

560 |     JSType getDeclaredTypeInAnnotation(String sourceName,
561 |         Node node, JSDocInfo info) {
562 |       JSType jsType = null;
563 |       Node objNode = node.getType() == Token.GETPROP ?
564 |           node.getFirstChild() : null;
565 |       if (info != null) {
566 |         if (info.hasType()) {
567 |           jsType = info.getType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
568 |         } else if (FunctionTypeBuilder.isFunctionTypeDeclaration(info)) {
569 |           String fnName = node.getQualifiedName();
570 | 
571 |           // constructors are often handled separately.
572 |           if (info.isConstructor() && typeRegistry.getType(fnName) != null) {
573 |             return null;
574 |           }
575 | 
576 |           FunctionTypeBuilder builder =
577 |               new FunctionTypeBuilder(
578 |                   fnName, compiler, node, sourceName, scope)
579 |               .inferTemplateTypeName(info)
580 |               .inferReturnType(info)
581 |               .inferParameterTypes(info)
582 |               .inferInheritance(info);
583 | 
584 |           // Infer the context type.
585 |           boolean searchedForThisType = false;
586 |           if (objNode != null) {
587 |             if (objNode.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
588 |                 objNode.getLastChild().getString().equals("prototype")) {
589 |               builder.inferThisType(info, objNode.getFirstChild());
590 |               searchedForThisType = true;
591 |             } else if (objNode.getType() == Token.THIS) {
592 |               builder.inferThisType(info, objNode.getJSType());
593 |               searchedForThisType = true;
594 |             }
595 |           }
596 | 
597 |           if (!searchedForThisType) {
598 |             builder.inferThisType(info, (Node) null);
599 |           }
600 | 
601 |           jsType = builder.buildAndRegister();
602 |         }
603 |       }
604 |       return jsType;
605 |     }

/**
   * Visits a CALL node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1363 |   private void visitCall(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1364 |     Node child = n.getFirstChild();
1365 |     JSType childType = getJSType(child).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1366 | 
1367 |     if (!childType.canBeCalled()) {
1368 |       report(t, n, NOT_CALLABLE, childType.toString());
1369 |       ensureTyped(t, n);
1370 |       return;
1371 |     }
1372 | 
1373 |     // A couple of types can be called as if they were functions.
1374 |     // If it is a function type, then validate parameters.
1375 |     if (childType instanceof FunctionType) {
1376 |       FunctionType functionType = (FunctionType) childType;
1377 | 
1378 |       // Non-native constructors should never be called directly.
1379 |       if (functionType.isConstructor() &&
1380 |           !functionType.isNativeObjectType()) {
1381 |         report(t, n, CONSTRUCTOR_NOT_CALLABLE, childType.toString());
1382 |       }
1383 | 
1384 |       visitParameterList(t, n, functionType);
1385 |       ensureTyped(t, n, functionType.getReturnType());
1386 |     } else {
1387 |       ensureTyped(t, n);
1388 |     }
1389 | 
1390 |     // TODO: Add something to check for calls of RegExp objects, which is not
1391 |     // supported by IE.  Either say something about the return type or warn
1392 |     // about the non-portability of the call or both.
1393 |   }

===== M0274 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 148-190
Documentation:

Method code:
148 |   @Override
149 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
150 |     this.root = root;
151 |     astPositionCounter = 0;
152 |     astPosition = Maps.newHashMap();
153 |     nodePriorities = Maps.newHashMap();
154 |     cfg = new AstControlFlowGraph(computeFallThrough(root), nodePriorities,
155 |                                   edgeAnnotations);
156 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, this);
157 |     astPosition.put(null, ++astPositionCounter); // the implicit return is last.
158 | 
159 |     // Now, generate the priority of nodes by doing a depth-first
160 |     // search on the CFG.
161 |     priorityCounter = 0;
162 |     DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry = cfg.getEntry();
163 |     prioritizeFromEntryNode(entry);
164 | 
165 |     if (shouldTraverseFunctions) {
166 |       // If we're traversing inner functions, we need to rank the
167 |       // priority of them too.
168 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
169 |         Node value = candidate.getValue();
170 |         if (value != null && value.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
171 |           Preconditions.checkState(
172 |               !nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate) || candidate == entry);
173 |           prioritizeFromEntryNode(candidate);
174 |         }
175 |       }
176 |     }
177 | 
178 |     // At this point, all reachable nodes have been given a priority, but
179 |     // unreachable nodes have not been given a priority. Put them last.
180 |     // Presumably, it doesn't really matter what priority they get, since
181 |     // this shouldn't happen in real code.
182 |     for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
183 |       if (!nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate)) {
184 |         nodePriorities.put(candidate, ++priorityCounter);
185 |       }
186 |     }
187 | 
188 |     // Again, the implicit return node is always last.
189 |     nodePriorities.put(cfg.getImplicitReturn(), ++priorityCounter);
190 |   }

===== M0286 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1515-1517
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1515 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1516 |     return n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION && !isStatement(n);
1517 |   }

===== M0289 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PureFunctionIdentifier.getDebugReport()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PureFunctionIdentifier.java
Original range: 124-176
Documentation:
/**
   * Compute debug report that includes:
   *  - List of all pure functions.
   *  - Reasons we think the remaining functions have side effects.
   */
Method code:
124 |   String getDebugReport() {
125 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(externs);
126 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(root);
127 | 
128 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
129 | 
130 |     FunctionNames functionNames = new FunctionNames(compiler);
131 |     functionNames.process(null, externs);
132 |     functionNames.process(null, root);
133 | 
134 |     sb.append("Pure functions:\n");
135 |     for (Map.Entry<Node, FunctionInformation> entry :
136 |              functionSideEffectMap.entrySet()) {
137 |       Node function = entry.getKey();
138 |       FunctionInformation functionInfo = entry.getValue();
139 | 
140 |       boolean isPure =
141 |           functionInfo.mayBePure() && !functionInfo.mayHaveSideEffects();
142 |       if (isPure) {
143 |         sb.append("  " + functionNames.getFunctionName(function) + "\n");
144 |       }
145 |     }
146 |     sb.append("\n");
147 | 
148 |     for (Map.Entry<Node, FunctionInformation> entry :
149 |              functionSideEffectMap.entrySet()) {
150 |       Node function = entry.getKey();
151 |       FunctionInformation functionInfo = entry.getValue();
152 | 
153 |       Set<String> depFunctionNames = Sets.newHashSet();
154 |       for (Node callSite : functionInfo.getCallsInFunctionBody()) {
155 |         Collection<Definition> defs =
156 |             getCallableDefinitions(definitionProvider,
157 |                                    callSite.getFirstChild());
158 | 
159 |         if (defs == null) {
160 |           depFunctionNames.add("<null def list>");
161 |           continue;
162 |         }
163 | 
164 |         for (Definition def : defs) {
165 |           depFunctionNames.add(
166 |               functionNames.getFunctionName(def.getRValue()));
167 |         }
168 |       }
169 | 
170 |       sb.append(functionNames.getFunctionName(function) + " " +
171 |                 functionInfo.toString() +
172 |                 " Calls: " + depFunctionNames + "\n");
173 |     }
174 | 
175 |     return sb.toString();
176 |   }

===== M0291 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitReturn(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1441-1476
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a RETURN node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1441 |   private void visitReturn(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1442 |     Node function = t.getEnclosingFunction();
1443 | 
1444 |     // This is a misplaced return, but the real JS will fail to compile,
1445 |     // so let it go.
1446 |     if (function == null) {
1447 |       return;
1448 |     }
1449 |     JSType jsType = getJSType(function);
1450 | 
1451 |     if (jsType instanceof FunctionType) {
1452 |       FunctionType functionType = (FunctionType) jsType;
1453 | 
1454 |       JSType returnType = functionType.getReturnType();
1455 | 
1456 |       // if no return type is specified, undefined must be returned
1457 |       // (it's a void function)
1458 |       if (returnType == null) {
1459 |         returnType = getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
1460 |       }
1461 | 
1462 |       // fetching the returned value's type
1463 |       Node valueNode = n.getFirstChild();
1464 |       JSType actualReturnType;
1465 |       if (valueNode == null) {
1466 |         actualReturnType = getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
1467 |         valueNode = n;
1468 |       } else {
1469 |         actualReturnType = getJSType(valueNode);
1470 |       }
1471 | 
1472 |       // verifying
1473 |       validator.expectCanAssignTo(t, valueNode, actualReturnType, returnType,
1474 |           "inconsistent return type");
1475 |     }
1476 |   }

===== M0306 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getCharIgnoreLineEnd()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 393-437
Documentation:

Method code:
393 |   private int getCharIgnoreLineEnd() {
394 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
395 |       cursor++;
396 |       --ungetCursor;
397 |       if (charno == -1) {
398 |         charno = getOffset();
399 |       }
400 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
401 |     }
402 | 
403 |     for(;;) {
404 |       int c;
405 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
406 |         hitEOF = true;
407 |         if (charno == -1) {
408 |           charno = getOffset();
409 |         }
410 |         return EOF_CHAR;
411 |       }
412 |       cursor++;
413 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
414 | 
415 | 
416 |       if (c <= 127) {
417 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
418 |           lineEndChar = c;
419 |           c = '\n';
420 |         }
421 |       } else {
422 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
423 |           continue;
424 |         }
425 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
426 |           lineEndChar = c;
427 |           c = '\n';
428 |         }
429 |       }
430 | 
431 |       if (charno == -1) {
432 |         charno = getOffset();
433 |       }
434 | 
435 |       return c;
436 |     }
437 |   }

684 |   static boolean constructorCallHasSideEffects(
685 |       Node callNode, AbstractCompiler compiler) {
686 |     if (callNode.getType() != Token.NEW) {
687 |       throw new IllegalStateException(
688 |           "Expected NEW node, got " + Token.name(callNode.getType()));

690 |

692 |       return false;
693 |     }

696 |     if (nameNode.getType() == Token.NAME &&
697 |         CONSTRUCTORS_WITHOUT_SIDE_EFFECTS.contains(nameNode.getString())) {
698 |       return false;
699 |     }
700 | 
701 |     return true;
702 |   }
