===== M0319 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getFunctionInfo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2368-2383
Documentation:
/**
   * Get the JSDocInfo for a function.
   */
Method code:
2368 |   static JSDocInfo getFunctionInfo(Node n) {
2369 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
2370 |     JSDocInfo fnInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
2371 |     if (fnInfo == null && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
2372 |       // Look for the info on other nodes.
2373 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
2374 |       if (parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN) {
2375 |         // on ASSIGNs
2376 |         fnInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
2377 |       } else if (parent.getType() == Token.NAME) {
2378 |         // on var NAME = function() { ... };
2379 |         fnInfo = parent.getParent().getJSDocInfo();
2380 |       }
2381 |     }
2382 |     return fnInfo;
2383 |   }

/**
   * Counts the given node in the typed statistics.
   * @param n a node that should be typed
   */
Method code:
799 |   private void doPercentTypedAccounting(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
800 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
801 |     if (type == null) {
802 |       nullCount++;
803 |     } else if (type.isUnknownType()) {

807 |       }
808 |       unknownCount++;
809 |     } else {
810 |       typedCount++;
811 |     }
812 |   }

===== M0339 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.annotateFunctions(Node,Node)

Original range: 234-256

/**
     * Annotate optional and var_arg function parameters.
     */
Method code:
234 |     private void annotateFunctions(Node n, Node parent) {
235 |       JSDocInfo fnInfo = NodeUtil.getFunctionInfo(n);
236 | 
237 |       // Compute which function parameters are optional and
238 |       // which are var_args.
239 |       Node args = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
240 |       for (Node arg = args.getFirstChild();
241 |            arg != null;
242 |            arg = arg.getNext()) {
243 |         String argName = arg.getString();
244 |         JSTypeExpression typeExpr = fnInfo == null ?
245 |             null : fnInfo.getParameterType(argName);
246 | 
247 |         if (convention.isOptionalParameter(arg) ||
248 |             typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isOptionalArg()) {
249 |           arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_OPTIONAL_PARAM, true);
250 |         }
251 |         if (convention.isVarArgsParameter(arg) ||
252 |             typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isVarArgs()) {
253 |           arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_VAR_ARGS_PARAM, true);
254 |         }
255 |       }
256 |     }

===== M0357 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 294-302
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
294 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
295 |       Node ret = transform(n);
296 |       if (ret.getType() == Token.STRING) {
297 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
298 |       } else if (ret.getType() == Token.NAME) {
299 |         ret.setType(Token.STRING);
300 |       }
301 |       return ret;
302 |     }

===== M0361 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1595-1632
Documentation:
/** Returns true if this node is equivalent semantically to another */
Method code:
1595 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(Node node) {
1596 |     if (type == Token.ARRAYLIT) {
1597 |       try {
1598 |         int[] indices1 = (int[]) getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1599 |         int[] indices2 = (int[]) node.getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1600 |         if (indices1 == null) {
1601 |           if (indices2 != null) {
1602 |             return false;
1603 |           }
1604 |         } else if (indices2 == null) {
1605 |           return false;
1606 |         } else if (indices1.length != indices2.length) {
1607 |           return false;
1608 |         } else {
1609 |           for (int i = 0; i < indices1.length; i++) {
1610 |             if (indices1[i] != indices2[i]) {
1611 |               return false;
1612 |             }
1613 |           }
1614 |         }
1615 |       } catch (Exception e) {
1616 |         return false;
1617 |       }
1618 |     } else if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1619 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1620 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1621 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1622 |         return false;
1623 |       }
1624 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING) {
1625 |       int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1626 |       int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1627 |       if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1628 |         return false;
1629 |       }
1630 |     }
1631 |     return true;
1632 |   }

/**
   * Converts an operator's token value (see {@link Token}) to a string
   * representation.
   *
   * @param operator the operator's token value to convert
   * @return the string representation or {@code null} if the token value is
   * not an operator
   */
Method code:
1663 |   static String opToStr(int operator) {
1664 |     switch (operator) {
1665 |       case Token.BITOR: return "|";
1666 |       case Token.OR: return "||";
1667 |       case Token.BITXOR: return "^";
1668 |       case Token.AND: return "&&";
1669 |       case Token.BITAND: return "&";
1670 |       case Token.SHEQ: return "===";
1671 |       case Token.EQ: return "==";
1672 |       case Token.NOT: return "!";
1673 |       case Token.NE: return "!=";
1674 |       case Token.SHNE: return "!==";
1675 |       case Token.LSH: return "<<";
1676 |       case Token.IN: return "in";
1677 |       case Token.LE: return "<=";
1678 |       case Token.LT: return "<";
1679 |       case Token.URSH: return ">>>";
1680 |       case Token.RSH: return ">>";
1681 |       case Token.GE: return ">=";
1682 |       case Token.GT: return ">";
1683 |       case Token.MUL: return "*";
1684 |       case Token.DIV: return "/";
1685 |       case Token.MOD: return "%";
1686 |       case Token.BITNOT: return "~";
1687 |       case Token.ADD: return "+";
1688 |       case Token.SUB: return "-";
1689 |       case Token.POS: return "+";
1690 |       case Token.NEG: return "-";
1691 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return "=";
1692 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR: return "|=";
1693 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR: return "^=";
1694 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND: return "&=";
1695 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH: return "<<=";
1696 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH: return ">>=";
1697 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH: return ">>>=";
1698 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD: return "+=";
1699 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB: return "-=";
1700 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL: return "*=";
1701 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV: return "/=";
1702 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD: return "%=";
1703 |       case Token.VOID: return "void";
1704 |       case Token.TYPEOF: return "typeof";
1705 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF: return "instanceof";
1706 |       default: return null;
1707 |     }
1708 |   }

===== M0372 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1019-1039
Documentation:

Method code:
1019 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
1020 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
1021 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
1022 |       return s;
1023 |     }
1024 | 
1025 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-latin characters if needed.
1026 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
1027 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1028 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1029 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
1030 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
1031 |       // crazy.
1032 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
1033 |         sb.append(c);
1034 |       } else {
1035 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1036 |       }
1037 |     }
1038 |     return sb.toString();
1039 |   }

===== M0380 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanRoot(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 82-112
Documentation:

Method code:
82 |   private void scanRoot(Node n, Scope parent) {
83 |     if (n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
84 |       sourceName = (String) n.getProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP);
85 | 
86 |       final Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
87 |       final Node args = fnNameNode.getNext();
88 |       final Node body = args.getNext();
89 | 
90 |       // Bleed the function name into the scope, if it hasn't
91 |       // been declared in the outer scope.
92 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
93 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty() && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
94 |         declareVar(fnNameNode);
95 |       }
96 | 
97 |       // Args: Declare function variables
98 |       Preconditions.checkState(args.getType() == Token.LP);
99 |       for (Node a = args.getFirstChild(); a != null;
100 |            a = a.getNext()) {
101 |         Preconditions.checkState(a.getType() == Token.NAME);
102 |         declareVar(a);
103 |       }
104 | 
105 |       // Body
106 |       scanVars(body, n);
107 |     } else {
108 |       // It's the global block
109 |       Preconditions.checkState(scope.getParent() == null);
110 |       scanVars(n, null);
111 |     }
112 |   }

===== M0381 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.strEscape(String,char,String,String,String,CharsetEncoder)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 956-1017
Documentation:
/** Helper to escape javascript string as well as regular expression */
Method code:
956 |   static String strEscape(String s, char quote,
957 |                           String doublequoteEscape,
958 |                           String singlequoteEscape,
959 |                           String backslashEscape,
960 |                           CharsetEncoder outputCharsetEncoder) {
961 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder(s.length() + 2);
962 |     sb.append(quote);
963 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
964 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
965 |       switch (c) {
966 |         case '\n': sb.append("\\n"); break;
967 |         case '\r': sb.append("\\r"); break;
968 |         case '\t': sb.append("\\t"); break;
969 |         case '\\': sb.append(backslashEscape); break;
970 |         case '\"': sb.append(doublequoteEscape); break;
971 |         case '\'': sb.append(singlequoteEscape); break;
972 |         case '>':                       // Break --> into --\> or ]]> into ]]\>
973 |           if (i >= 2 &&
974 |               ((s.charAt(i - 1) == '-' && s.charAt(i - 2) == '-') ||
975 |                (s.charAt(i - 1) == ']' && s.charAt(i - 2) == ']'))) {
976 |             sb.append("\\>");
977 |           } else {
978 |             sb.append(c);
979 |           }
980 |           break;
981 |         case '<':                       // Break </script into <\/script
982 |           final String END_SCRIPT = "/script";
983 |           if (s.regionMatches(true, i + 1, END_SCRIPT, 0,
984 |               END_SCRIPT.length())) {
985 |             sb.append("<\\");
986 |           } else {
987 |             sb.append(c);
988 |           }
989 |           break;
990 |         default:
991 |           // If we're given an outputCharsetEncoder, then check if the
992 |           //  character can be represented in this character set.
993 |           if (outputCharsetEncoder != null) {
994 |             if (outputCharsetEncoder.canEncode(c)) {
995 |               sb.append(c);
996 |             } else {
997 |               // Unicode-escape the character.
998 |               appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
999 |             }
1000 |           } else {
1001 |             // No charsetEncoder provided - pass straight latin characters
1002 |             // through, and escape the rest.  Doing the explicit character
1003 |             // check is measurably faster than using the CharsetEncoder.
1004 |             if (c > 0x1f && c <= 0x7f) {
1005 |               sb.append(c);
1006 |             } else {
1007 |               // Other characters can be misinterpreted by some js parsers,
1008 |               // or perhaps mangled by proxies along the way,
1009 |               // so we play it safe and unicode escape them.
1010 |               appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1011 |             }
1012 |           }
1013 |       }
1014 |     }
1015 |     sb.append(quote);
1016 |     return sb.toString();
1017 |   }

===== M0384 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ArrowType.hasEqualParameters(ArrowType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ArrowType.java
Original range: 146-169
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if our parameter spec is equal to {@code that}'s parameter
   *     spec.
   */
Method code:
146 |   boolean hasEqualParameters(ArrowType that) {
147 |     Node thisParam = parameters.getFirstChild();
148 |     Node otherParam = that.parameters.getFirstChild();
149 |     while (thisParam != null && otherParam != null) {
150 |       JSType thisParamType = thisParam.getJSType();
151 |       JSType otherParamType = otherParam.getJSType();
152 |       if (thisParamType != null) {
153 |         // Both parameter lists give a type for this param, it should be equal
154 |         if (otherParamType != null &&
155 |             !thisParamType.isEquivalentTo(otherParamType)) {
156 |           return false;
157 |         }
158 |       } else {
159 |         if (otherParamType != null) {
160 |           return false;
161 |         }
162 |       }
163 |       thisParam = thisParam.getNext();
164 |       otherParam = otherParam.getNext();
165 |     }
166 |     // One of the parameters is null, so the types are only equal if both
167 |     // parameter lists are null (they are equal).
168 |     return thisParam == otherParam;
169 |   }

===== M0394 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 502-552
Documentation:

Method code:
502 |    @Override
503 |   Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
504 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
505 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
506 |       if (name == null) {
507 |         name = new Name();
508 |         name.setIdentifier("");
509 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
510 |       }
511 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
512 |       Node newName = transform(name);
513 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
514 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
515 |         // declaration.
516 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
517 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
518 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
519 |         // same line as the function keyword.
520 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
521 |             functionNode.getLp();
522 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
523 |       }
524 | 
525 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
526 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.LP);
527 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
528 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
529 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
530 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
531 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
532 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
533 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
534 |       if (fnName != null) {
535 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
536 |       } else {
537 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
538 |       }
539 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
540 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
541 | 
542 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
543 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
544 |         lp.addChildToBack(transform(param));
545 |       }
546 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
547 | 
548 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
549 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
550 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
551 |      return node;
552 |     }

/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array

1151 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1152 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);

===== M0409 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PureFunctionIdentifier.FunctionAnalyzer.exitScope(NodeTraversal)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PureFunctionIdentifier.java
Original range: 474-513
Documentation:

Method code:
474 |     @Override
475 |     public void exitScope(NodeTraversal t) {
476 |       if (t.inGlobalScope()) {
477 |         return;
478 |       }
479 | 
480 |       // Handle deferred local variable modifications:
481 |       //
482 |       FunctionInformation sideEffectInfo =
483 |         functionSideEffectMap.get(t.getScopeRoot());
484 |       if (sideEffectInfo.mutatesGlobalState()){
485 |         sideEffectInfo.resetLocalVars();
486 |         return;
487 |       }
488 | 
489 |       for (Iterator<Var> i = t.getScope().getVars(); i.hasNext();) {
490 |         Var v = i.next();
491 |         boolean localVar = false;
492 |         // Parameters and catch values come can from other scopes.
493 |         if (v.getParentNode().getType() == Token.VAR) {
494 |           // TODO(johnlenz): create a useful parameter list
495 |           sideEffectInfo.knownLocals.add(v.getName());
496 |           localVar = true;
497 |         }
498 | 
499 |         // Take care of locals that might have been tainted.
500 |         if (!localVar || sideEffectInfo.blacklisted.contains(v)) {
501 |           if (sideEffectInfo.taintedLocals.contains(v)) {
502 |             // If the function has global side-effects
503 |             // don't bother with the local side-effects.
504 |             sideEffectInfo.setTaintsUnknown();
505 |             sideEffectInfo.resetLocalVars();
506 |             break;
507 |           }
508 |         }
509 |       }
510 | 
511 |       sideEffectInfo.taintedLocals = null;
512 |       sideEffectInfo.blacklisted = null;
513 |     }

242 |       LinkedFlowScope linkedB = (LinkedFlowScope) b;

244 |       linkedB.frozen = true;
245 |       if (linkedA.optimize() == linkedB.optimize()) {
246 |         return linkedA.createChildFlowScope();
247 |       }
248 |       return new LinkedFlowScope(new FlatFlowScopeCache(linkedA, linkedB));
249 |     }

===== M0425 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 217-292
Documentation:

Method code:
217 |   @Override
218 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
219 |       NodeTraversal nodeTraversal, Node n, Node parent) {
220 |     astPosition.put(n, astPositionCounter++);
221 | 
222 |     switch (n.getType()) {
223 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
224 |         if (shouldTraverseFunctions || n == cfg.getEntry().getValue()) {
225 |           exceptionHandler.push(n);
226 |           return true;
227 |         }
228 |         return false;
229 |       case Token.TRY:
230 |         exceptionHandler.push(n);
231 |         return true;
232 |     }
233 | 
234 |     /*
235 |      * We are going to stop the traversal depending on what the node's parent
236 |      * is.
237 |      *
238 |      * We are only interested in adding edges between nodes that change control
239 |      * flow. The most obvious ones are loops and IF-ELSE's. A statement
240 |      * transfers control to its next sibling.
241 |      *
242 |      * In case of an expression tree, there is no control flow within the tree
243 |      * even when there are short circuited operators and conditionals. When we
244 |      * are doing data flow analysis, we will simply synthesize lattices up the
245 |      * expression tree by finding the meet at each expression node.
246 |      *
247 |      * For example: within a Token.SWITCH, the expression in question does not
248 |      * change the control flow and need not to be considered.
249 |      */
250 |     if (parent != null) {
251 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
252 |         case Token.FOR:
253 |           // Only traverse the body of the for loop.
254 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
255 | 
256 |         // Skip the conditions.
257 |         case Token.IF:
258 |         case Token.WHILE:
259 |         case Token.WITH:
260 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
261 |         case Token.DO:
262 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
263 |         // Only traverse the body of the cases
264 |         case Token.SWITCH:
265 |         case Token.CASE:
266 |         case Token.CATCH:
267 |         case Token.LABEL:
268 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
269 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
270 |           return n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
271 |         case Token.CONTINUE:
272 |         case Token.BREAK:
273 |         case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
274 |         case Token.VAR:
275 |         case Token.RETURN:
276 |         case Token.THROW:
277 |           return false;
278 |         case Token.TRY:
279 |           /* Just before we are about to visit the second child of the TRY node,
280 |            * we know that we will be visiting either the CATCH or the FINALLY.
281 |            * In other words, we know that the post order traversal of the TRY
282 |            * block has been finished, no more exceptions can be caught by the
283 |            * handler at this TRY block and should be taken out of the stack.
284 |            */
285 |           if (n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext()) {
286 |             Preconditions.checkState(exceptionHandler.peek() == parent);
287 |             exceptionHandler.pop();
288 |           }
289 |       }
290 |     }
291 |     return true;
292 |   }

===== M0447 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.FixedPointGraphTraversal.computeFixedPoint(DiGraph<N, E>,Set<N>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/graph/FixedPointGraphTraversal.java
Original range: 93-128
Documentation:
/**
   * Compute a fixed point for the given graph, entering from the given nodes.
   * @param graph The graph to traverse.
   * @param entrySet The nodes to begin traversing from.
   */
Method code:
93 |   public void computeFixedPoint(DiGraph<N, E> graph, Set<N> entrySet) {
94 |     int cycleCount = 0;
95 |     long nodeCount = graph.getNodes().size();
96 | 
97 |     // Choose a bail-out heuristically in case the computation
98 |     // doesn't converge.
99 |     long maxIterations = Math.max(nodeCount * nodeCount * nodeCount, 100);
100 | 
101 |     // Use a LinkedHashSet, so that the traversal is deterministic.
102 |     LinkedHashSet<DiGraphNode<N, E>> workSet =
103 |         Sets.newLinkedHashSet();
104 |     for (N n : entrySet) {
105 |       workSet.add(graph.getDirectedGraphNode(n));
106 |     }
107 |     for (; !workSet.isEmpty() && cycleCount < maxIterations; cycleCount++) {
108 |       // For every out edge in the workSet, traverse that edge. If that
109 |       // edge updates the state of the graph, then add the destination
110 |       // node to the resultSet, so that we can update all of its out edges
111 |       // on the next iteration.
112 |       DiGraphNode<N, E> source = workSet.iterator().next();
113 |       N sourceValue = source.getValue();
114 | 
115 |       workSet.remove(source);
116 | 
117 |       List<DiGraphEdge<N, E>> outEdges = source.getOutEdges();
118 |       for (DiGraphEdge<N, E> edge : outEdges) {
119 |         N destNode = edge.getDestination().getValue();
120 |         if (callback.traverseEdge(sourceValue, edge.getValue(), destNode)) {
121 |           workSet.add(edge.getDestination());
122 |         }
123 |       }
124 |     }
125 | 
126 |     Preconditions.checkState(cycleCount != maxIterations,
127 |         NON_HALTING_ERROR_MSG);
128 |   }

===== M0448 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.addChildToBack(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 601-612
Documentation:

Method code:
601 |   public void addChildToBack(Node child) {
602 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(child.parent == null);
603 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(child.next == null);
604 |     child.parent = this;
605 |     child.next = null;
606 |     if (last == null) {
607 |       first = last = child;
608 |       return;
609 |     }
610 |     last.next = child;
611 |     last = child;
612 |   }

===== M0452 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleReturn(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 622-649
Documentation:

Method code:
622 |   private void handleReturn(Node node) {
623 |     Node lastJump = null;
624 |     for (Iterator<Node> iter = exceptionHandler.iterator(); iter.hasNext();) {
625 |       Node curHandler = iter.next();
626 |       if (NodeUtil.isFunction(curHandler)) {
627 |         break;
628 |       }
629 |       if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(curHandler)) {
630 |         if (lastJump == null) {
631 |           createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, curHandler.getLastChild());
632 |         } else {
633 |           finallyMap.put(lastJump,
634 |               computeFallThrough(curHandler.getLastChild()));
635 |         }
636 |         lastJump = curHandler;
637 |       }
638 |     }
639 | 
640 |     if (node.hasChildren()) {
641 |       connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(node, node.getFirstChild());
642 |     }
643 | 
644 |     if (lastJump == null) {
645 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, null);
646 |     } else {
647 |       finallyMap.put(lastJump, null);
648 |     }
649 |   }

===== M0456 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.isPropertyTest(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1199-1225
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether this node is testing for the existence of a property.
   * If true, we will not emit warnings about a missing property.
   *
   * @param getProp The GETPROP being tested.
   */
Method code:
1199 |   private boolean isPropertyTest(Node getProp) {
1200 |     Node parent = getProp.getParent();
1201 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
1202 |       case Token.CALL:
1203 |         return parent.getFirstChild() != getProp &&
1204 |             compiler.getCodingConvention().isPropertyTestFunction(parent);
1205 | 
1206 |       case Token.IF:
1207 |       case Token.WHILE:
1208 |       case Token.DO:
1209 |       case Token.FOR:
1210 |         return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) == getProp;
1211 | 
1212 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
1213 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
1214 |         return true;
1215 | 
1216 |       case Token.AND:
1217 |       case Token.HOOK:
1218 |         return parent.getFirstChild() == getProp;
1219 | 
1220 |       case Token.NOT:
1221 |         return parent.getParent().getType() == Token.OR &&
1222 |             parent.getParent().getFirstChild() == parent;
1223 |     }
1224 |     return false;
1225 |   }

/**
   * Visits a GETPROP node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of <code>n</code>
   */
Method code:
1138 |   private void visitGetProp(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1139 |     // GETPROP nodes have an assigned type on their node by the scope creator
1140 |     // if this is an enum declaration. The only namespaced enum declarations
1141 |     // that we allow are of the form object.name = ...;
1142 |     if (n.getJSType() != null && parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN) {
1143 |       return;
1144 |     }
1145 | 
1146 |     // obj.prop or obj.method()
1147 |     // Lots of types can appear on the left, a call to a void function can
1148 |     // never be on the left. getPropertyType will decide what is acceptable
1149 |     // and what isn't.
1150 |     Node property = n.getLastChild();
1151 |     Node objNode = n.getFirstChild();
1152 |     JSType childType = getJSType(objNode);
1153 | 
1154 |     // TODO(user): remove in favor of flagging every property access on
1155 |     // non-object.
1156 |     if (!validator.expectNotVoid(t, n, childType,
1157 |             "undefined has no properties", getNativeType(OBJECT_TYPE))) {
1158 |       ensureTyped(t, n);
1159 |       return;
1160 |     }
1161 | 
1162 |     checkPropertyAccess(childType, property.getString(), t, n);
1163 |     ensureTyped(t, n);
1164 |   }

===== M0471 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1040-1177
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1040 |   Node parseInputs() {
1041 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1042 | 
1043 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1044 |     // individual file parse trees.
1045 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1046 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1047 |     }
1048 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1049 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1050 |     }
1051 | 
1052 |     // Parse main js sources.
1053 |     jsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1054 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1055 | 
1056 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1057 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot,
1058 |           options.tracer == TracerMode.ALL);
1059 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1060 |     }
1061 | 
1062 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1063 | 
1064 |     try {
1065 |       // Parse externs sources.
1066 |       externsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1067 |       externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1068 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1069 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1070 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1071 |           return null;
1072 |         }
1073 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1074 |       }
1075 | 
1076 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1077 |       if (options.manageClosureDependencies) {
1078 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1079 |           input.setCompiler(this);
1080 | 
1081 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1082 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1083 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1084 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1085 |           }
1086 |         }
1087 | 
1088 |         try {
1089 |           inputs =
1090 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1091 |               .manageDependencies(
1092 |                   options.manageClosureDependenciesEntryPoints, inputs);
1093 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1094 |           report(JSError.make(
1095 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1096 |           return null;
1097 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1098 |           report(JSError.make(
1099 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1100 |           return null;
1101 |         }
1102 |       }
1103 | 
1104 |       // Check if inputs need to be rebuilt from modules.
1105 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1106 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1107 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1108 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1109 |           return null;
1110 |         }
1111 | 
1112 |         // Inputs can have a null AST during initial parse.
1113 |         if (n == null) {
1114 |           continue;
1115 |         }
1116 | 
1117 |         if (n.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
1118 |           JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1119 |           if (info.isExterns()) {
1120 |             // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1121 |             // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1122 |             // the externs pile anyways.
1123 |             externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1124 |             input.setIsExtern(true);
1125 | 
1126 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1127 | 
1128 |             externs.add(input);
1129 |             staleInputs = true;
1130 |           } else if (info.isNoCompile()) {
1131 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1132 |             staleInputs = true;
1133 |           }
1134 |         }
1135 |       }
1136 | 
1137 |       if (staleInputs) {
1138 |         fillEmptyModules(modules);
1139 |         rebuildInputsFromModules();
1140 |       }
1141 | 
1142 |       // Build the AST.
1143 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1144 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1145 |         if (n == null) {
1146 |           continue;
1147 |         }
1148 | 
1149 |         if (devMode) {
1150 |           runSanityCheck();
1151 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1152 |             return null;
1153 |           }
1154 |         }
1155 | 
1156 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1157 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1158 | 
1159 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1160 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1161 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1162 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1163 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1164 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1165 |         }
1166 | 
1167 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1168 |       }
1169 | 
1170 |       externAndJsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK, externsRoot, jsRoot);
1171 |       externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1172 | 
1173 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1174 |     } finally {
1175 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1176 |     }
1177 |   }

===== M0472 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.add(Node,Context)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 75-712
Documentation:

Method code:
75 |   void add(Node n, Context context) {
76 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {
77 |       return;
78 |     }
79 | 
80 |     int type = n.getType();
81 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);
82 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
83 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
84 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
85 | 
86 |     // Handle all binary operators
87 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
88 |       Preconditions.checkState(
89 |           childCount == 2,
90 |           "Bad binary operator \"%s\": expected 2 arguments but got %s",
91 |           opstr, childCount);
92 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
93 |       addLeftExpr(first, p, context);
94 |       cc.addOp(opstr, true);
95 | 
96 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
97 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
98 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
99 | 
100 |       // Handle associativity.
101 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
102 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.
103 |       if (last.getType() == type &&
104 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {
105 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
106 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
107 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
108 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
109 |       } else {
110 |         addExpr(last, p + 1, rhsContext);
111 |       }
112 |       return;
113 |     }
114 | 
115 |     cc.startSourceMapping(n);
116 | 
117 |     switch (type) {
118 |       case Token.TRY: {
119 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().getType() == Token.BLOCK &&
120 |                 !first.getNext().hasMoreThanOneChild());
121 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
122 | 
123 |         add("try");
124 |         add(first, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
125 | 
126 |         // second child contains the catch block, or nothing if there
127 |         // isn't a catch block
128 |         Node catchblock = first.getNext().getFirstChild();
129 |         if (catchblock != null) {
130 |           add(catchblock);
131 |         }
132 | 
133 |         if (childCount == 3) {
134 |           add("finally");
135 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
136 |         }
137 |         break;
138 |       }
139 | 
140 |       case Token.CATCH:
141 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
142 |         add("catch(");
143 |         add(first);
144 |         add(")");
145 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
146 |         break;
147 | 
148 |       case Token.THROW:
149 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
150 |         add("throw");
151 |         add(first);
152 | 
153 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
154 |         // parse this.
155 |         cc.endStatement(true);
156 |         break;
157 | 
158 |       case Token.RETURN:
159 |         add("return");
160 |         if (childCount == 1) {
161 |           add(first);
162 |         } else {
163 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
164 |         }
165 |         cc.endStatement();
166 |         break;
167 | 
168 |       case Token.VAR:
169 |         if (first != null) {
170 |           add("var ");
171 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
172 |         }
173 |         break;
174 | 
175 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
176 |         Preconditions.checkState(!n.getString().isEmpty());
177 |         addIdentifier(n.getString());
178 |         break;
179 | 
180 |       case Token.NAME:
181 |         if (first == null || first.getType() == Token.EMPTY) {
182 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
183 |         } else {
184 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
185 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
186 |           cc.addOp("=", true);
187 |           if (first.getType() == Token.COMMA) {
188 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN));
189 |           } else {
190 |             // Add expression, consider nearby code at lowest level of
191 |             // precedence.
192 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
193 |           }
194 |         }
195 |         break;
196 | 
197 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
198 |         add("[");
199 |         addList(first, (int[]) n.getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP));
200 |         add("]");
201 |         break;
202 | 
203 |       case Token.LP:
204 |         add("(");
205 |         addList(first);
206 |         add(")");
207 |         break;
208 | 
209 |       case Token.COMMA:
210 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
211 |         addList(first, false, context);
212 |         break;
213 | 
214 |       case Token.NUMBER:
215 |         Preconditions.checkState(
216 |             childCount ==
217 |             ((n.getParent() != null &&
218 |               n.getParent().getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) ? 1 : 0));
219 |         cc.addNumber(n.getDouble());
220 |         break;
221 | 
222 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
223 |       case Token.VOID:
224 |       case Token.NOT:
225 |       case Token.BITNOT:
226 |       case Token.POS: {
227 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
228 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
229 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
230 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type));
231 |         break;
232 |       }
233 | 
234 |       case Token.NEG: {
235 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
236 | 
237 |         // It's important to our sanity checker that the code
238 |         // we print produces the same AST as the code we parse back.
239 |         // NEG is a weird case because Rhino parses "- -2" as "2".
240 |         if (n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NUMBER) {
241 |           cc.addNumber(-n.getFirstChild().getDouble());
242 |         } else {
243 |           cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
244 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type));
245 |         }
246 | 
247 |         break;
248 |       }
249 | 
250 |       case Token.HOOK: {
251 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
252 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
253 |         addLeftExpr(first, p + 1, context);
254 |         cc.addOp("?", true);
255 |         addExpr(first.getNext(), 1);
256 |         cc.addOp(":", true);
257 |         addExpr(last, 1);
258 |         break;
259 |       }
260 | 
261 |       case Token.REGEXP:
262 |         if (first.getType() != Token.STRING ||
263 |             last.getType() != Token.STRING) {
264 |           throw new Error("Expected children to be strings");
265 |         }
266 | 
267 |         String regexp = regexpEscape(first.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder);
268 | 
269 |         // I only use one .add because whitespace matters
270 |         if (childCount == 2) {
271 |           add(regexp + last.getString());
272 |         } else {
273 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
274 |           add(regexp);
275 |         }
276 |         break;
277 | 
278 |       case Token.GET_REF:
279 |         add(first);
280 |         break;
281 | 
282 |       case Token.REF_SPECIAL:
283 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
284 |         add(first);
285 |         add(".");
286 |         add((String) n.getProp(Node.NAME_PROP));
287 |         break;
288 | 
289 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
290 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
291 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
292 |         }
293 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
294 |         boolean funcNeedsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
295 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
296 |           add("(");
297 |         }
298 | 
299 |         add("function");
300 |         add(first);
301 | 
302 |         add(first.getNext());
303 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
304 |         cc.endFunction(context == Context.STATEMENT);
305 | 
306 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
307 |           add(")");
308 |         }
309 |         break;
310 | 
311 |       case Token.GET:
312 |       case Token.SET:
313 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getParent().getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT);
314 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
315 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
316 | 
317 |         // Get methods are unnamed
318 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getFirstChild().getString().isEmpty());
319 |         if (type == Token.GET) {
320 |           // Get methods have no parameters.
321 |           Preconditions.checkState(!first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasChildren());
322 |           add("get ");
323 |         } else {
324 |           // Set methods have one parameter.
325 |           Preconditions.checkState(first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasOneChild());
326 |           add("set ");
327 |         }
328 | 
329 |         // The name is on the GET or SET node.
330 |         String name = n.getString();
331 |         Node fn = first;
332 |         Node parameters = fn.getChildAtIndex(1);
333 |         Node body = fn.getLastChild();
334 | 
335 |         // Add the property name.
336 |         if (TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(name) &&
337 |             // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
338 |             // unicode escaped.
339 |             NodeUtil.isLatin(name)) {
340 |           add(name);
341 |         } else {
342 |           add(jsString(n.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder));
343 |         }
344 | 
345 |         add(parameters);
346 |         add(body, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
347 |         break;
348 | 
349 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
350 |       case Token.BLOCK: {
351 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
352 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
353 |         }
354 |         boolean preserveBlock = context == Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
355 |         if (preserveBlock) {
356 |           cc.beginBlock();
357 |         }
358 | 
359 |         boolean preferLineBreaks =
360 |             type == Token.SCRIPT ||
361 |             (type == Token.BLOCK &&
362 |                 !preserveBlock &&
363 |                 n.getParent() != null &&
364 |                 n.getParent().getType() == Token.SCRIPT);
365 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
366 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);
367 | 
368 |           // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions
369 |           if (c.getType() == Token.VAR) {
370 |             cc.endStatement();
371 |           }
372 | 
373 |           if (c.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
374 |             cc.maybeLineBreak();
375 |           }
376 | 
377 |           // Prefer to break lines in between top-level statements
378 |           // because top level statements are more homogeneous.
379 |           if (preferLineBreaks) {
380 |             cc.notePreferredLineBreak();
381 |           }
382 |         }
383 |         if (preserveBlock) {
384 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
385 |         }
386 |         break;
387 |       }
388 | 
389 |       case Token.FOR:
390 |         if (childCount == 4) {
391 |           add("for(");
392 |           if (first.getType() == Token.VAR) {
393 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
394 |           } else {
395 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
396 |           }
397 |           add(";");
398 |           add(first.getNext());
399 |           add(";");
400 |           add(first.getNext().getNext());
401 |           add(")");
402 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
403 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
404 |         } else {
405 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
406 |           add("for(");
407 |           add(first);
408 |           add("in");
409 |           add(first.getNext());
410 |           add(")");
411 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
412 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
413 |         }
414 |         break;
415 | 
416 |       case Token.DO:
417 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
418 |         add("do");
419 |         addNonEmptyStatement(first, Context.OTHER, false);
420 |         add("while(");
421 |         add(last);
422 |         add(")");
423 |         cc.endStatement();
424 |         break;
425 | 
426 |       case Token.WHILE:
427 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
428 |         add("while(");
429 |         add(first);
430 |         add(")");
431 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
432 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
433 |         break;
434 | 
435 |       case Token.EMPTY:
436 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
437 |         break;
438 | 
439 |       case Token.GETPROP: {
440 |         Preconditions.checkState(
441 |             childCount == 2,
442 |             "Bad GETPROP: expected 2 children, but got %s", childCount);
443 |         Preconditions.checkState(
444 |             last.getType() == Token.STRING,
445 |             "Bad GETPROP: RHS should be STRING");
446 |         boolean needsParens = (first.getType() == Token.NUMBER);
447 |         if (needsParens) {
448 |           add("(");
449 |         }
450 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
451 |         if (needsParens) {
452 |           add(")");
453 |         }
454 |         add(".");
455 |         addIdentifier(last.getString());
456 |         break;
457 |       }
458 | 
459 |       case Token.GETELEM:
460 |         Preconditions.checkState(
461 |             childCount == 2,
462 |             "Bad GETELEM: expected 2 children but got %s", childCount);
463 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
464 |         add("[");
465 |         add(first.getNext());
466 |         add("]");
467 |         break;
468 | 
469 |       case Token.WITH:
470 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
471 |         add("with(");
472 |         add(first);
473 |         add(")");
474 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
475 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
476 |         break;
477 | 
478 |       case Token.INC:
479 |       case Token.DEC: {
480 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
481 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
482 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP);
483 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
484 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
485 |         if (postProp != 0) {
486 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
487 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
488 |         } else {
489 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
490 |           add(first);
491 |         }
492 |         break;
493 |       }
494 | 
495 |       case Token.CALL:
496 |         // We have two special cases here:
497 |         // 1) If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
498 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
499 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
500 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
501 |         // 2) If the left hand side of the call is a property reference,
502 |         // then the call must not a FREE_CALL annotation. If it does, then
503 |         // that means it was originally an call without an explicit this and
504 |         // that must be preserved.
505 |         if (isIndirectEval(first)
506 |             || n.getBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL) && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
507 |           add("(0,");
508 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.COMMA));
509 |           add(")");
510 |         } else {
511 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
512 |         }
513 |         add("(");
514 |         addList(first.getNext());
515 |         add(")");
516 |         break;
517 | 
518 |       case Token.IF:
519 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
520 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
521 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
522 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
523 |           cc.beginBlock();
524 |         }
525 | 
526 |         add("if(");
527 |         add(first);
528 |         add(")");
529 | 
530 |         if (hasElse) {
531 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
532 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
533 |           add("else");
534 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
535 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
536 |         } else {
537 |           addNonEmptyStatement(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
538 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
539 |         }
540 | 
541 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
542 |           cc.endBlock();
543 |         }
544 |         break;
545 | 
546 |       case Token.NULL:
547 |       case Token.THIS:
548 |       case Token.FALSE:
549 |       case Token.TRUE:
550 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
551 |         add(Node.tokenToName(type));
552 |         break;
553 | 
554 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
555 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
556 |         add("continue");
557 |         if (childCount == 1) {
558 |           if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
559 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
560 |           }
561 |           add(" ");
562 |           add(first);
563 |         }
564 |         cc.endStatement();
565 |         break;
566 | 
567 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
568 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
569 |         add("debugger");
570 |         cc.endStatement();
571 |         break;
572 | 
573 |       case Token.BREAK:
574 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
575 |         add("break");
576 |         if (childCount == 1) {
577 |           if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
578 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
579 |           }
580 |           add(" ");
581 |           add(first);
582 |         }
583 |         cc.endStatement();
584 |         break;
585 | 
586 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
587 |         throw new Error("Unexpected EXPR_VOID. Should be EXPR_RESULT.");
588 | 
589 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
590 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
591 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
592 |         cc.endStatement();
593 |         break;
594 | 
595 |       case Token.NEW:
596 |         add("new ");
597 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
598 | 
599 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
600 |         // to force parentheses. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
601 |         // first viable parentheses (don't traverse into functions).
602 |         if (NodeUtil.containsType(first, Token.CALL, new MatchNotFunction())) {
603 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
604 |         }
605 |         addExpr(first, precedence);
606 | 
607 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
608 |         Node next = first.getNext();
609 |         if (next != null) {
610 |           add("(");
611 |           addList(next);
612 |           add(")");
613 |         }
614 |         break;
615 | 
616 |       case Token.STRING:
617 |         if (childCount !=
618 |             ((n.getParent() != null &&
619 |               n.getParent().getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) ? 1 : 0)) {
620 |           throw new IllegalStateException(
621 |               "Unexpected String children: " + n.getParent().toStringTree());
622 |         }
623 |         add(jsString(n.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder));
624 |         break;
625 | 
626 |       case Token.DELPROP:
627 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
628 |         add("delete ");
629 |         add(first);
630 |         break;
631 | 
632 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
633 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
634 |         if (needsParens) {
635 |           add("(");
636 |         }
637 |         add("{");
638 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
639 |           if (c != first) {
640 |             cc.listSeparator();
641 |           }
642 | 
643 |           if (c.getType() == Token.GET || c.getType() == Token.SET) {
644 |             add(c);
645 |           } else {
646 |             // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they are
647 |             // not JavaScript keywords
648 |             if (c.getType() == Token.STRING &&
649 |                 !TokenStream.isKeyword(c.getString()) &&
650 |                 TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(c.getString()) &&
651 |                 // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
652 |                 // unicode escaped.
653 |                 NodeUtil.isLatin(c.getString())) {
654 |               add(c.getString());
655 |             } else {
656 |               addExpr(c, 1);
657 |             }
658 |             add(":");
659 |             addExpr(c.getFirstChild(), 1);
660 |           }
661 |         }
662 |         add("}");
663 |         if (needsParens) {
664 |           add(")");
665 |         }
666 |         break;
667 |       }
668 | 
669 |       case Token.SWITCH:
670 |         add("switch(");
671 |         add(first);
672 |         add(")");
673 |         cc.beginBlock();
674 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
675 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
676 |         break;
677 | 
678 |       case Token.CASE:
679 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
680 |         add("case ");
681 |         add(first);
682 |         addCaseBody(last);
683 |         break;
684 | 
685 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
686 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
687 |         add("default");
688 |         addCaseBody(first);
689 |         break;
690 | 
691 |       case Token.LABEL:
692 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
693 |         if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
694 |           throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
695 |         }
696 |         add(first);
697 |         add(":");
698 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
699 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
700 |         break;
701 | 
702 |       // This node is auto generated in anonymous functions and should just get
703 |       // ignored for our purposes.
704 |       case Token.SETNAME:
705 |         break;
706 | 
707 |       default:
708 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
709 |     }
710 | 
711 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
712 |   }

===== M0473 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 221-257
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
221 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
222 |     this.options = options;
223 |     if (errorManager == null) {
224 |       if (outStream == null) {
225 |         setErrorManager(
226 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
227 |       } else {
228 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
229 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
230 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
231 |         setErrorManager(printer);
232 |       }
233 |     }
234 | 
235 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
236 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
237 |     guards.add(
238 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
239 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
240 |     WarningsGuard warningsGuard = options.getWarningsGuard();
241 |     if (warningsGuard != null) {
242 |       guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
243 |     }
244 | 
245 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
246 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
247 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
248 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
249 |     // error off.
250 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
251 |         (warningsGuard == null || !warningsGuard.disables(
252 |             DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES))) {
253 |       guards.add(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
254 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
255 |     }
256 |     this.warningsGuard = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
257 |   }

50 |       // outside of straight ASCII), then don't use the encoder, but

===== M0479 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(String,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 85-119
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceName The filename.
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
85 |   public static Node parse(String sourceName,
86 |                            String sourceString,
87 |                            Config config,
88 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
89 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
90 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
91 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
92 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
93 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
94 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
95 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
96 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
97 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(true);
98 |     if (config.isIdeMode) {
99 |       compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(true);
100 |       compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(true);
101 |     }
102 | 
103 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
104 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
105 |     try {
106 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceName, 1);
107 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
108 |       logger.info("Error parsing " + sourceName + ": " + e.getMessage());
109 |     } finally {
110 |       Context.exit();
111 |     }
112 |     Node root = null;
113 |     if (astRoot != null) {
114 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
115 |           astRoot, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
116 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
117 |     }
118 |     return root;
119 |   }

===== M0480 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getInfoForNameNode(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2349-2363
Documentation:
/**
   * @param nameNode A name node
   * @return The JSDocInfo for the name node
   */
Method code:
2349 |   static JSDocInfo getInfoForNameNode(Node nameNode) {
2350 |     JSDocInfo info = null;
2351 |     Node parent = null;

===== M0483 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.tightenTypesAfterAssertions(FlowScope,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 809-853
Documentation:

Method code:
809 |   private FlowScope tightenTypesAfterAssertions(FlowScope scope,
810 |       Node callNode) {
811 |     Node left = callNode.getFirstChild();
812 |     Node firstParam = left.getNext();
813 |     AssertionFunctionSpec assertionFunctionSpec =
814 |         assertionFunctionsMap.get(left.getQualifiedName());
815 |     if (assertionFunctionSpec == null || firstParam == null) {
816 |       return scope;
817 |     }
818 |     Node assertedNode = assertionFunctionSpec.getAssertedParam(firstParam);
819 |     if (assertedNode == null) {
820 |       return scope;
821 |     }
822 |     JSTypeNative assertedType = assertionFunctionSpec.getAssertedType();
823 |     String assertedNodeName = assertedNode.getQualifiedName();
824 |     // Handle assertions that enforce expressions evaluate to true.
825 |     if (assertedType == null) {
826 |       if (assertedNodeName != null) {
827 |         JSType type = getJSType(assertedNode);
828 |         JSType narrowed = type.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
829 |         if (type != narrowed) {
830 |           scope = scope.createChildFlowScope();
831 |           redeclare(scope, assertedNodeName, narrowed);
832 |           callNode.setJSType(narrowed);
833 |         }
834 |       } else if (assertedNode.getType() == Token.AND ||
835 |                  assertedNode.getType() == Token.OR) {
836 |         BooleanOutcomePair conditionOutcomes =
837 |             traverseWithinShortCircuitingBinOp(assertedNode, scope);
838 |         scope = reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
839 |             assertedNode, conditionOutcomes.getOutcomeFlowScope(
840 |                 assertedNode.getType(), true), true);
841 |       }
842 |     } else if (assertedNodeName != null) {
843 |       // Handle assertions that enforce expressions are of a certain type.
844 |       JSType type = getJSType(assertedNode);
845 |       JSType narrowed = type.getGreatestSubtype(getNativeType(assertedType));
846 |       if (type != narrowed) {
847 |         scope = scope.createChildFlowScope();
848 |         redeclare(scope, assertedNodeName, narrowed);
849 |         callNode.setJSType(narrowed);
850 |       }
851 |     }
852 |     return scope;
853 |   }

/**
   * Resolve this type in the given scope.
   *
   * The returned value must be equal to {@code this}, as defined by
   * {@link #isEquivalentTo}. It may or may not be the same object. This method
   * may modify the internal state of {@code this}, as long as it does
   * so in a way that preserves Object equality.
   *
   * For efficiency, we should only resolve a type once per compilation job.
   * For incremental compilations, one compilation job may need the
   * artifacts from a previous generation, so we will eventually need
   * a generational flag instead of a boolean one.
   */
Method code:
905 |   public final JSType resolve(ErrorReporter t, StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
906 |     if (resolved) {
907 |       // TODO(nicksantos): Check to see if resolve() looped back on itself.
908 |       // Preconditions.checkNotNull(resolveResult);
909 |       if (resolveResult == null) {
910 |         return registry.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.UNKNOWN_TYPE);
911 |       }
912 |       return resolveResult;
913 |     }
914 |     resolved = true;
915 |     resolveResult = resolveInternal(t, scope);
916 |     resolveResult.setResolvedTypeInternal(resolveResult);
917 |     return resolveResult;
918 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.BranchedForwardDataFlowAnalysis.flow(DiGraphNode<N, Branch>)

Documentation:

Method code:
443 |     @Override
444 |     protected final boolean flow(DiGraphNode<N, Branch> node) {
445 |       BranchedFlowState<L> state = node.getAnnotation();
446 |       List<L> outBefore = state.out;
447 |       state.out = branchedFlowThrough(node.getValue(), state.in);
448 |       Preconditions.checkState(outBefore.size() == state.out.size());
449 |       for (int i = 0; i < outBefore.size(); i++) {
450 |         if (!outBefore.get(i).equals(state.out.get(i))) {
451 |           return true;
452 |         }
453 |       }
454 |       return false;

===== M0505 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFallThrough(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 779-793
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the destination node of n when we want to fallthough into the
   * subtree of n. We don't always create a CFG edge into n itself because of
   * DOs and FORs.
   */
Method code:
779 |   static Node computeFallThrough(Node n) {
780 |     switch (n.getType()) {
781 |       case Token.DO:
782 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
783 |       case Token.FOR:
784 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
785 |           return n;
786 |         }
787 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
788 |       case Token.LABEL:
789 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getLastChild());
790 |       default:
791 |         return n;
792 |     }
793 |   }

===== M0515 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseModifiesTag(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 928-967
Documentation:
/**
   * Parse a {@code @modifies} tag of the form
   * {@code @modifies&#123;this|arguments|param&#125;}.
   *
   * @param token The current token.
   */
Method code:
928 |   private JsDocToken parseModifiesTag(JsDocToken token) {
929 |     if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
930 |       Set<String> modifies = new HashSet<String>();
931 |       while (true) {
932 |         if (match(JsDocToken.STRING)) {
933 |           String name = stream.getString();
934 |           if (!modifiesAnnotationKeywords.contains(name)
935 |               && !jsdocBuilder.hasParameter(name)) {
936 |               parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.modifies.unknown", name,
937 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
938 |           }
939 | 
940 |           modifies.add(stream.getString());
941 |           token = next();
942 |         } else {
943 |           parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.modifies",
944 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
945 |           return token;
946 |         }
947 | 
948 |         if (match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
949 |           token = next();
950 |         } else {
951 |           break;
952 |         }
953 |       }
954 | 
955 |       if (!match(JsDocToken.RC)) {
956 |         parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.modifies",
957 |             stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
958 |       } else {
959 |         token = next();
960 |         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordModifies(modifies)) {
961 |           parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.modifies.duplicate",
962 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
963 |         }
964 |       }
965 |     }
966 |     return token;
967 |   }

===== M0519 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PureFunctionIdentifier.FunctionAnalyzer.visitAssignmentOrUnaryOperator(FunctionInformation,Scope,Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PureFunctionIdentifier.java
Original range: 526-577
Documentation:
/**
     * Record information about the side effects caused by an
     * assigment or mutating unary operator.
     *
     * If the operation modifies this or taints global state, mark the
     * enclosing function as having those side effects.
     * @param op operation being performed.
     * @param lhs The store location (name or get) being operated on.
     * @param rhs The right have value, if any.
     */
Method code:
526 |     private void visitAssignmentOrUnaryOperator(
527 |         FunctionInformation sideEffectInfo,
528 |         Scope scope, Node op, Node lhs, Node rhs) {
529 |       if (NodeUtil.isName(lhs)) {
530 |         Var var = scope.getVar(lhs.getString());
531 |         if (var == null || var.scope != scope) {
532 |           sideEffectInfo.setTaintsGlobalState();
533 |         } else {
534 |           // Assignment to local, if the value isn't a safe local value,
535 |           // a literal or new object creation, add it to the local blacklist.
536 |           // parameter values depend on the caller.
537 | 
538 |           // Note: other ops result in the name or prop being assigned a local
539 |           // value (x++ results in a number, for instance)
540 |           Preconditions.checkState(
541 |               NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(op)
542 |               || isIncDec(op) || op.getType() == Token.DELPROP);
543 |           if (rhs != null
544 |               && NodeUtil.isAssign(op)
545 |               && !NodeUtil.evaluatesToLocalValue(rhs)) {
546 |             sideEffectInfo.blacklistLocal(var);
547 |           }
548 |         }
549 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isGet(lhs)) {
550 |         if (NodeUtil.isThis(lhs.getFirstChild())) {
551 |           sideEffectInfo.setTaintsThis();
552 |         } else {
553 |           Var var = null;
554 |           Node objectNode = lhs.getFirstChild();
555 |           if (NodeUtil.isName(objectNode)) {
556 |             var = scope.getVar(objectNode.getString());
557 |           }
558 |           if (var == null || var.scope != scope) {
559 |             sideEffectInfo.setTaintsUnknown();
560 |           } else {
561 |             // Maybe a local object modification.  We won't know for sure until
562 |             // we exit the scope and can validate the value of the local.
563 |             //
564 |             sideEffectInfo.addTaintedLocalObject(var);
565 |           }
566 |         }
567 |       } else {
568 |         // TODO(johnlenz): track down what is inserting NULL on the lhs
569 |         // of an assign.
570 | 
571 |         // The only valid lhs expressions are NAME, GETELEM, or GETPROP.
572 |         // throw new IllegalStateException(
573 |         //     "Unexpected lhs expression:" + lhs.toStringTree()
574 |         //    + ", parent: " + op.toStringTree() );
575 |         sideEffectInfo.setTaintsUnknown();
576 |       }
577 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.getPrototypePropertyOwner(Node)

Documentation:
/**
   * Given a node, determines whether that node names a prototype
   * property, and if so, returns the qualified name node representing
   * the owner of that property. Otherwise, returns null.
   */
Method code:
339 |   private static Node getPrototypePropertyOwner(Node n) {
340 |     if (n.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
341 |       Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();
342 |       if (firstChild.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
343 |           firstChild.getLastChild().getString().equals("prototype")) {
344 |         Node maybeOwner = firstChild.getFirstChild();
345 |         if (maybeOwner.isQualifiedName()) {
346 |           return maybeOwner;
347 |         }
348 |       }
349 |     }
350 |     return null;
351 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PureFunctionIdentifier.FunctionAnalyzer.visitFunction(NodeTraversal,Node,Node,Node)

Original range: 601-662
