/**
     * Record function and check for @nosideeffects annotations.
     */
Method code:
601 |     private void visitFunction(NodeTraversal traversal,
602 |                                Node node,
603 |                                Node parent,
604 |                                Node gramp) {
605 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(!functionSideEffectMap.containsKey(node));
606 | 
607 |       FunctionInformation sideEffectInfo = new FunctionInformation(inExterns);
608 |       functionSideEffectMap.put(node, sideEffectInfo);
609 | 
610 |       if (inExterns) {
611 |         JSType jstype = node.getJSType();
612 |         boolean knownLocalResult = false;
613 |         if (jstype != null && jstype.isFunctionType()) {
614 |           FunctionType functionType = (FunctionType) jstype;
615 |           JSType jstypeReturn = functionType.getReturnType();
616 |           if (isLocalValueType(jstypeReturn, true)) {
617 |             knownLocalResult = true;
618 |           }
619 |         }
620 |         if (!knownLocalResult) {
621 |           sideEffectInfo.setTaintsReturn();
622 |         }
623 |       }
624 | 
625 |       JSDocInfo info = getJSDocInfoForFunction(node, parent, gramp);
626 |       if (info != null) {
627 |         boolean hasSpecificSideEffects = false;
628 |         if (hasSideEffectsThisAnnotation(info)) {
629 |           if (inExterns) {
630 |             hasSpecificSideEffects = true;
631 |             sideEffectInfo.setTaintsThis();
632 |           } else {
633 |             traversal.report(node, INVALID_MODIFIES_ANNOTATION);
634 |           }
635 |         }
636 | 
637 |         if (hasSideEffectsArgumentsAnnotation(info)) {
638 |           if (inExterns) {
639 |             hasSpecificSideEffects = true;
640 |             sideEffectInfo.setTaintsArguments();
641 |           } else {
642 |             traversal.report(node, INVALID_MODIFIES_ANNOTATION);
643 |           }
644 |         }
645 | 
646 |         if (!hasSpecificSideEffects) {
647 |           if (hasNoSideEffectsAnnotation(info)) {
648 |             if (inExterns) {
649 |               sideEffectInfo.setIsPure();
650 |             } else {
651 |               traversal.report(node, INVALID_NO_SIDE_EFFECT_ANNOTATION);
652 |             }
653 |           } else if (inExterns) {
654 |             sideEffectInfo.setTaintsGlobalState();
655 |           }
656 |         }
657 |       } else {
658 |         if (inExterns) {
659 |           sideEffectInfo.setTaintsGlobalState();
660 |         }
661 |       }
662 |     }

===== M0522 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 447-793
Documentation:
/**
   * This is the meat of the type checking.  It is basically one big switch,
   * with each case representing one type of parse tree node.  The individual
   * cases are usually pretty straightforward.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of the node n.
   */
Method code:
447 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
448 |     JSType childType;
449 |     JSType leftType, rightType;
450 |     Node left, right;
451 |     // To be explicitly set to false if the node is not typeable.
452 |     boolean typeable = true;
453 | 
454 |     switch (n.getType()) {
455 |       case Token.NAME:
456 |         typeable = visitName(t, n, parent);
457 |         break;
458 | 
459 |       case Token.LP:
460 |         // If this is under a FUNCTION node, it is a parameter list and can be
461 |         // ignored here.
462 |         if (parent.getType() != Token.FUNCTION) {
463 |           ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getFirstChild()));
464 |         } else {
465 |           typeable = false;
466 |         }
467 |         break;
468 | 
469 |       case Token.COMMA:
470 |         ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getLastChild()));
471 |         break;
472 | 
473 |       case Token.TRUE:
474 |       case Token.FALSE:
475 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
476 |         break;
477 | 
478 |       case Token.THIS:
479 |         ensureTyped(t, n, t.getScope().getTypeOfThis());
480 |         break;
481 | 
482 |       case Token.REF_SPECIAL:
483 |         ensureTyped(t, n);
484 |         break;
485 | 
486 |       case Token.GET_REF:
487 |         ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getFirstChild()));
488 |         break;
489 | 
490 |       case Token.NULL:
491 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NULL_TYPE);
492 |         break;
493 | 
494 |       case Token.NUMBER:
495 |         if (n.getParent().getType() != Token.OBJECTLIT) {
496 |           ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
497 |         } else {
498 |           typeable = false;
499 |         }
500 |         break;
501 | 
502 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
503 |         ensureTyped(t, n, ARRAY_TYPE);
504 |         break;
505 | 
506 |       case Token.STRING:
507 |         if (n.getParent().getType() != Token.OBJECTLIT) {
508 |           ensureTyped(t, n, STRING_TYPE);
509 |         } else {
510 |           typeable = false;
511 |         }
512 |         break;
513 | 
514 |       case Token.REGEXP:
515 |         ensureTyped(t, n, REGEXP_TYPE);
516 |         break;
517 | 
518 |       case Token.GETPROP:
519 |         visitGetProp(t, n, parent);
520 |         typeable = !(parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN &&
521 |                      parent.getFirstChild() == n);
522 |         break;
523 | 
524 |       case Token.GETELEM:
525 |         visitGetElem(t, n);
526 |         // The type of GETELEM is always unknown, so no point counting that.
527 |         // If that unknown leaks elsewhere (say by an assignment to another
528 |         // variable), then it will be counted.
529 |         typeable = false;
530 |         break;
531 | 
532 |       case Token.VAR:
533 |         visitVar(t, n);
534 |         typeable = false;
535 |         break;
536 | 
537 |       case Token.NEW:
538 |         visitNew(t, n);
539 |         typeable = true;
540 |         break;
541 | 
542 |       case Token.CALL:
543 |         visitCall(t, n);
544 |         typeable = !NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(parent);
545 |         break;
546 | 
547 |       case Token.RETURN:
548 |         visitReturn(t, n);
549 |         typeable = false;
550 |         break;
551 | 
552 |       case Token.DEC:
553 |       case Token.INC:
554 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
555 |         validator.expectNumber(
556 |             t, left, getJSType(left), "increment/decrement");
557 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
558 |         break;
559 | 
560 |       case Token.NOT:
561 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
562 |         break;
563 | 
564 |       case Token.VOID:
565 |         ensureTyped(t, n, VOID_TYPE);
566 |         break;
567 | 
568 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
569 |         ensureTyped(t, n, STRING_TYPE);
570 |         break;
571 | 
572 |       case Token.BITNOT:
573 |         childType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
574 |         if (!childType.matchesInt32Context()) {
575 |           report(t, n, BIT_OPERATION, NodeUtil.opToStr(n.getType()),
576 |               childType.toString());
577 |         }
578 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
579 |         break;
580 | 
581 |       case Token.POS:
582 |       case Token.NEG:
583 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
584 |         validator.expectNumber(t, left, getJSType(left), "sign operator");
585 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
586 |         break;
587 | 
588 |       case Token.EQ:
589 |       case Token.NE: {
590 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
591 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
592 | 
593 |         JSType leftTypeRestricted = leftType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
594 |         JSType rightTypeRestricted = rightType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
595 |         TernaryValue result =
596 |             leftTypeRestricted.testForEquality(rightTypeRestricted);
597 |         if (result != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
598 |           if (n.getType() == Token.NE) {
599 |             result = result.not();
600 |           }
601 |           report(t, n, DETERMINISTIC_TEST, leftType.toString(),
602 |               rightType.toString(), result.toString());
603 |         }
604 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
605 |         break;
606 |       }
607 | 
608 |       case Token.SHEQ:
609 |       case Token.SHNE: {
610 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
611 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
612 | 
613 |         JSType leftTypeRestricted = leftType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
614 |         JSType rightTypeRestricted = rightType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
615 |         if (!leftTypeRestricted.canTestForShallowEqualityWith(
616 |                 rightTypeRestricted)) {
617 |           report(t, n, DETERMINISTIC_TEST_NO_RESULT, leftType.toString(),
618 |               rightType.toString());
619 |         }
620 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
621 |         break;
622 |       }
623 | 
624 |       case Token.LT:
625 |       case Token.LE:
626 |       case Token.GT:
627 |       case Token.GE:
628 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
629 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
630 |         if (rightType.isNumber()) {
631 |           validator.expectNumber(
632 |               t, n, leftType, "left side of numeric comparison");
633 |         } else if (leftType.isNumber()) {
634 |           validator.expectNumber(
635 |               t, n, rightType, "right side of numeric comparison");
636 |         } else if (leftType.matchesNumberContext() &&
637 |                    rightType.matchesNumberContext()) {
638 |           // OK.
639 |         } else {
640 |           // Whether the comparison is numeric will be determined at runtime
641 |           // each time the expression is evaluated. Regardless, both operands
642 |           // should match a string context.
643 |           String message = "left side of comparison";
644 |           validator.expectString(t, n, leftType, message);
645 |           validator.expectNotVoid(
646 |               t, n, leftType, message, getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
647 |           message = "right side of comparison";
648 |           validator.expectString(t, n, rightType, message);
649 |           validator.expectNotVoid(
650 |               t, n, rightType, message, getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
651 |         }
652 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
653 |         break;
654 | 
655 |       case Token.IN:
656 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
657 |         right = n.getLastChild();
658 |         leftType = getJSType(left);
659 |         rightType = getJSType(right);
660 |         validator.expectObject(t, n, rightType, "'in' requires an object");
661 |         validator.expectString(t, left, leftType, "left side of 'in'");
662 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
663 |         break;
664 | 
665 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
666 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
667 |         right = n.getLastChild();
668 |         leftType = getJSType(left);
669 |         rightType = getJSType(right).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
670 | 
671 |         validator.expectAnyObject(
672 |             t, left, leftType, "deterministic instanceof yields false");
673 |         validator.expectActualObject(
674 |             t, right, rightType, "instanceof requires an object");
675 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
676 |         break;
677 | 
678 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
679 |         visitAssign(t, n);
680 |         typeable = false;
681 |         break;
682 | 
683 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
684 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
685 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
686 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
687 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
688 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
689 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
690 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
691 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
692 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
693 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
694 |       case Token.LSH:
695 |       case Token.RSH:
696 |       case Token.URSH:
697 |       case Token.DIV:
698 |       case Token.MOD:
699 |       case Token.BITOR:
700 |       case Token.BITXOR:
701 |       case Token.BITAND:
702 |       case Token.SUB:
703 |       case Token.ADD:
704 |       case Token.MUL:
705 |         visitBinaryOperator(n.getType(), t, n);
706 |         break;
707 | 
708 |       case Token.DELPROP:
709 |         if (!isReference(n.getFirstChild())) {
710 |           report(t, n, BAD_DELETE);
711 |         }
712 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
713 |         break;
714 | 
715 |       case Token.CASE:
716 |         JSType switchType = getJSType(parent.getFirstChild());
717 |         JSType caseType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
718 |         validator.expectSwitchMatchesCase(t, n, switchType, caseType);
719 |         typeable = false;
720 |         break;
721 | 
722 |       case Token.WITH: {
723 |         Node child = n.getFirstChild();
724 |         childType = getJSType(child);
725 |         validator.expectObject(
726 |             t, child, childType, "with requires an object");
727 |         typeable = false;
728 |         break;
729 |       }
730 | 
731 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
732 |         visitFunction(t, n);
733 |         break;
734 | 
735 |       // These nodes have no interesting type behavior.
736 |       case Token.LABEL:
737 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
738 |       case Token.SWITCH:
739 |       case Token.BREAK:
740 |       case Token.CATCH:
741 |       case Token.TRY:
742 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
743 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
744 |       case Token.BLOCK:
745 |       case Token.EMPTY:
746 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
747 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
748 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
749 |       case Token.THROW:
750 |         typeable = false;
751 |         break;
752 | 
753 |       // These nodes require data flow analysis.
754 |       case Token.DO:
755 |       case Token.FOR:
756 |       case Token.IF:
757 |       case Token.WHILE:
758 |         typeable = false;
759 |         break;
760 | 
761 |       // These nodes are typed during the type inference.
762 |       case Token.AND:
763 |       case Token.HOOK:
764 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
765 |       case Token.OR:
766 |         if (n.getJSType() != null) { // If we didn't run type inference.
767 |           ensureTyped(t, n);
768 |         } else {
769 |           // If this is an enum, then give that type to the objectlit as well.
770 |           if ((n.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT)
771 |               && (parent.getJSType() instanceof EnumType)) {
772 |             ensureTyped(t, n, parent.getJSType());
773 |           } else {
774 |             ensureTyped(t, n);
775 |           }
776 |         }
777 |         break;
778 | 
779 |       default:
780 |         report(t, n, UNEXPECTED_TOKEN, Token.name(n.getType()));
781 |         ensureTyped(t, n);
782 |         break;
783 |     }
784 | 
785 |     // Don't count externs since the user's code may not even use that part.
786 |     typeable = typeable && !inExterns;
787 | 
788 |     if (typeable) {
789 |       doPercentTypedAccounting(t, n);
790 |     }
791 | 
792 |     checkNoTypeCheckSection(n, false);
793 |   }

/**
   * @return Whether the predicate is true for the node or any of its children.
   */
Method code:
2193 |   static boolean has(Node node,
2194 |                      Predicate<Node> pred,
2195 |                      Predicate<Node> traverseChildrenPred) {
2196 |     if (pred.apply(node)) {
2197 |       return true;
2198 |     }
2199 | 
2200 |     if (!traverseChildrenPred.apply(node)) {
2201 |       return false;
2202 |     }
2203 | 
2204 |     for (Node c = node.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
2205 |       if (has(c, pred, traverseChildrenPred)) {
2206 |         return true;
2207 |       }
2208 |     }
2209 | 
2210 |     return false;
2211 |   }

/**

===== M0544 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1456-1491
Documentation:
/**
   * ParamTypeExpressionAnnotation :=
   *     '{' OptionalParameterType '}' |
   *     '{' TopLevelTypeExpression '}' |
   *     '{' '...' TopLevelTypeExpression '}'
   *
   * OptionalParameterType :=
   *     TopLevelTypeExpression '='
   */
Method code:
1456 |   private Node parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(JsDocToken token) {
1457 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(token == JsDocToken.LC);
1458 | 
1459 |     skipEOLs();
1460 | 
1461 |     boolean restArg = false;
1462 |     token = next();
1463 |     if (token == JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS) {
1464 |       token = next();
1465 |       if (token == JsDocToken.RC) {
1466 |         // EMPTY represents the UNKNOWN type in the Type AST.
1467 |         return wrapNode(Token.ELLIPSIS, new Node(Token.EMPTY));
1468 |       }
1469 |       restArg = true;
1470 |     }
1471 | 
1472 |     Node typeNode = parseTopLevelTypeExpression(token);
1473 |     if (typeNode != null) {
1474 |       skipEOLs();
1475 |       if (restArg) {
1476 |         typeNode = wrapNode(Token.ELLIPSIS, typeNode);
1477 |       } else if (match(JsDocToken.EQUALS)) {
1478 |         next();
1479 |         skipEOLs();
1480 |         typeNode = wrapNode(Token.EQUALS, typeNode);
1481 |       }
1482 | 
1483 |       if (!match(JsDocToken.RC)) {
1484 |         reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc");
1485 |       } else {
1486 |         next();
1487 |       }
1488 |     }
1489 | 
1490 |     return typeNode;
1491 |   }

===== M0547 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 813-840
Documentation:
/**
   * Connects cfgNode to the proper CATCH block if target subtree might throw
   * an exception. If there are FINALLY blocks reached before a CATCH, it will
   * make the corresponding entry in finallyMap.
   */
Method code:
813 |   private void connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node cfgNode, Node target) {
814 |     if (mayThrowException(target) && !exceptionHandler.isEmpty()) {
815 |       Node lastJump = cfgNode;
816 |       for (Node handler : exceptionHandler) {
817 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunction(handler)) {
818 |           return;
819 |         }
820 |         Preconditions.checkState(handler.getType() == Token.TRY);
821 |         Node catchBlock = NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(handler);
822 | 
823 |         if (!NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(catchBlock)) { // No catch but a FINALLY.
824 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
825 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, handler.getLastChild());
826 |           } else {
827 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, handler.getLastChild());
828 |           }
829 |         } else { // Has a catch.
830 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
831 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, catchBlock);
832 |             return;
833 |           } else {
834 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, catchBlock);
835 |           }
836 |         }
837 |         lastJump = handler;
838 |       }
839 |     }
840 |   }

===== M0550 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 95-150
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
95 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
96 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
97 |     // just bail out early here.
98 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
99 |       return this;
100 |     }
101 | 
102 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
103 | 
104 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
105 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
106 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
107 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
108 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
109 |     }
110 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
111 |       if (alternate instanceof UnionType) {
112 |         UnionType union = (UnionType) alternate;
113 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
114 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
115 |         }
116 |       } else {
117 |         if (alternates.size() > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
118 |           return this;
119 |         }
120 | 
121 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
122 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
123 |         // one another.
124 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
125 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
126 |           JSType current = it.next();
127 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
128 |               current.isUnknownType()) {
129 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
130 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
131 |               return this;
132 |             }
133 |           } else {
134 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
135 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
136 |               return this;
137 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
138 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
139 |               it.remove();
140 |             }
141 |           }
142 |         }
143 |         alternates.add(alternate);
144 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
145 |       }
146 |     } else {
147 |       result = null;
148 |     }
149 |     return this;
150 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferThisType(JSDocInfo,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 409-438
Documentation:
/**
   * Infers the type of {@code this}.
   * @param info The JSDocInfo for this function.
   * @param owner The node for the object whose prototype "owns" this function.
   *     For example, {@code A} in the expression {@code A.prototype.foo}. May
   *     be null to indicate that this is not a prototype property.
   */
Method code:
409 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferThisType(JSDocInfo info,
410 |       @Nullable Node owner) {
411 |     ObjectType maybeThisType = null;
412 |     if (info != null && info.hasThisType()) {
413 |       maybeThisType = ObjectType.cast(
414 |           info.getThisType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry));
415 |     }
416 |     if (maybeThisType != null) {
417 |       thisType = maybeThisType;
418 |       thisType.setValidator(new ThisTypeValidator());
419 |     } else if (owner != null &&
420 |                (info == null || !info.hasType())) {
421 |       // If the function is of the form:
422 |       // x.prototype.y = function() {}
423 |       // then we can assume "x" is the @this type. On the other hand,
424 |       // if it's of the form:
425 |       // /** @type {Function} */ x.prototype.y;
426 |       // then we should not give it a @this type.
427 |       String ownerTypeName = owner.getQualifiedName();
428 |       ObjectType ownerType = ObjectType.cast(
429 |           typeRegistry.getForgivingType(
430 |               scope, ownerTypeName, sourceName,
431 |               owner.getLineno(), owner.getCharno()));
432 |       if (ownerType != null) {
433 |         thisType = ownerType;
434 |       }
435 |     }
436 | 
437 |     return this;
438 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.Tracer(String,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 250-293
Documentation:
/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totalling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
250 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
251 |     this.type = type;
252 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
253 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
254 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
255 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
256 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
257 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
258 |       int i = 0;
259 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
260 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
261 |         i++;
262 |       }
263 |     }
264 | 
265 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
266 | 
267 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
268 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
269 |       return;
270 |     }
271 | 
272 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
273 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
274 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
275 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
276 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
277 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
278 |       trace.truncateEvents();
279 |     }
280 | 
281 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
282 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
283 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
284 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
285 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
286 |                     + "try/finally block. "
287 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
288 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
289 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
290 |     }
291 | 
292 |     trace.startEvent(this);
293 |   }

===== M0584 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PureFunctionIdentifier.SideEffectPropagationCallback.traverseEdge(FunctionInformation,Node,FunctionInformation)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PureFunctionIdentifier.java
Original range: 777-835
Documentation:

Method code:
777 |     public boolean traverseEdge(FunctionInformation callee,
778 |                                 Node callSite,
779 |                                 FunctionInformation caller) {
780 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(callSite.getType() == Token.CALL ||
781 |                                   callSite.getType() == Token.NEW);
782 | 
783 |       boolean changed = false;
784 |       if (!caller.mutatesGlobalState() && callee.mutatesGlobalState()) {
785 |         caller.setTaintsGlobalState();
786 |         changed = true;
787 |       }
788 | 
789 |       if (!caller.functionThrows() && callee.functionThrows()) {
790 |         caller.setFunctionThrows();
791 |         changed = true;
792 |       }
793 | 
794 |       if (callee.mutatesThis()) {
795 |         // Side effects only propagate via regular calls.
796 |         // Calling a constructor that modifies "this" has no side effects.
797 |         if (callSite.getType() != Token.NEW) {
798 |           Node objectNode = getCallThisObject(callSite);
799 |           if (objectNode != null && NodeUtil.isName(objectNode)
800 |               && !isCallOrApply(callSite)) {
801 |             // Exclude ".call" and ".apply" as the value may still be may be
802 |             // null or undefined. We don't need to worry about this with a
803 |             // direct method call because null and undefined don't have any
804 |             // properties.
805 |             String name = objectNode.getString();
806 | 
807 |             // TODO(nicksantos): Turn this back on when locals-tracking
808 |             // is fixed. See testLocalizedSideEffects11.
809 |             //if (!caller.knownLocals.contains(name)) {
810 |               if (!caller.mutatesGlobalState()) {
811 |                 caller.setTaintsGlobalState();
812 |                 changed = true;
813 |               }
814 |             //}
815 |           } else if (objectNode != null && NodeUtil.isThis(objectNode)) {
816 |             if (!caller.mutatesThis()) {
817 |               caller.setTaintsThis();
818 |               changed = true;
819 |             }
820 |           } else if (objectNode != null
821 |               && NodeUtil.evaluatesToLocalValue(objectNode)
822 |               && !isCallOrApply(callSite)) {
823 |             // Modifying 'this' on a known local object doesn't change any
824 |             // significant state.
825 |             // TODO(johnlenz): We can improve this by including literal values
826 |             // that we know for sure are not null.
827 |           } else if (!caller.mutatesGlobalState()) {
828 |             caller.setTaintsGlobalState();
829 |             changed = true;
830 |           }
831 |         }
832 |       }
833 | 
834 |       return changed;
835 |     }

===== M0605 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.findOverriddenFunction(ObjectType,String)

Original range: 838-858
Documentation:
/**
     * Find the function that's being overridden on this type, if any.
     */
Method code:
838 |     private FunctionType findOverriddenFunction(
839 |         ObjectType ownerType, String propName) {
840 |       // First, check to see if the property is implemented
841 |       // on a superclass.
842 |       JSType propType = ownerType.getPropertyType(propName);
843 |       if (propType instanceof FunctionType) {
844 |         return (FunctionType) propType;
845 |       } else {
846 |         // If it's not, then check to see if it's implemented
847 |         // on an implemented interface.
848 |         for (ObjectType iface :
849 |                  ownerType.getCtorImplementedInterfaces()) {
850 |           propType = iface.getPropertyType(propName);
851 |           if (propType instanceof FunctionType) {
852 |             return (FunctionType) propType;
853 |           }
854 |         }
855 |       }
856 | 
857 |       return null;
858 |     }

===== M0610 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitParameterList(NodeTraversal,Node,FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1398-1432
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits the parameters of a CALL or a NEW node.
   */
Method code:
1398 |   private void visitParameterList(NodeTraversal t, Node call,
1399 |       FunctionType functionType) {
1400 |     Iterator<Node> arguments = call.children().iterator();
1401 |     arguments.next(); // skip the function name
1402 | 
1403 |     Iterator<Node> parameters = functionType.getParameters().iterator();
1404 |     int ordinal = 0;
1405 |     Node parameter = null;
1406 |     Node argument = null;
1407 |     while (arguments.hasNext() &&
1408 |            (parameters.hasNext() ||
1409 |             parameter != null && parameter.isVarArgs())) {
1410 |       // If there are no parameters left in the list, then the while loop
1411 |       // above implies that this must be a var_args function.
1412 |       if (parameters.hasNext()) {
1413 |         parameter = parameters.next();
1414 |       }
1415 |       argument = arguments.next();
1416 |       ordinal++;
1417 | 
1418 |       validator.expectArgumentMatchesParameter(t, argument,
1419 |           getJSType(argument), getJSType(parameter), call, ordinal);
1420 |     }
1421 | 
1422 |     int numArgs = call.getChildCount() - 1;
1423 |     int minArgs = functionType.getMinArguments();
1424 |     int maxArgs = functionType.getMaxArguments();
1425 |     if (minArgs > numArgs || maxArgs < numArgs) {
1426 |       report(t, call, WRONG_ARGUMENT_COUNT,
1427 |               validator.getReadableJSTypeName(call.getFirstChild(), false),
1428 |               String.valueOf(numArgs), String.valueOf(minArgs),
1429 |               maxArgs != Integer.MAX_VALUE ?
1430 |               " and no more than " + maxArgs + " argument(s)" : "");
1431 |     }
1432 |   }

===== M0612 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.checkDeclaredPropertyInheritance(NodeTraversal,Node,FunctionType,String,JSDocInfo,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 949-1035
Documentation:
/**
   * Given a constructor type and a property name, check that the property has
   * the JSDoc annotation @override iff the property is declared on a
   * superclass. Several checks regarding inheritance correctness are also
   * performed.
   */
Method code:
949 |   private void checkDeclaredPropertyInheritance(
950 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, FunctionType ctorType, String propertyName,
951 |       JSDocInfo info, JSType propertyType) {
952 |     // TODO(user): We're not 100% confident that type-checking works,
953 |     // so we return quietly if the unknown type is a superclass of this type.
954 |     // Remove this check as we become more confident. We should flag a warning
955 |     // when the unknown type is on the inheritance chain, as it is likely
956 |     // because of a programmer error.
957 |     if (ctorType.hasUnknownSupertype()) {
958 |       return;
959 |     }
960 | 
961 |     FunctionType superClass = ctorType.getSuperClassConstructor();
962 |     boolean superClassHasProperty = superClass != null &&
963 |         superClass.getPrototype().hasProperty(propertyName);
964 |     boolean declaredOverride = info != null && info.isOverride();
965 | 
966 |     boolean foundInterfaceProperty = false;
967 |     if (ctorType.isConstructor()) {
968 |       for (JSType implementedInterface : ctorType.getImplementedInterfaces()) {
969 |         if (implementedInterface.isUnknownType()) {
970 |           continue;
971 |         }
972 |         FunctionType interfaceType =
973 |             implementedInterface.toObjectType().getConstructor();
974 |         boolean interfaceHasProperty =
975 |             interfaceType.getPrototype().hasProperty(propertyName);
976 |         foundInterfaceProperty = foundInterfaceProperty || interfaceHasProperty;
977 |         if (reportMissingOverride.isOn() && !declaredOverride &&
978 |             interfaceHasProperty) {
979 |           // @override not present, but the property does override an interface
980 |           // property
981 |           compiler.report(t.makeError(n, reportMissingOverride,
982 |               HIDDEN_INTERFACE_PROPERTY, propertyName,
983 |               interfaceType.getTopMostDefiningType(propertyName).toString()));
984 |         }
985 |         // Check that it is ok
986 |         if (interfaceHasProperty) {
987 |           JSType interfacePropType =
988 |               interfaceType.getPrototype().getPropertyType(propertyName);
989 |           if (!propertyType.canAssignTo(interfacePropType)) {
990 |             compiler.report(t.makeError(n,
991 |                 HIDDEN_INTERFACE_PROPERTY_MISMATCH, propertyName,
992 |                 interfaceType.getTopMostDefiningType(propertyName).toString(),
993 |                 interfacePropType.toString(), propertyType.toString()));
994 |           }
995 |         }
996 |       }
997 |     }
998 | 
999 |     if (!declaredOverride && !superClassHasProperty) {
1000 |       // nothing to do here, it's just a plain new property
1001 |       return;
1002 |     }
1003 | 
1004 |     JSType topInstanceType = superClassHasProperty ?
1005 |         superClass.getTopMostDefiningType(propertyName) : null;
1006 |     if (reportMissingOverride.isOn() && ctorType.isConstructor() &&
1007 |         !declaredOverride && superClassHasProperty) {
1008 |       // @override not present, but the property does override a superclass
1009 |       // property
1010 |       compiler.report(t.makeError(n, reportMissingOverride,
1011 |           HIDDEN_SUPERCLASS_PROPERTY, propertyName,
1012 |           topInstanceType.toString()));
1013 |     }
1014 |     if (!declaredOverride) {
1015 |       // there's no @override to check
1016 |       return;
1017 |     }
1018 |     // @override is present and we have to check that it is ok
1019 |     if (superClassHasProperty) {
1020 |       // there is a superclass implementation
1021 |       JSType superClassPropType =
1022 |           superClass.getPrototype().getPropertyType(propertyName);
1023 |       if (!propertyType.canAssignTo(superClassPropType)) {
1024 |         compiler.report(
1025 |             t.makeError(n, HIDDEN_SUPERCLASS_PROPERTY_MISMATCH,
1026 |                 propertyName, topInstanceType.toString(),
1027 |                 superClassPropType.toString(), propertyType.toString()));
1028 |       }
1029 |     } else if (!foundInterfaceProperty) {
1030 |       // there is no superclass nor interface implementation
1031 |       compiler.report(
1032 |           t.makeError(n, UNKNOWN_OVERRIDE,
1033 |               propertyName, ctorType.getInstanceType().toString()));
1034 |     }
1035 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
598 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
599 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
600 |     if (typeSet == null) {
601 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this);
602 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
603 |     }
604 | 
605 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
606 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
607 | 
608 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
609 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
610 |   }

===== M0632 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferFromOverriddenFunction(FunctionType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 233-284
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the parameter and return types of a function from
   * the parameter and return types of the function it is overriding.
   *
   * @param oldType The function being overridden. Does nothing if this is null.
   * @param paramsParent The LP node of the function that we're assigning to.
   *     If null, that just means we're not initializing this to a function
   *     literal.
   */
Method code:
233 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferFromOverriddenFunction(
234 |       @Nullable FunctionType oldType, @Nullable Node paramsParent) {
235 |     if (oldType == null) {
236 |       return this;
237 |     }
238 | 
239 |     returnType = oldType.getReturnType();
240 |     returnTypeInferred = oldType.isReturnTypeInferred();
241 |     if (paramsParent == null) {
242 |       // Not a function literal.
243 |       parametersNode = oldType.getParametersNode();
244 |       if (parametersNode == null) {
245 |         parametersNode = new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry).build();
246 |       }
247 |     } else {
248 |       // We're overriding with a function literal. Apply type information
249 |       // to each parameter of the literal.
250 |       FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder =
251 |           new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry);
252 |       Iterator<Node> oldParams = oldType.getParameters().iterator();
253 |       boolean warnedAboutArgList = false;
254 |       boolean oldParamsListHitOptArgs = false;
255 |       for (Node currentParam = paramsParent.getFirstChild();
256 |            currentParam != null; currentParam = currentParam.getNext()) {
257 |         if (oldParams.hasNext()) {
258 |           Node oldParam = oldParams.next();
259 |           Node newParam = paramBuilder.newParameterFromNode(oldParam);
260 | 
261 |           oldParamsListHitOptArgs = oldParamsListHitOptArgs ||
262 |               oldParam.isVarArgs() ||
263 |               oldParam.isOptionalArg();
264 | 
265 |           // The subclass method might right its var_args as individual
266 |           // arguments.
267 |           if (currentParam.getNext() != null && newParam.isVarArgs()) {
268 |             newParam.setVarArgs(false);
269 |             newParam.setOptionalArg(true);
270 |           }
271 |         } else {
272 |           warnedAboutArgList |= addParameter(
273 |               paramBuilder,
274 |               typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE),
275 |               warnedAboutArgList,
276 |               codingConvention.isOptionalParameter(currentParam) ||
277 |                   oldParamsListHitOptArgs,
278 |               codingConvention.isVarArgsParameter(currentParam));
279 |         }
280 |       }
281 |       parametersNode = paramBuilder.build();
282 |     }
283 |     return this;
284 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitName(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1099-1128
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a NAME node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of the node n.
   * @return whether the node is typeable or not
   */
Method code:
1099 |   boolean visitName(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1100 |     // At this stage, we need to determine whether this is a leaf
1101 |     // node in an expression (which therefore needs to have a type
1102 |     // assigned for it) versus some other decorative node that we
1103 |     // can safely ignore.  Function names, arguments (children of LP nodes) and
1104 |     // variable declarations are ignored.
1105 |     // TODO(user): remove this short-circuiting in favor of a
1106 |     // pre order traversal of the FUNCTION, CATCH, LP and VAR nodes.
1107 |     int parentNodeType = parent.getType();
1108 |     if (parentNodeType == Token.FUNCTION ||
1109 |         parentNodeType == Token.CATCH ||
1110 |         parentNodeType == Token.LP ||
1111 |         parentNodeType == Token.VAR) {
1112 |       return false;
1113 |     }
1114 | 
1115 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
1116 |     if (type == null) {
1117 |       type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1118 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
1119 |       if (var != null) {
1120 |         JSType varType = var.getType();
1121 |         if (varType != null) {
1122 |           type = varType;
1123 |         }
1124 |       }
1125 |     }
1126 |     ensureTyped(t, n, type);
1127 |     return true;
1128 |   }

===== M0644 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.branchedFlowThrough(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 192-304
Documentation:

Method code:
192 |   @Override
193 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
194 |   List<FlowScope> branchedFlowThrough(Node source, FlowScope input) {
195 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): Right now, we just treat ON_EX edges like UNCOND
196 |     // edges. If we wanted to be perfect, we'd actually JOIN all the out
197 |     // lattices of this flow with the in lattice, and then make that the out
198 |     // lattice for the ON_EX edge. But it's probably to expensive to be
199 |     // worthwhile.
200 |     FlowScope output = flowThrough(source, input);
201 |     Node condition = null;
202 |     FlowScope conditionFlowScope = null;
203 |     BooleanOutcomePair conditionOutcomes = null;
204 | 
205 |     List<DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch>> branchEdges = getCfg().getOutEdges(source);
206 |     List<FlowScope> result = Lists.newArrayListWithCapacity(branchEdges.size());
207 |     for (DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch> branchEdge : branchEdges) {
208 |       Branch branch = branchEdge.getValue();
209 |       FlowScope newScope = output;
210 | 
211 |       switch (branch) {
212 |         case ON_TRUE:
213 |           if (NodeUtil.isForIn(source)) {
214 |             // item is assigned a property name, so its type should be string.
215 |             Node item = source.getFirstChild();
216 |             Node obj = item.getNext();
217 | 
218 |             FlowScope informed = traverse(obj, output.createChildFlowScope());
219 | 
220 |             if (item.getType() == Token.VAR) {
221 |               item = item.getFirstChild();
222 |             }
223 |             if (item.getType() == Token.NAME) {
224 |               JSType iterKeyType = getNativeType(STRING_TYPE);
225 |               ObjectType objType = getJSType(obj).dereference();
226 |               JSType objIndexType = objType == null ?
227 |                   null : objType.getIndexType();
228 |               if (objIndexType != null && !objIndexType.isUnknownType()) {
229 |                 JSType narrowedKeyType =
230 |                     iterKeyType.getGreatestSubtype(objIndexType);
231 |                 if (!narrowedKeyType.isEmptyType()) {
232 |                   iterKeyType = narrowedKeyType;
233 |                 }
234 |               }
235 |               redeclare(informed, item.getString(), iterKeyType);
236 |             }
237 |             newScope = informed;
238 |             break;
239 |           }
240 | 
241 |           // FALL THROUGH
242 | 
243 |         case ON_FALSE:
244 |           if (condition == null) {
245 |             condition = NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(source);
246 |             if (condition == null && source.getType() == Token.CASE) {
247 |               condition = source;
248 | 
249 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
250 |               // of the loop.
251 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
252 |                 conditionFlowScope = traverse(
253 |                     condition.getFirstChild(), output.createChildFlowScope());
254 |               }
255 |             }
256 |           }
257 | 
258 |           if (condition != null) {
259 |             if (condition.getType() == Token.AND ||
260 |                 condition.getType() == Token.OR) {
261 |               // When handling the short-circuiting binary operators,
262 |               // the outcome scope on true can be different than the outcome
263 |               // scope on false.
264 |               //
265 |               // TODO(nicksantos): The "right" way to do this is to
266 |               // carry the known outcome all the way through the
267 |               // recursive traversal, so that we can construct a
268 |               // different flow scope based on the outcome. However,
269 |               // this would require a bunch of code and a bunch of
270 |               // extra computation for an edge case. This seems to be
271 |               // a "good enough" approximation.
272 | 
273 |               // conditionOutcomes is cached from previous iterations
274 |               // of the loop.
275 |               if (conditionOutcomes == null) {
276 |                 conditionOutcomes = condition.getType() == Token.AND ?
277 |                     traverseAnd(condition, output.createChildFlowScope()) :
278 |                     traverseOr(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
279 |               }
280 |               newScope =
281 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
282 |                       condition,
283 |                       conditionOutcomes.getOutcomeFlowScope(
284 |                           condition.getType(), branch == Branch.ON_TRUE),
285 |                       branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
286 |             } else {
287 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
288 |               // of the loop.
289 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
290 |                 conditionFlowScope =
291 |                     traverse(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
292 |               }
293 |               newScope =
294 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
295 |                       condition, conditionFlowScope, branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
296 |             }
297 |           }
298 |           break;
299 |       }
300 | 
301 |       result.add(newScope.optimize());
302 |     }
303 |     return result;
304 |   }

===== M0663 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.defineSlot(Node,Node,JSType,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 941-1044
Documentation:
/**
     * Defines a typed variable. The defining node will be annotated with the
     * variable's type of {@link JSTypeNative#UNKNOWN_TYPE} if its type is
     * inferred.
     *
     * Slots may be any variable or any qualified name in the global scope.
     *
     * @param n the defining NAME or GETPROP node.
     * @param parent the {@code n}'s parent.
     * @param type the variable's type. It may be {@code null} if
     *     {@code inferred} is {@code true}.
     */
Method code:
941 |     void defineSlot(Node n, Node parent, JSType type, boolean inferred) {
942 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(inferred || type != null);
943 | 
944 |       // Only allow declarations of NAMEs and qualfied names.
945 |       boolean shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis = false;
946 |       if (n.getType() == Token.NAME) {
947 |         Preconditions.checkArgument(
948 |             parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION ||
949 |             parent.getType() == Token.VAR ||
950 |             parent.getType() == Token.LP ||
951 |             parent.getType() == Token.CATCH);
952 |         shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis = scope.isGlobal() &&
953 |             (parent.getType() == Token.VAR ||
954 |              parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
955 |       } else {
956 |         Preconditions.checkArgument(
957 |             n.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
958 |             (parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN ||
959 |              parent.getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT));
960 |       }
961 |       String variableName = n.getQualifiedName();
962 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(!variableName.isEmpty());
963 | 
964 |       // If n is a property, then we should really declare it in the
965 |       // scope where the root object appears. This helps out people
966 |       // who declare "global" names in an anonymous namespace.
967 |       Scope scopeToDeclareIn = scope;
968 |       if (n.getType() == Token.GETPROP && !scope.isGlobal() &&
969 |           isQnameRootedInGlobalScope(n)) {
970 |         Scope globalScope = scope.getGlobalScope();
971 | 
972 |         // don't try to declare in the global scope if there's
973 |         // already a symbol there with this name.
974 |         if (!globalScope.isDeclared(variableName, false)) {
975 |           scopeToDeclareIn = scope.getGlobalScope();
976 |         }
977 |       }
978 | 
979 |       // declared in closest scope?
980 |       if (scopeToDeclareIn.isDeclared(variableName, false)) {
981 |         Var oldVar = scopeToDeclareIn.getVar(variableName);
982 |         validator.expectUndeclaredVariable(
983 |             sourceName, n, parent, oldVar, variableName, type);
984 |       } else {
985 |         if (!inferred) {
986 |           setDeferredType(n, type);
987 |         }
988 |         CompilerInput input = compiler.getInput(sourceName);
989 |         boolean isExtern = input.isExtern();
990 |         Var newVar =
991 |             scopeToDeclareIn.declare(variableName, n, type, input, inferred);
992 | 
993 |         if (shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis) {
994 |           ObjectType globalThis =
995 |               typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(JSTypeNative.GLOBAL_THIS);
996 |           if (inferred) {
997 |             globalThis.defineInferredProperty(variableName,
998 |                 type == null ?
999 |                     getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_TYPE) :
1000 |                     type,
1001 |                 isExtern);
1002 |           } else {
1003 |             globalThis.defineDeclaredProperty(variableName, type, isExtern);
1004 |           }
1005 |         }
1006 | 
1007 |         // We need to do some additional work for constructors and interfaces.
1008 |         if (type instanceof FunctionType &&
1009 |             // We don't want to look at empty function types.
1010 |             !type.isEmptyType()) {
1011 |           FunctionType fnType = (FunctionType) type;
1012 |           if ((fnType.isConstructor() || fnType.isInterface()) &&
1013 |               !fnType.equals(getNativeType(U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE))) {
1014 |             // Declare var.prototype in the scope chain.
1015 |             FunctionType superClassCtor = fnType.getSuperClassConstructor();
1016 |             scopeToDeclareIn.declare(variableName + ".prototype", n,
1017 |                 fnType.getPrototype(), input,
1018 |                 /* declared iff there's an explicit supertype */
1019 |                 superClassCtor == null ||
1020 |                 superClassCtor.getInstanceType().equals(
1021 |                     getNativeType(OBJECT_TYPE)));
1022 | 
1023 |             // Make sure the variable is initialized to something if
1024 |             // it constructs itself.
1025 |             if (newVar.getInitialValue() == null &&
1026 |                 !isExtern &&
1027 |                 // We want to make sure that when we declare a new instance
1028 |                 // type (with @constructor) that there's actually a ctor for it.
1029 |                 // This doesn't apply to structural constructors
1030 |                 // (like function(new:Array). Checking the constructed
1031 |                 // type against the variable name is a sufficient check for
1032 |                 // this.
1033 |                 variableName.equals(
1034 |                     fnType.getInstanceType().getReferenceName())) {
1035 |               compiler.report(
1036 |                   JSError.make(sourceName, n,
1037 |                       fnType.isConstructor() ?
1038 |                           CTOR_INITIALIZER : IFACE_INITIALIZER,
1039 |                       variableName));
1040 |             }
1041 |           }
1042 |         }
1043 |       }
1044 |     }

===== M0664 =====

/**
   * Expect that the type of an argument matches the type of the parameter
   * that it's fulfilling.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal.

   * @param callNode The call node, to help with the warning message.
   * @param ordinal The argument ordinal, to help with the warning message.
   */
Method code:
358 |   void expectArgumentMatchesParameter(NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType argType,

===== M0666 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LinkedFlowScope.equals(Object)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LinkedFlowScope.java
Original range: 252-302
Documentation:

Method code:
252 |   @Override
253 |   public boolean equals(Object other) {
254 |     if (other instanceof LinkedFlowScope) {
255 |       LinkedFlowScope that = (LinkedFlowScope) other;
256 |       if (this.optimize() == that.optimize()) {
257 |         return true;
258 |       }
259 | 
260 |       // If two flow scopes are in the same function, then they could have
261 |       // two possible function scopes: the real one and the BOTTOM scope.
262 |       // If they have different function scopes, we *should* iterate thru all
263 |       // the variables in each scope and compare. However, 99.9% of the time,
264 |       // they're not equal. And the other .1% of the time, we can pretend
265 |       // they're equal--this just means that data flow analysis will have
266 |       // to propagate the entry lattice a little bit further than it
267 |       // really needs to. Everything will still come out ok.
268 |       if (this.getFunctionScope() != that.getFunctionScope()) {
269 |         return false;
270 |       }
271 | 
272 |       if (cache == that.cache) {
273 |         // If the two flow scopes have the same cache, then we can check
274 |         // equality a lot faster: by just looking at the "dirty" elements
275 |         // in the cache, and comparing them in both scopes.
276 |         for (String name : cache.dirtySymbols) {
277 |           if (diffSlots(getSlot(name), that.getSlot(name))) {
278 |             return false;
279 |           }
280 |         }
281 | 
282 |         return true;
283 |       }
284 | 
285 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> myFlowSlots = allFlowSlots();
286 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> otherFlowSlots = that.allFlowSlots();
287 | 
288 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : myFlowSlots.values()) {
289 |         if (diffSlots(slot, otherFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
290 |           return false;
291 |         }
292 |         otherFlowSlots.remove(slot.getName());
293 |       }
294 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : otherFlowSlots.values()) {
295 |         if (diffSlots(slot, myFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
296 |           return false;
297 |         }
298 |       }
299 |       return true;
300 |     }
301 |     return false;
302 |   }

===== M0674 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferInheritance(JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 344-388
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the role of the function (whether it's a constructor or interface)
   * and what it inherits from in JSDocInfo.
   */
Method code:
344 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferInheritance(@Nullable JSDocInfo info) {
345 |     if (info != null) {
346 |       isConstructor = info.isConstructor();
347 |       isInterface = info.isInterface();
348 | 
349 |       // base type
350 |       if (info.hasBaseType()) {
351 |         if (isConstructor || isInterface) {
352 |           JSType maybeBaseType =
353 |               info.getBaseType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
354 |           if (maybeBaseType != null &&
355 |               maybeBaseType.setValidator(new ExtendedTypeValidator())) {
356 |             baseType = (ObjectType) maybeBaseType;
357 |           }
358 |         } else {
359 |           reportWarning(EXTENDS_WITHOUT_TYPEDEF, fnName);
360 |         }
361 |       }
362 | 
363 |       // implemented interfaces
364 |       if (isConstructor || isInterface) {
365 |         implementedInterfaces = Lists.newArrayList();
366 |         for (JSTypeExpression t : info.getImplementedInterfaces()) {
367 |           JSType maybeInterType = t.evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
368 |           if (maybeInterType != null &&
369 |               maybeInterType.setValidator(new ImplementedTypeValidator())) {
370 |             implementedInterfaces.add((ObjectType) maybeInterType);
371 |           }
372 |         }
373 |         if (baseType != null) {
374 |           JSType maybeFunctionType = baseType.getConstructor();
375 |           if (maybeFunctionType instanceof FunctionType) {
376 |             FunctionType functionType = baseType.getConstructor();
377 |             Iterables.addAll(
378 |                 implementedInterfaces,
379 |                 functionType.getImplementedInterfaces());
380 |           }
381 |         }
382 |       } else if (info.getImplementedInterfaceCount() > 0) {
383 |         reportWarning(IMPLEMENTS_WITHOUT_CONSTRUCTOR, fnName);
384 |       }
385 |     }
386 | 
387 |     return this;
388 |   }

===== M0677 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 755-797
Documentation:
/**
   * A function is a subtype of another if their call methods are related via
   * subtyping and {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that} with regard to
   * the prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
755 |   @Override
756 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
757 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
758 |       return true;
759 |     }
760 | 
761 |     if (that.isFunctionType()) {
762 |       if (((FunctionType) that).isInterface()) {
763 |         // Any function can be assigned to an interface function.
764 |         return true;
765 |       }
766 |       if (this.isInterface()) {
767 |         // An interface function cannot be assigned to anything.
768 |         return false;
769 |       }
770 |       // If functionA is a subtype of functionB, then their "this" types
771 |       // should be contravariant. However, this causes problems because
772 |       // of the way we enforce overrides. Because function(this:SubFoo)
773 |       // is not a subtype of function(this:Foo), our override check treats
774 |       // this as an error. It also screws up out standard method
775 |       // for aliasing constructors. Let's punt on all this for now.
776 |       // TODO(nicksantos): fix this.
777 |       FunctionType other = (FunctionType) that;
778 |       boolean treatThisTypesAsCovariant =
779 |         // If either one of these is a ctor, skip 'this' checking.
780 |         this.isConstructor() || other.isConstructor() ||
781 | 
782 |         // An interface 'this'-type is non-restrictive.
783 |         // In practical terms, if C implements I, and I has a method m,
784 |         // then any m doesn't necessarily have to C#m's 'this'
785 |         // type doesn't need to match I.
786 |         (other.typeOfThis.getConstructor() != null &&
787 |              other.typeOfThis.getConstructor().isInterface()) ||
788 | 
789 |         // If one of the 'this' types is covariant of the other,
790 |         // then we'll treat them as covariant (see comment above).
791 |         other.typeOfThis.isSubtype(this.typeOfThis) ||
792 |         this.typeOfThis.isSubtype(other.typeOfThis);
793 |       return treatThisTypesAsCovariant && this.call.isSubtype(other.call);
794 |     }
795 | 
796 |     return getNativeType(JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_PROTOTYPE).isSubtype(that);
797 |   }

===== M0683 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getStringValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 149-184
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the value of a node as a String, or null if it cannot be converted.
   * When it returns a non-null String, this method effectively emulates the
   * <code>String()</code> JavaScript cast function.
   */
Method code:
149 |   static String getStringValue(Node n) {
150 |     // TODO(user): Convert constant array, object, and regex literals as well.
151 |     switch (n.getType()) {
152 |       case Token.STRING:
153 |         return n.getString();
154 | 
155 |       case Token.NAME:
156 |         String name = n.getString();
157 |         if ("undefined".equals(name)
158 |             || "Infinity".equals(name)
159 |             || "NaN".equals(name)) {
160 |           return name;
161 |         }
162 |         break;
163 | 
164 |       case Token.NUMBER:
165 |         double value = n.getDouble();
166 |         long longValue = (long) value;
167 | 
168 |         // Return "1" instead of "1.0"
169 |         if (longValue == value) {
170 |           return Long.toString(longValue);
171 |         } else {
172 |           return Double.toString(n.getDouble());
173 |         }
174 | 
175 |       case Token.FALSE:
176 |       case Token.TRUE:
177 |       case Token.NULL:
178 |         return Node.tokenToName(n.getType());
179 | 
180 |       case Token.VOID:
181 |         return "undefined";
182 |     }
183 |     return null;
184 |   }

===== M0684 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addOp(String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 203-231
Documentation:

Method code:
203 |   void addOp(String op, boolean binOp) {
204 |     maybeEndStatement();
205 | 
206 |     char first = op.charAt(0);
207 |     char prev = getLastChar();
208 | 
209 |     if ((first == '+' || first == '-') && prev == first) {
210 |       // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of
211 |       // things like "x + ++y" or "x++ + ++y"
212 |       append(" ");
213 |     } else if (Character.isLetter(first) &&
214 |                isWordChar(prev)) {
215 |       // Make sure there is a space after e.g. instanceof , typeof
216 |       append(" ");
217 |     } else if (prev == '-' && first == '>') {
218 |       // Make sure that we don't emit -->
219 |       append(" ");
220 |     }
221 | 
222 |     // Allow formating around the operator.
223 |     appendOp(op, binOp);
224 | 
225 |     // Line breaking after an operator is always safe. Line breaking before an
226 |     // operator on the other hand is not. We only line break after a bin op
227 |     // because it looks strange.
228 |     if (binOp) {
229 |       maybeCutLine();
230 |     }
231 |   }

===== M0692 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.defineName(Node,Node,Node,JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 708-742
Documentation:
/**
     * Defines a variable based on the {@link Token#NAME} node passed.
     * @param name The {@link Token#NAME} node.
     * @param var The parent of the {@code name} node, which must be a
     *     {@link Token#VAR} node.
     * @param parent {@code var}'s parent.
     * @param info the {@link JSDocInfo} information relating to this
     *     {@code name} node.
     */
Method code:
708 |     private void defineName(Node name, Node var, Node parent, JSDocInfo info) {
709 |       Node value = name.getFirstChild();
710 | 
711 |       if (value != null && value.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
712 |         // function
713 |         String functionName = name.getString();
714 |         FunctionType functionType =
715 |             getFunctionType(functionName, value, info, null);
716 |         if (functionType.isReturnTypeInferred() &&
717 |             scope.isLocal()) {
718 |           defineSlot(name, var, null);
719 |         } else {
720 |           defineSlot(name, var, functionType);
721 |         }
722 |       } else {
723 |         // variable's type
724 |         JSType type = null;
725 |         if (info == null) {
726 |           // the variable's type will be inferred
727 |           CompilerInput input = compiler.getInput(sourceName);
728 |           Preconditions.checkNotNull(input, sourceName);
729 |           type = input.isExtern() ?
730 |               getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : null;
731 |         } else if (info.hasEnumParameterType()) {
732 |           type = getEnumType(name.getString(), var, value,
733 |               info.getEnumParameterType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry));
734 |         } else if (info.isConstructor()) {
735 |           type = getFunctionType(name.getString(), value, info, name);
736 |         } else {
737 |           type = getDeclaredTypeInAnnotation(sourceName, name, info);
738 |         }
739 | 
740 |         defineSlot(name, var, type);
741 |       }
742 |     }

===== M0696 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleStmtList(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 482-521
Documentation:

Method code:
482 |   private void handleStmtList(Node node) {
483 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
484 |     // Special case, don't add a block of empty CATCH block to the graph.
485 |     if (node.getType() == Token.BLOCK && parent != null &&
486 |         parent.getType() == Token.TRY &&
487 |         NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node &&
488 |         !NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(node)) {
489 |       return;
490 |     }
491 | 
492 |     // A block transfer control to its first child if it is not empty.
493 |     Node child = node.getFirstChild();
494 | 
495 |     // Function declarations are skipped since control doesn't go into that
496 |     // function (unless it is called)
497 |     while (child != null && child.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
498 |       child = child.getNext();
499 |     }
500 | 
501 |     if (child != null) {
502 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFallThrough(child));
503 |     } else {
504 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFollowNode(node, this));
505 |     }
506 | 
507 |     // Synthetic blocks
508 |     if (parent != null) {
509 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
510 |         case Token.DEFAULT:
511 |         case Token.CASE:
512 |         case Token.TRY:
513 |           break;
514 |         default:
515 |           if (node.getType() == Token.BLOCK && node.isSyntheticBlock()) {
516 |             createEdge(node, Branch.SYN_BLOCK, computeFollowNode(node, this));
517 |           }
518 |           break;
519 |       }
520 |     }
521 |   }

===== M0707 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.getOrCreateConstructor()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 672-708
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns a constructor function either by returning it from the
   * registry if it exists or creating and registering a new type. If
   * there is already a type, then warn if the existing type is
   * different than the one we are creating, though still return the
   * existing function if possible.  The primary purpose of this is
   * that registering a constructor will fail for all built-in types
   * that are initialized in {@link JSTypeRegistry}.  We a) want to
   * make sure that the type information specified in the externs file
   * matches what is in the registry and b) annotate the externs with
   * the {@link JSType} from the registry so that there are not two
   * separate JSType objects for one type.
   */
Method code:
672 |   private FunctionType getOrCreateConstructor() {
673 |     FunctionType fnType = typeRegistry.createConstructorType(
674 |         fnName, sourceNode, parametersNode, returnType);
675 |     JSType existingType = typeRegistry.getType(fnName);
676 | 
677 |     if (existingType != null) {
678 |       boolean isInstanceObject = existingType instanceof InstanceObjectType;
679 |       if (isInstanceObject || fnName.equals("Function")) {
680 |         FunctionType existingFn =
681 |             isInstanceObject ?
682 |             ((InstanceObjectType) existingType).getConstructor() :
683 |             typeRegistry.getNativeFunctionType(FUNCTION_FUNCTION_TYPE);
684 | 
685 |         if (existingFn.getSource() == null) {
686 |           existingFn.setSource(sourceNode);
687 |         }
688 | 
689 |         if (!existingFn.hasEqualCallType(fnType)) {
690 |           reportWarning(TYPE_REDEFINITION, fnName,
691 |               fnType.toString(), existingFn.toString());
692 |         }
693 | 
694 |         return existingFn;
695 |       } else {
696 |         // We fall through and return the created type, even though it will fail
697 |         // to register. We have no choice as we have to return a function. We
698 |         // issue an error elsewhere though, so the user should fix it.
699 |       }
700 |     }
701 | 
702 |     maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
703 | 
704 |     if (getScopeDeclaredIn().isGlobal() && !fnName.isEmpty()) {
705 |       typeRegistry.declareType(fnName, fnType.getInstanceType());
706 |     }
707 |     return fnType;
708 |   }

===== M0713 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PureFunctionIdentifier.propagateSideEffects()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PureFunctionIdentifier.java
Original range: 252-302
Documentation:
/**
   * Propagate side effect information by building a graph based on
   * call site information stored in FunctionInformation and the
   * DefinitionProvider and then running GraphReachability to
   * determine the set of functions that have side effects.
   */
Method code:
252 |   private void propagateSideEffects() {
253 |     // Nodes are function declarations; Edges are function call sites.
254 |     DiGraph<FunctionInformation, Node> sideEffectGraph =
255 |         LinkedDirectedGraph.createWithoutAnnotations();
256 | 
257 |     // create graph nodes
258 |     for (FunctionInformation functionInfo : functionSideEffectMap.values()) {
259 |       sideEffectGraph.createNode(functionInfo);
260 |     }
261 | 
262 |     // add connections to called functions and side effect root.
263 |     for (FunctionInformation functionInfo : functionSideEffectMap.values()) {
264 |       if (!functionInfo.mayHaveSideEffects()) {
265 |         continue;
266 |       }
267 | 
268 |       for (Node callSite : functionInfo.getCallsInFunctionBody()) {
269 |         Node callee = callSite.getFirstChild();
270 |         Collection<Definition> defs =
271 |             getCallableDefinitions(definitionProvider, callee);
272 |         if (defs == null) {
273 |           // Definition set is not complete or eligible.  Possible
274 |           // causes include:
275 |           //  * "callee" is not of type NAME or GETPROP.
276 |           //  * One or more definitions are not functions.
277 |           //  * One or more definitions are complex.
278 |           //    (e.i. return value of a call that returns a function).
279 |           functionInfo.setTaintsUnknown();
280 |           break;
281 |         }
282 | 
283 |         for (Definition def : defs) {
284 |           Node defValue = def.getRValue();
285 |           FunctionInformation dep = functionSideEffectMap.get(defValue);
286 |           Preconditions.checkNotNull(dep);
287 |           sideEffectGraph.connect(dep, callSite, functionInfo);
288 |         }
289 |       }
290 |     }
291 | 
292 |     // Propagate side effect information to a fixed point.
293 |     FixedPointGraphTraversal.newTraversal(new SideEffectPropagationCallback())
294 |         .computeFixedPoint(sideEffectGraph);
295 | 
296 |     // Mark remaining functions "pure".
297 |     for (FunctionInformation functionInfo : functionSideEffectMap.values()) {
298 |       if (functionInfo.mayBePure()) {
299 |         functionInfo.setIsPure();
300 |       }
301 |     }
302 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 420-446
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
420 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
421 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
422 | 
423 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
424 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
425 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
426 |       return 0;
427 |     }
428 | 
429 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
430 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
431 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than extraTracingStatistics.size() because
432 |       // a new statistic may have been added
433 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
434 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
435 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
436 |       }
437 |     }
438 | 
439 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
440 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
441 |       return 0;
442 |     }
443 | 
444 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
445 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
446 |   }

===== M0732 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.createScope(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 179-222
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a scope with all types declared. Declares newly discovered types
   * and type properties in the type registry.
   */
Method code:
179 |   public Scope createScope(Node root, Scope parent) {
180 |     // Constructing the global scope is very different than constructing
181 |     // inner scopes, because only global scopes can contain named classes that
182 |     // show up in the type registry.
183 |     Scope newScope = null;
184 |     AbstractScopeBuilder scopeBuilder = null;
185 |     if (parent == null) {
186 |       // Find all the classes in the global scope.
187 |       newScope = createInitialScope(root);
188 | 
189 |       GlobalScopeBuilder globalScopeBuilder = new GlobalScopeBuilder(newScope);
190 |       scopeBuilder = globalScopeBuilder;
191 |       NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, scopeBuilder);
192 |     } else {
193 |       newScope = new Scope(parent, root);
194 |       LocalScopeBuilder localScopeBuilder = new LocalScopeBuilder(newScope);
195 |       scopeBuilder = localScopeBuilder;
196 |       localScopeBuilder.build();
197 |     }
198 | 
199 |     scopeBuilder.resolveStubDeclarations();
200 |     scopeBuilder.resolveTypes();
201 | 
202 |     // Gather the properties in each function that we found in the
203 |     // global scope, if that function has a @this type that we can
204 |     // build properties on.
205 |     for (Node functionNode : scopeBuilder.nonExternFunctions) {
206 |       JSType type = functionNode.getJSType();
207 |       if (type != null && type instanceof FunctionType) {
208 |         FunctionType fnType = (FunctionType) type;
209 |         ObjectType fnThisType = fnType.getTypeOfThis();
210 |         if (!fnThisType.isUnknownType()) {
211 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, functionNode.getLastChild(),
212 |               scopeBuilder.new CollectProperties(fnThisType));
213 |         }
214 |       }
215 |     }
216 | 
217 |     if (parent == null) {
218 |       codingConvention.defineDelegateProxyPrototypeProperties(
219 |           typeRegistry, newScope, delegateProxyPrototypes);
220 |     }
221 |     return newScope;
222 |   }
