===== M0742 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowGraph.java
Original range: 150-195
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if n should be represented by a new CFG node in the control
   * flow graph.
   */
Method code:
150 |   public static boolean isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node n) {
151 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
152 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
153 |       case Token.BLOCK:
154 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
155 |       case Token.TRY:
156 |       case Token.FINALLY:
157 |         return true;
158 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
159 |         // A function node represents the start of a function where the name
160 |         // is bleed into the local scope and parameters has been assigned
161 |         // to the formal argument names. The node includes the name of the
162 |         // function and the LP list since we assume the whole set up process
163 |         // is atomic without change in control flow. The next change of
164 |         // control is going into the function's body represent by the second
165 |         // child.
166 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
167 |       case Token.WHILE:
168 |       case Token.DO:
169 |       case Token.IF:
170 |         // Theses control structure is represented by its node that holds the
171 |         // condition. Each of them is a branch node based on its condition.
172 |         return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
173 | 
174 |       case Token.FOR:
175 |         // The FOR(;;) node differs from other control structure in that
176 |         // it has a initialization and a increment statement. Those
177 |         // two statements have its corresponding CFG nodes to represent them.
178 |         // The FOR node represents the condition check for each iteration.
179 |         // That way the following:
180 |         // for(var x = 0; x < 10; x++) { } has a graph that is isomorphic to
181 |         // var x = 0; while(x<10) {  x++; }
182 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
183 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
184 |         } else {
185 |           return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
186 |         }
187 |       case Token.SWITCH:
188 |       case Token.CASE:
189 |       case Token.CATCH:
190 |       case Token.WITH:
191 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild();
192 |       default:
193 |         return false;
194 |     }
195 |   }

===== M0743 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.annotateCalls(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 175-191
Documentation:
/**
     * There are two types of calls we are interested in calls without explicit
     * "this" values (what we are call "free" calls) and direct call to eval.
     */
Method code:
175 |     private void annotateCalls(Node n) {
176 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.CALL);
177 | 
178 |       // Keep track of of the "this" context of a call.  A call without an
179 |       // explicit "this" is a free call.
180 |       Node first = n.getFirstChild();
181 |       if (!NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
182 |         n.putBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL, true);
183 |       }

Original range: 1404-1427
Documentation:
/**
   * Compare this node to node2 recursively and return the first pair of nodes
   * that differs doing a preorder depth-first traversal. Package private for
   * testing. Returns null if the nodes are equivalent.
   */
Method code:
1404 |   NodeMismatch checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node node2) {
1405 |     boolean eq = false;
1406 | 
1407 |     if (type == node2.getType() && getChildCount() == node2.getChildCount()
1408 |         && getNodeClass(this) == getNodeClass(node2)) {
1409 |       eq = this.isEquivalentTo(node2);
1410 |     }
1411 | 
1412 |     if (!eq) {
1413 |       return new NodeMismatch(this, node2);
1414 |     }
1415 | 
1416 |     NodeMismatch res = null;
1417 |     Node n, n2;
1418 |     for (n = first, n2 = node2.first;
1419 |          res == null && n != null;
1420 |          n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1421 |       res = n.checkTreeEqualsImpl(n2);
1422 |       if (res != null) {
1423 |         return res;
1424 |       }
1425 |     }
1426 |     return res;
1427 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 196-215
Documentation:
/**
   * Given an entry node, find all the nodes reachable from that node
   * and prioritize them.
   */
Method code:
196 |   private void prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry) {
197 |     PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> worklist =
198 |         new PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>>(10, priorityComparator);
199 |     worklist.add(entry);
200 | 
201 |     while (!worklist.isEmpty()) {
202 |       DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> current = worklist.remove();
203 |       if (nodePriorities.containsKey(current)) {
204 |         continue;
205 |       }
206 | 
207 |       nodePriorities.put(current, ++priorityCounter);
208 | 
209 |       List<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> successors =
210 |           cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(current);
211 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : successors) {
212 |         worklist.add(candidate);
213 |       }
214 |     }
215 |   }

===== M0780 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.updateScopeForTypeChange(FlowScope,Node,JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 497-543
Documentation:
/**
   * Updates the scope according to the result of a type change, like
   * an assignment or a type cast.
   */
Method code:
497 |   private void updateScopeForTypeChange(
498 |       FlowScope scope, Node left, JSType leftType, JSType resultType) {
499 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(resultType);
500 |     switch (left.getType()) {
501 |       case Token.NAME:
502 |         String varName = left.getString();
503 |         Var var = syntacticScope.getVar(varName);
504 |         if (var != null && var.isLocal() && var.getScope() != syntacticScope) {
505 |           assignedOuterLocalVars.put(var.getScope(), var);
506 |         }
507 | 
508 |         // When looking at VAR initializers for declared VARs, we trust
509 |         // the declared type over the type it's being initialized to.
510 |         // This has two purposes:
511 |         // 1) We avoid re-declaring declared variables so that built-in
512 |         //    types defined in externs are not redeclared.
513 |         // 2) When there's a lexical closure like
514 |         //    /** @type {?string} */ var x = null;
515 |         //    function f() { x = 'xyz'; }
516 |         //    the inference will ignore the lexical closure,
517 |         //    which is just wrong. This bug needs to be fixed eventually.
518 |         boolean isVarDeclaration = left.hasChildren();
519 |         if (!isVarDeclaration || var == null || var.isTypeInferred()) {
520 |           redeclare(scope, varName, resultType);
521 |         }
522 |         left.setJSType(isVarDeclaration || leftType == null ?
523 |             resultType : null);
524 | 
525 |         if (var != null && var.isTypeInferred()) {
526 |           JSType oldType = var.getType();
527 |           var.setType(oldType == null ?
528 |               resultType : oldType.getLeastSupertype(resultType));
529 |         }
530 |         break;
531 |       case Token.GETPROP:
532 |         String qualifiedName = left.getQualifiedName();
533 |         if (qualifiedName != null) {
534 |           scope.inferQualifiedSlot(qualifiedName,
535 |               leftType == null ? getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : leftType,
536 |               resultType);
537 |         }
538 | 
539 |         left.setJSType(resultType);
540 |         ensurePropertyDefined(left, resultType);
541 |         break;
542 |     }
543 |   }

306 |   private FlowScope traverse(Node n, FlowScope scope) {

349 |         break;

354 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));

389 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));
390 |         break;

395 |         n.setJSType(getJSType(n.getFirstChild()));
396 |         break;

400 |         n.setJSType(getJSType(n.getLastChild()));
401 |         break;

404 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
405 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
406 |         break;

409 |       case Token.LE:

418 |       case Token.IN:

430 |           ensurePropertyDeclared(n.getFirstChild());
431 |         }

440 |       case Token.THROW:

442 |         break;
443 | 
444 |       case Token.CATCH:
445 |         scope = traverseCatch(n, scope);
446 |         break;
447 |     }

449 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
450 |       if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
451 |         JSType castType = info.getType().evaluate(syntacticScope, registry);

===== M0796 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isEquivalentTo(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 653-679
Documentation:
/**
   * Two function types are equal if their signatures match. Since they don't
   * have signatures, two interfaces are equal if their names match.
   */
Method code:
653 |   @Override
654 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(JSType otherType) {
655 |     if (!(otherType instanceof FunctionType)) {
656 |       return false;
657 |     }
658 |     FunctionType that = (FunctionType) otherType;
659 |     if (!that.isFunctionType()) {
660 |       return false;
661 |     }
662 |     if (this.isConstructor()) {
663 |       if (that.isConstructor()) {
664 |         return this == that;
665 |       }
666 |       return false;
667 |     }
668 |     if (this.isInterface()) {
669 |       if (that.isInterface()) {
670 |         return this.getReferenceName().equals(that.getReferenceName());
671 |       }
672 |       return false;
673 |     }
674 |     if (that.isInterface()) {
675 |       return false;
676 |     }
677 |     return this.typeOfThis.isEquivalentTo(that.typeOfThis) &&
678 |         this.call.isEquivalentTo(that.call);
679 |   }

===== M0810 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ClosureCodingConvention.getClassesDefinedByCall(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ClosureCodingConvention.java
Original range: 69-110
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>Understands several different inheritance patterns that occur in
   * Google code (various uses of {@code inherits} and {@code mixin}).
   */
Method code:
69 |   @Override
70 |   public SubclassRelationship getClassesDefinedByCall(Node callNode) {
71 |     Node callName = callNode.getFirstChild();
72 |     SubclassType type = typeofClassDefiningName(callName);
73 |     if (type != null) {
74 |       Node subclass = null;
75 |       Node superclass = callNode.getLastChild();
76 | 
77 |       // There are six possible syntaxes for a class-defining method:
78 |       // SubClass.inherits(SuperClass)
79 |       // goog.inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
80 |       // goog$inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
81 |       // SubClass.mixin(SuperClass.prototype)
82 |       // goog.mixin(SubClass.prototype, SuperClass.prototype)
83 |       // goog$mixin(SubClass.prototype, SuperClass.prototype)
84 |       if (callNode.getChildCount() == 2 &&
85 |           callName.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
86 |         // SubClass.inherits(SuperClass)
87 |         subclass = callName.getFirstChild();
88 |       } else if (callNode.getChildCount() == 3) {
89 |         // goog.inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
90 |         subclass = callName.getNext();
91 |       }
92 | 
93 |       // bail out if either of the side of the "inherits"
94 |       // isn't a real class name. This prevents us from
95 |       // doing something weird in cases like:
96 |       // goog.inherits(MySubClass, cond ? SuperClass1 : BaseClass2)
97 |       if (subclass != null &&
98 |           subclass.isUnscopedQualifiedName() &&
99 |           superclass.isUnscopedQualifiedName()) {
100 |         // make sure to strip the prototype off of the nodes
101 |         // to normalize for goog.mixin
102 |         return new SubclassRelationship(
103 |             type,
104 |             stripPrototype(subclass),
105 |             stripPrototype(superclass));
106 |       }
107 |     }
108 | 
109 |     return null;
110 |   }

===== M0812 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferReturnStatementsAsLastResort(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 307-338
Documentation:
/**
   * If we haven't found a return value yet, try to look at the "return"
   * statements in the function.
   */
Method code:
307 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferReturnStatementsAsLastResort(
308 |       @Nullable Node functionBlock) {
309 |     if (functionBlock == null || compiler.getInput(sourceName).isExtern()) {
310 |       return this;
311 |     }
312 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(functionBlock.getType() == Token.BLOCK);
313 |     if (returnType == null) {
314 |       boolean hasNonEmptyReturns = false;
315 |       List<Node> worklist = Lists.newArrayList(functionBlock);
316 |       while (!worklist.isEmpty()) {
317 |         Node current = worklist.remove(worklist.size() - 1);
318 |         int cType = current.getType();
319 |         if (cType == Token.RETURN && current.getFirstChild() != null ||
320 |             cType == Token.THROW) {
321 |           hasNonEmptyReturns = true;
322 |           break;
323 |         } else if (NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(current) ||
324 |             NodeUtil.isControlStructure(current)) {
325 |           for (Node child = current.getFirstChild();
326 |                child != null; child = child.getNext()) {
327 |             worklist.add(child);
328 |           }
329 |         }
330 |       }
331 | 
332 |       if (!hasNonEmptyReturns) {
333 |         returnType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
334 |         returnTypeInferred = true;
335 |       }
336 |     }
337 |     return this;
338 |   }

===== M0813 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1615-1640
Documentation:
/**
   * BasicTypeExpression := '*' | 'null' | 'undefined' | TypeName
   *     | FunctionType | UnionType | RecordType | ArrayType
   */
Method code:
1615 |   private Node parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1616 |     if (token == JsDocToken.STAR) {
1617 |       return newNode(Token.STAR);
1618 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LB) {
1619 |       skipEOLs();
1620 |       return parseArrayType(next());
1621 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
1622 |       skipEOLs();
1623 |       return parseRecordType(next());
1624 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LP) {
1625 |       skipEOLs();
1626 |       return parseUnionType(next());
1627 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
1628 |       String string = stream.getString();
1629 |       if ("function".equals(string)) {
1630 |         skipEOLs();
1631 |         return parseFunctionType(next());
1632 |       } else if ("null".equals(string) || "undefined".equals(string)) {
1633 |         return newStringNode(string);
1634 |       } else {
1635 |         return parseTypeName(token);
1636 |       }
1637 |     }
1638 | 
1639 |     return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1640 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createFromTypeNodesInternal(Node,String,StaticScope<JSType>,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 1371-1528
Documentation:
/** @see #createFromTypeNodes(Node, String, StaticScope, boolean) */
Method code:
1371 |   private JSType createFromTypeNodesInternal(Node n, String sourceName,
1372 |       StaticScope<JSType> scope, boolean forgiving) {
1373 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1374 |       case Token.LC: // Record type.
1375 |         return createRecordTypeFromNodes(
1376 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope);
1377 | 
1378 |       case Token.BANG: // Not nullable
1379 |         return createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1380 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope, forgiving)
1381 |             .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1382 | 
1383 |       case Token.QMARK: // Nullable or unknown
1384 |         Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();
1385 |         if (firstChild == null) {
1386 |           return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1387 |         }
1388 |         return createDefaultObjectUnion(
1389 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1390 |                 firstChild, sourceName, scope, forgiving));
1391 | 
1392 |       case Token.EQUALS: // Optional
1393 |         return createOptionalType(
1394 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1395 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope, false));
1396 | 
1397 |       case Token.ELLIPSIS: // Var args
1398 |         return createOptionalType(
1399 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1400 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope, false));
1401 | 
1402 |       case Token.STAR: // The AllType
1403 |         return getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
1404 | 
1405 |       case Token.LB: // Array type
1406 |         // TODO(nicksantos): Enforce membership restrictions on the Array.
1407 |         return getNativeType(ARRAY_TYPE);
1408 | 
1409 |       case Token.PIPE: // Union type
1410 |         UnionTypeBuilder builder = new UnionTypeBuilder(this);
1411 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
1412 |              child = child.getNext()) {
1413 |           builder.addAlternate(
1414 |               createFromTypeNodesInternal(child, sourceName, scope, false));
1415 |         }
1416 |         return builder.build();
1417 | 
1418 |       case Token.EMPTY: // When the return value of a function is not specified
1419 |         return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1420 | 
1421 |       case Token.VOID: // Only allowed in the return value of a function.
1422 |         return getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
1423 | 
1424 |       case Token.STRING:
1425 |         JSType namedType = getType(scope, n.getString(), sourceName,
1426 |             n.getLineno(), n.getCharno());
1427 |         if (forgiving) {
1428 |           namedType.forgiveUnknownNames();
1429 |         }
1430 |         if (resolveMode != ResolveMode.LAZY_NAMES) {
1431 |           namedType = namedType.resolveInternal(reporter, scope);
1432 |         }
1433 |         if ((namedType instanceof ObjectType) &&
1434 |             !(nonNullableTypeNames.contains(n.getString()))) {
1435 |           Node typeList = n.getFirstChild();
1436 |           if (typeList != null &&
1437 |               ("Array".equals(n.getString()) ||
1438 |                "Object".equals(n.getString()))) {
1439 |             JSType parameterType =
1440 |                 createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1441 |                     typeList.getLastChild(), sourceName, scope, false);
1442 |             namedType = new ParameterizedType(
1443 |                 this, (ObjectType) namedType, parameterType);
1444 |             if (typeList.hasMoreThanOneChild()) {
1445 |               JSType indexType =
1446 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1447 |                       typeList.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope, false);
1448 |               namedType = new IndexedType(
1449 |                   this, (ObjectType) namedType, indexType);
1450 |             }
1451 |           }
1452 |           return createDefaultObjectUnion(namedType);
1453 |         } else {
1454 |           return namedType;
1455 |         }
1456 | 
1457 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1458 |         ObjectType thisType = null;
1459 |         boolean isConstructor = false;
1460 |         Node current = n.getFirstChild();
1461 |         if (current.getType() == Token.THIS ||
1462 |             current.getType() == Token.NEW) {
1463 |           Node contextNode = current.getFirstChild();
1464 |           thisType =
1465 |               ObjectType.cast(
1466 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1467 |                       contextNode, sourceName, scope, false)
1468 |                   .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
1469 |           if (thisType == null) {
1470 |             reporter.warning(
1471 |                 ScriptRuntime.getMessage0(
1472 |                     current.getType() == Token.THIS ?
1473 |                     "msg.jsdoc.function.thisnotobject" :
1474 |                     "msg.jsdoc.function.newnotobject"),
1475 |                 sourceName,
1476 |                 contextNode.getLineno(), "", contextNode.getCharno());
1477 |           }
1478 | 
1479 |           isConstructor = current.getType() == Token.NEW;
1480 |           current = current.getNext();
1481 |         }
1482 | 
1483 |         FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1484 | 
1485 |         if (current.getType() == Token.LP) {
1486 |           Node args = current.getFirstChild();
1487 |           for (Node arg = current.getFirstChild(); arg != null;
1488 |                arg = arg.getNext()) {
1489 |             if (arg.getType() == Token.ELLIPSIS) {
1490 |               if (arg.getChildCount() == 0) {
1491 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
1492 |               } else {
1493 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(
1494 |                     createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1495 |                         arg.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope, false));
1496 |               }
1497 |             } else {
1498 |               JSType type = createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1499 |                   arg, sourceName, scope, false);
1500 |               if (arg.getType() == Token.EQUALS) {
1501 |                 boolean addSuccess = paramBuilder.addOptionalParams(type);
1502 |                 if (!addSuccess) {
1503 |                   reporter.warning(
1504 |                       ScriptRuntime.getMessage0("msg.jsdoc.function.varargs"),
1505 |                       sourceName, arg.getLineno(), "", arg.getCharno());
1506 |                 }
1507 |               } else {
1508 |                 paramBuilder.addRequiredParams(type);
1509 |               }
1510 |             }
1511 |           }
1512 |           current = current.getNext();
1513 |         }
1514 | 
1515 |         JSType returnType =
1516 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(current, sourceName, scope, false);
1517 | 
1518 |         return new FunctionBuilder(this)
1519 |             .withParams(paramBuilder)
1520 |             .withReturnType(returnType)
1521 |             .withTypeOfThis(thisType)
1522 |             .setIsConstructor(isConstructor)
1523 |             .build();
1524 |     }
1525 | 
1526 |     throw new IllegalStateException(
1527 |         "Unexpected node in type expression: " + n.toString());
1528 |   }

===== M0823 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 292-313
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if this is an immutable value.
   */
Method code:
292 |   static boolean isImmutableValue(Node n) {
293 |     switch (n.getType()) {
294 |       case Token.STRING:
295 |       case Token.NUMBER:
296 |       case Token.NULL:
297 |       case Token.TRUE:
298 |       case Token.FALSE:
299 |         return true;
300 |       case Token.VOID:
301 |       case Token.NEG:
302 |         return isImmutableValue(n.getFirstChild());
303 |       case Token.NAME:
304 |         String name = n.getString();
305 |         // We assume here that programs don't change the value of the keyword
306 |         // undefined to something other than the value undefined.
307 |         return "undefined".equals(name)
308 |             || "Infinity".equals(name)
309 |             || "NaN".equals(name);
310 |     }
311 | 
312 |     return false;
313 |   }

===== M0836 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.maybeLineBreak()

Method code:
425 |     @Override
426 |     void maybeLineBreak() {
427 |       if (lineBreak) {
428 |         if (sawFunction) {
429 |           startNewLine();
430 |           sawFunction = false;
431 |         }
432 |       }
433 | 
434 |       // Since we are at a legal line break, can we upgrade the
435 |       // preferred break position?  We prefer to break after a
436 |       // semicolon rather than before it.
437 |       int len = code.length();
438 |       if (preferredBreakPosition == len - 1) {
439 |         char ch = code.charAt(len - 1);
440 |         if (ch == ';') {
441 |           preferredBreakPosition = len;
442 |         }
443 |       }
444 |       maybeCutLine();
445 |     }

===== M0846 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.precedence(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 900-975
Documentation:
/*
   *  0 comma ,
   *  1 assignment = += -= *= /= %= <<= >>= >>>= &= ^= |=
   *  2 conditional ?:
   *  3 logical-or ||
   *  4 logical-and &&
   *  5 bitwise-or |
   *  6 bitwise-xor ^
   *  7 bitwise-and &
   *  8 equality == !=
   *  9 relational < <= > >=
   * 10 bitwise shift << >> >>>
   * 11 addition/subtraction + -
   * 12 multiply/divide * / %
   * 13 negation/increment ! ~ - ++ --
   * 14 call, member () [] .
   */
Method code:
900 |   static int precedence(int type) {
901 |     switch (type) {
902 |       case Token.COMMA:  return 0;
903 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
904 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
905 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
906 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
907 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
908 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
909 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
910 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
911 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
912 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
913 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
914 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return 1;
915 |       case Token.HOOK:   return 2;  // ?: operator
916 |       case Token.OR:     return 3;
917 |       case Token.AND:    return 4;
918 |       case Token.BITOR:  return 5;
919 |       case Token.BITXOR: return 6;
920 |       case Token.BITAND: return 7;
921 |       case Token.EQ:
922 |       case Token.NE:
923 |       case Token.SHEQ:
924 |       case Token.SHNE:   return 8;
925 |       case Token.LT:
926 |       case Token.GT:
927 |       case Token.LE:
928 |       case Token.GE:
929 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
930 |       case Token.IN:     return 9;
931 |       case Token.LSH:
932 |       case Token.RSH:
933 |       case Token.URSH:   return 10;
934 |       case Token.SUB:
935 |       case Token.ADD:    return 11;
936 |       case Token.MUL:
937 |       case Token.MOD:
938 |       case Token.DIV:    return 12;
939 |       case Token.INC:
940 |       case Token.DEC:
941 |       case Token.NEW:
942 |       case Token.DELPROP:
943 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
944 |       case Token.VOID:
945 |       case Token.NOT:
946 |       case Token.BITNOT:
947 |       case Token.POS:
948 |       case Token.NEG:    return 13;
949 | 
950 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
951 |       case Token.CALL:
952 |       case Token.EMPTY:
953 |       case Token.FALSE:
954 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
955 |       case Token.GETELEM:
956 |       case Token.GETPROP:
957 |       case Token.GET_REF:
958 |       case Token.IF:
959 |       case Token.LP:
960 |       case Token.NAME:
961 |       case Token.NULL:
962 |       case Token.NUMBER:
963 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
964 |       case Token.REGEXP:
965 |       case Token.RETURN:
966 |       case Token.STRING:
967 |       case Token.THIS:
968 |       case Token.TRUE:
969 |         return 15;
970 | 
971 |       default: throw new Error("Unknown precedence for " +
972 |                                Node.tokenToName(type) +
973 |                                " (type " + type + ")");
974 |     }
975 |   }

===== M0848 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PureFunctionIdentifier.getCallableDefinitions(DefinitionProvider,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PureFunctionIdentifier.java
Original range: 189-244
Documentation:
/**
   * Query the DefinitionProvider for the list of definitions that
   * correspond to a given qualified name subtree.  Return null if
   * DefinitionProvider does not contain an entry for a given name,
   * one or more of the values returned by getDeclarations is not
   * callable, or the "name" node is not a GETPROP or NAME.
   *
   * @param definitionProvider The name reference graph
   * @param name Query node
   * @return non-empty definition list or null
   */
Method code:
189 |   private static Collection<Definition> getCallableDefinitions(
190 |       DefinitionProvider definitionProvider, Node name) {
191 |     if (NodeUtil.isGetProp(name) || NodeUtil.isName(name)) {
192 |       List<Definition> result = Lists.newArrayList();
193 | 
194 |       Collection<Definition> decls =
195 |           definitionProvider.getDefinitionsReferencedAt(name);
196 |       if (decls == null) {
197 |         return null;
198 |       }
199 | 
200 |       for (Definition current : decls) {
201 |         Node rValue = current.getRValue();
202 |         if ((rValue != null) && NodeUtil.isFunction(rValue)) {
203 |           result.add(current);
204 |         } else {
205 |           return null;
206 |         }
207 |       }
208 | 
209 |       return result;
210 |     } else if (name.getType() == Token.OR || name.getType() == Token.HOOK) {
211 |       Node firstVal;
212 |       if (name.getType() == Token.HOOK) {
213 |         firstVal = name.getFirstChild().getNext();
214 |       } else {
215 |         firstVal = name.getFirstChild();
216 |       }
217 | 
218 |       Collection<Definition> defs1 = getCallableDefinitions(definitionProvider,
219 |                                                             firstVal);
220 |       Collection<Definition> defs2 = getCallableDefinitions(definitionProvider,
221 |                                                             firstVal.getNext());
222 |       if (defs1 != null && defs2 != null) {
223 |         defs1.addAll(defs2);
224 |         return defs1;
225 |       } else {
226 |         return null;
227 |       }
228 |     } else if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(name)) {
229 |       // The anonymous function reference is also the definition.
230 |       // TODO(user) Change SimpleDefinitionFinder so it is possible to query for
231 |       // function expressions by function node.
232 | 
233 |       // isExtern is false in the call to the constructor for the
234 |       // FunctionExpressionDefinition below because we know that
235 |       // getCallableDefinitions() will only be called on the first
236 |       // child of a call and thus the function expression
237 |       // definition will never be an extern.
238 |       return Lists.newArrayList(
239 |           (Definition)
240 |               new DefinitionsRemover.FunctionExpressionDefinition(name, false));
241 |     } else {
242 |       return null;
243 |     }
244 |   }

===== M0849 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.maybeCutLine()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 451-467
Documentation:
/**
     * This may start a new line if the current line is longer than the line
     * length threshold.
     */
Method code:
451 |     @Override
452 |     void maybeCutLine() {
453 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold) {
454 |         // Use the preferred position provided it will break the line.
455 |         if (preferredBreakPosition > lineStartPosition &&
456 |             preferredBreakPosition < lineStartPosition + lineLength) {
457 |           int position = preferredBreakPosition;
458 |           code.insert(position, '\n');
459 |           reportLineCut(lineIndex, position - lineStartPosition);
460 |           lineIndex++;
461 |           lineLength -= (position - lineStartPosition);
462 |           lineStartPosition = position + 1;
463 |         } else {
464 |           startNewLine();
465 |         }
466 |       }
467 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverseNew(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 979-1002
Documentation:

Method code:
979 |   private FlowScope traverseNew(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
980 |     Node constructor = n.getFirstChild();
981 |     scope = traverse(constructor, scope);
982 | 
983 |     JSType constructorType = constructor.getJSType();
984 |     JSType type = null;
985 |     if (constructorType != null) {
986 |       constructorType = constructorType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
987 |       if (constructorType.isUnknownType()) {
988 |         type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
989 |       } else if (constructorType instanceof FunctionType) {
990 |         FunctionType ct = (FunctionType) constructorType;
991 |         if (ct.isConstructor()) {
992 |           type = ct.getInstanceType();
993 |         }
994 |       }
995 |     }
996 |     n.setJSType(type);
997 | 
998 |     for (Node arg = constructor.getNext(); arg != null; arg = arg.getNext()) {
999 |       scope = traverse(arg, scope);
1000 |     }
1001 |     return scope;
1002 |   }

859 |   private void updateTypeOfParametersOnClosure(Node n, FunctionType fnType) {
860 |     int i = 0;
861 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
862 |     for (Node iParameter : fnType.getParameters()) {
863 |       JSType iParameterType = iParameter.getJSType();
864 |       if (iParameterType instanceof FunctionType) {
865 |         FunctionType iParameterFnType = (FunctionType) iParameterType;
866 | 
867 |         if (i + 1 >= childCount) {
868 |           // TypeCheck#visitParametersList will warn so we bail.
869 |           return;
870 |         }
871 |         Node iArgument = n.getChildAtIndex(i + 1);
872 |         JSType iArgumentType = getJSType(iArgument);
873 |         if (iArgument.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
874 |             iArgumentType instanceof FunctionType &&
875 |             iArgument.getJSDocInfo() == null) {
876 |           iArgument.setJSType(iParameterFnType);
877 |         }
878 |       }
879 |       i++;
880 |     }
881 |   }

811 |   static boolean nodeTypeMayHaveSideEffects(Node n, AbstractCompiler compiler) {
812 |     if (isAssignmentOp(n)) {
813 |       return true;
814 |     }
815 | 
816 |     switch(n.getType()) {
817 |       case Token.DELPROP:

===== M0876 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addNumber(double)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 233-259
Documentation:

Method code:
233 |   void addNumber(double x) {
234 |     // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of x- -4 as
235 |     // x--4 (which is a syntax error).
236 |     char prev = getLastChar();
237 |     if (x < 0 && prev == '-') {
238 |       add(" ");
239 |     }
240 | 
241 |     if ((long) x == x) {
242 |       long value = (long) x;
243 |       long mantissa = value;
244 |       int exp = 0;
245 |       if (Math.abs(x) >= 100) {
246 |         while (mantissa / 10 * Math.pow(10, exp + 1) == value) {
247 |           mantissa /= 10;
248 |           exp++;
249 |         }
250 |       }
251 |       if (exp > 2) {
252 |         add(Long.toString(mantissa) + "E" + Integer.toString(exp));
253 |       } else {
254 |         add(Long.toString(value));
255 |       }
256 |     } else {
257 |       add(String.valueOf(x));
258 |     }
259 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SimpleDefinitionFinder.DefinitionGatheringCallback.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SimpleDefinitionFinder.java
Original range: 158-262
Documentation:

Method code:
158 |     @Override
159 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal traversal, Node node, Node parent) {
160 |       // Arguments of external functions should not count as name
161 |       // definitions.  They are placeholder names for documentation
162 |       // purposes only which are not reachable from anywhere.
163 |       if (inExterns && NodeUtil.isName(node) && parent.getType() == Token.LP) {
164 |         return;
165 |       }
166 | 
167 |       Definition def =
168 |           DefinitionsRemover.getDefinition(node, inExterns);
169 |       if (def != null) {
170 |         String name = getSimplifiedName(def.getLValue());
171 |         if (name != null) {
172 |           Node rValue = def.getRValue();
173 |           if ((rValue != null) &&
174 |               !NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(rValue) &&
175 |               !NodeUtil.isFunction(rValue)) {
176 | 
177 |             // Unhandled complex expression
178 |             Definition unknownDef =
179 |                 new UnknownDefinition(def.getLValue(), inExterns);
180 |             def = unknownDef;
181 |           }
182 | 
183 |           // TODO(johnlenz) : remove this stub dropping code if it becomes
184 |           // illegal to have untyped stubs in the externs definitions.
185 |           if (inExterns) {
186 |             // We need special handling of untyped externs stubs here:
187 |             //    the stub should be dropped if the name is provided elsewhere.
188 | 
189 |             List<Definition> stubsToRemove = Lists.newArrayList();
190 |             String qualifiedName = node.getQualifiedName();
191 | 
192 |             // If there is no qualified name for this, then there will be
193 |             // no stubs to remove. This will happen if node is an object
194 |             // literal key.
195 |             if (qualifiedName != null) {
196 |               for (Definition prevDef : nameDefinitionMultimap.get(name)) {
197 |                 if (prevDef instanceof ExternalNameOnlyDefinition
198 |                     && !jsdocContainsDeclarations(node)) {
199 |                   String prevName = prevDef.getLValue().getQualifiedName();
200 |                   if (qualifiedName.equals(prevName)) {
201 |                     // Drop this stub, there is a real definition.
202 |                     stubsToRemove.add(prevDef);
203 |                   }
204 |                 }
205 |               }
206 | 
207 |               for (Definition prevDef : stubsToRemove) {
208 |                 nameDefinitionMultimap.remove(name, prevDef);
209 |               }
210 |             }
211 |           }
212 | 
213 |           nameDefinitionMultimap.put(name, def);
214 |           definitionSiteMap.put(node,
215 |                                 new DefinitionSite(node,
216 |                                                    def,
217 |                                                    traversal.getModule(),
218 |                                                    traversal.inGlobalScope(),
219 |                                                    inExterns));
220 |         }
221 |       }
222 | 
223 |       if (inExterns && (parent != null) && NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(parent)) {
224 |         String name = getSimplifiedName(node);
225 |         if (name != null) {
226 | 
227 |           // TODO(johnlenz) : remove this code if it becomes illegal to have
228 |           // stubs in the externs definitions.
229 | 
230 |           // We need special handling of untyped externs stubs here:
231 |           //    the stub should be dropped if the name is provided elsewhere.
232 |           // We can't just drop the stub now as it needs to be used as the
233 |           //    externs definition if no other definition is provided.
234 | 
235 |           boolean dropStub = false;
236 |           if (!jsdocContainsDeclarations(node)) {
237 |             String qualifiedName = node.getQualifiedName();
238 |             if (qualifiedName != null) {
239 |               for (Definition prevDef : nameDefinitionMultimap.get(name)) {
240 |                 String prevName = prevDef.getLValue().getQualifiedName();
241 |                 if (qualifiedName.equals(prevName)) {
242 |                   dropStub = true;
243 |                   break;
244 |                 }
245 |               }
246 |             }
247 |           }
248 | 
249 |           if (!dropStub) {
250 |             // Incomplete definition
251 |             Definition definition = new ExternalNameOnlyDefinition(node);
252 |             nameDefinitionMultimap.put(name, definition);
253 |             definitionSiteMap.put(node,
254 |                                   new DefinitionSite(node,
255 |                                                      definition,
256 |                                                      traversal.getModule(),
257 |                                                      traversal.inGlobalScope(),
258 |                                                      inExterns));
259 |           }
260 |         }
261 |       }
262 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.toString(StringBuilder,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 923-1039
Documentation:

Method code:
923 |   private void toString(
924 |       StringBuilder sb,
925 |       boolean printSource,
926 |       boolean printAnnotations,
927 |       boolean printType) {
928 |     if (Token.printTrees) {
929 |       sb.append(Token.name(type));
930 |       if (this instanceof StringNode) {
931 |         sb.append(' ');
932 |         sb.append(getString());
933 |       } else if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
934 |         sb.append(' ');
935 |         // In the case of JsDoc trees, the first child is often not a string
936 |         // which causes exceptions to be thrown when calling toString or
937 |         // toStringTree.
938 |         if (first.getType() == Token.STRING) {
939 |           sb.append(first.getString());
940 |         }
941 |       } else if (this instanceof ScriptOrFnNode) {
942 |         ScriptOrFnNode sof = (ScriptOrFnNode) this;
943 |         if (this instanceof FunctionNode) {
944 |           FunctionNode fn = (FunctionNode) this;
945 |           sb.append(' ');
946 |           sb.append(fn.getFunctionName());
947 |         }
948 |         if (printSource) {
949 |           sb.append(" [source name: ");
950 |           sb.append(sof.getSourceName());
951 |           sb.append("] [encoded source length: ");
952 |           sb.append(sof.getEncodedSourceEnd() - sof.getEncodedSourceStart());
953 |           sb.append("] [base line: ");
954 |           sb.append(sof.getBaseLineno());
955 |           sb.append("] [end line: ");
956 |           sb.append(sof.getEndLineno());
957 |           sb.append(']');
958 |         }
959 |       } else if (type == Token.NUMBER) {
960 |         sb.append(' ');
961 |         sb.append(getDouble());
962 |       }
963 |       if (printSource) {
964 |         int lineno = getLineno();
965 |         if (lineno != -1) {
966 |           sb.append(' ');
967 |           sb.append(lineno);
968 |         }
969 |       }
970 | 
971 |       if (printAnnotations) {
972 |         int[] keys = getSortedPropTypes();
973 |         for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
974 |           int type = keys[i];
975 |           PropListItem x = lookupProperty(type);
976 |           sb.append(" [");
977 |           sb.append(propToString(type));
978 |           sb.append(": ");
979 |           String value;
980 |           switch (type) {
981 |             case TARGETBLOCK_PROP: // can't add this as it recurses
982 |               value = "target block property";
983 |               break;
984 |             case LOCAL_BLOCK_PROP: // can't add this as it is dull
985 |               value = "last local block";
986 |               break;
987 |             case ISNUMBER_PROP:
988 |               switch (x.intValue) {
989 |                 case BOTH:
990 |                   value = "both";
991 |                   break;
992 |                 case RIGHT:
993 |                   value = "right";
994 |                   break;
995 |                 case LEFT:
996 |                   value = "left";
997 |                   break;
998 |                 default:
999 |                   throw Kit.codeBug();
1000 |               }
1001 |               break;
1002 |             case SPECIALCALL_PROP:
1003 |               switch (x.intValue) {
1004 |                 case SPECIALCALL_EVAL:
1005 |                   value = "eval";
1006 |                   break;
1007 |                 case SPECIALCALL_WITH:
1008 |                   value = "with";
1009 |                   break;
1010 |                 default:
1011 |                   // NON_SPECIALCALL should not be stored
1012 |                   throw Kit.codeBug();
1013 |               }
1014 |               break;
1015 |             default:
1016 |               Object obj = x.objectValue;
1017 |               if (obj != null) {
1018 |                 value = obj.toString();
1019 |               } else {
1020 |                 value = String.valueOf(x.intValue);
1021 |               }
1022 |               break;
1023 |           }
1024 |           sb.append(value);
1025 |           sb.append(']');
1026 |         }
1027 |       }
1028 | 
1029 |       if (printType) {
1030 |         if (jsType != null) {
1031 |           String jsTypeString = jsType.toString();
1032 |           if (jsTypeString != null) {
1033 |             sb.append(" : ");
1034 |             sb.append(jsTypeString);
1035 |           }
1036 |         }
1037 |       }
1038 |     }
1039 |   }

===== M0904 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.getQualifiedName()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1726-1740
Documentation:
/**
   * This function takes a set of GETPROP nodes and produces a string that is
   * each property separated by dots. If the node ultimately under the left
   * sub-tree is not a simple name, this is not a valid qualified name.
   *
   * @return a null if this is not a qualified name, or a dot-separated string
   *         of the name and properties.
   */
Method code:
1726 |   public String getQualifiedName() {
1727 |     if (type == Token.NAME) {
1728 |       return getString();
1729 |     } else if (type == Token.GETPROP) {
1730 |       String left = getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
1731 |       if (left == null) {
1732 |         return null;
1733 |       }
1734 |       return left + "." + getLastChild().getString();
1735 |     } else if (type == Token.THIS) {
1736 |       return "this";
1737 |     } else {
1738 |       return null;
1739 |     }
1740 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.updateTypeOfThisOnClosure(Node,FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 888-977
Documentation:
/**
   * For functions with function(this: T, ...) and T as parameters, type
   * inference will set the type of this on a function literal argument to the
   * the actual type of T.
   */
Method code:
888 |   private void updateTypeOfThisOnClosure(Node n, FunctionType fnType) {
889 |     // TODO(user): Make the template logic more general.
890 | 
891 |     if (fnType.getTemplateTypeName() == null) {
892 |       return;
893 |     }
894 | 
895 |     int i = 0;
896 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
897 |     // Find the parameter whose type is the template type.
898 |     for (Node iParameter : fnType.getParameters()) {
899 |       JSType iParameterType =
900 |           getJSType(iParameter).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
901 |       if (iParameterType.isTemplateType()) {
902 |         // Find the actual type of this argument.
903 |         JSType iArgumentType = null;
904 |         if (i + 1 < childCount) {
905 |           Node iArgument = n.getChildAtIndex(i + 1);
906 |           iArgumentType = getJSType(iArgument).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
907 |           if (!(iArgumentType instanceof ObjectType)) {
908 |             compiler.report(
909 |                 JSError.make(NodeUtil.getSourceName(iArgument), iArgument,
910 |                     TEMPLATE_TYPE_NOT_OBJECT_TYPE));
911 |             return;
912 |           }
913 |         }
914 | 
915 |         // Find the parameter whose type is function(this: T, ...)
916 |         boolean foundTemplateTypeOfThisParameter = false;
917 |         int j = 0;
918 |         for (Node jParameter : fnType.getParameters()) {
919 |           JSType jParameterType =
920 |               getJSType(jParameter).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
921 |           if (jParameterType instanceof FunctionType) {
922 |             FunctionType jParameterFnType = (FunctionType) jParameterType;
923 |             if (jParameterFnType.getTypeOfThis().equals(iParameterType)) {
924 |               foundTemplateTypeOfThisParameter = true;
925 |               // Find the actual type of the this argument.
926 |               if (j + 1 >= childCount) {
927 |                 // TypeCheck#visitParameterList will warn so we bail.
928 |                 return;
929 |               }
930 |               Node jArgument = n.getChildAtIndex(j + 1);
931 |               JSType jArgumentType = getJSType(jArgument);
932 |               if (jArgument.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
933 |                   jArgumentType instanceof FunctionType) {
934 |                 if (iArgumentType != null &&
935 |                     // null and undefined get filtered out above.
936 |                     !iArgumentType.isNoType()) {
937 |                   // If it's an function expression, update the type of this
938 |                   // using the actual type of T.
939 |                   FunctionType jArgumentFnType = (FunctionType) jArgumentType;
940 |                   if (jArgumentFnType.getTypeOfThis().isUnknownType()) {
941 |                     // The new type will be picked up when we traverse the inner
942 |                     // function.
943 |                     jArgument.setJSType(
944 |                         registry.createFunctionTypeWithNewThisType(
945 |                             jArgumentFnType, (ObjectType) iArgumentType));
946 |                   }
947 |                   // Warn if the anonymous function literal does not reference this.
948 |                   if (!NodeUtil.referencesThis(
949 |                           NodeUtil.getFunctionBody(jArgument))) {
950 |                     compiler.report(JSError.make(NodeUtil.getSourceName(n), n,
951 |                         FUNCTION_LITERAL_UNREAD_THIS));
952 |                   }
953 |                 } else {
954 |                   // Warn if the anonymous function literal references this.
955 |                   if (NodeUtil.referencesThis(
956 |                           NodeUtil.getFunctionBody(jArgument))) {
957 |                     compiler.report(JSError.make(NodeUtil.getSourceName(n), n,
958 |                         FUNCTION_LITERAL_UNDEFINED_THIS));
959 |                   }
960 |                 }
961 |               }
962 |               // TODO(user): Add code to TypeCheck to check that the
963 |               // types of the arguments match.
964 |             }
965 |           }
966 |           j++;
967 |         }
968 | 
969 |         if (!foundTemplateTypeOfThisParameter) {
970 |           compiler.report(JSError.make(NodeUtil.getSourceName(n), n,
971 |               TEMPLATE_TYPE_OF_THIS_EXPECTED));
972 |           return;
973 |         }
974 |       }
975 |       i++;
976 |     }
977 |   }

670 |     void defineNamedTypeAssign(Node n, Node parent) {
671 |       assertDefinitionNode(n, Token.ASSIGN);
672 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
673 | 
674 |       // TODO(nicksantos): We should support direct assignment to a
675 |       // prototype, as in:
676 |       // Foo.prototype = {

678 |       //   b: function() { ... }

===== M0917 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 117-170
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
117 |   private void scanVars(Node n, Node parent) {
118 |     switch (n.getType()) {
119 |       case Token.VAR:
120 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
121 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
122 |              child != null;) {
123 |           Node next = child.getNext();
124 |           declareVar(child);
125 |           child = next;
126 |         }
127 |         return;
128 | 
129 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
130 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
131 |           return;
132 |         }
133 | 
134 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
135 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
136 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
137 |           return;
138 |         }
139 |         declareVar(n.getFirstChild());
140 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
141 | 
142 |       case Token.CATCH:
143 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
144 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NAME);
145 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
146 |         // is the code block
147 | 
148 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
149 |         final Node block = var.getNext();
150 | 
151 |         declareVar(var);
152 |         scanVars(block, n);
153 |         return;  // only one child to scan
154 | 
155 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
156 |         sourceName = (String) n.getProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP);
157 |         break;
158 |     }
159 | 
160 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
161 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
162 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
163 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
164 |            child != null;) {
165 |         Node next = child.getNext();
166 |         scanVars(child, n);
167 |         child = next;
168 |       }
169 |     }
170 |   }

/**
   * Builds the function type, and puts it in the registry.
   */
Method code:
613 |   FunctionType buildAndRegister() {
614 |     if (returnType == null) {
615 |       returnType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
616 |     }
617 | 
618 |     if (parametersNode == null) {
619 |       throw new IllegalStateException(
620 |           "All Function types must have params and a return type");

629 |         typeRegistry.declareType(fnName, fnType.getInstanceType());

/**
   * Defines a property whose type is inferred.
   * @param propertyName the property's name
   * @param type the type
   * @param inExterns {@code true} if this property was defined in an externs
   *        file. TightenTypes assumes that any function passed to an externs
   *        property could be called, so setting this incorrectly could result

Original range: 185-197

Method code:
185 |   private boolean isConnectedInDirection(N n1, Predicate<E> edgeMatcher, N n2) {
186 |     // Verify the nodes.
187 |     DiGraphNode<N, E> dNode1 = getNodeOrFail(n1);
188 |     DiGraphNode<N, E> dNode2 = getNodeOrFail(n2);
189 |     for (DiGraphEdge<N, E> outEdge : dNode1.getOutEdges()) {
190 |       if (outEdge.getDestination() == dNode2 &&
191 |           edgeMatcher.apply(outEdge.getValue())) {
192 |         return true;
193 |       }
194 |     }
195 | 
196 |     return false;
197 |   }

===== M0923 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.jsString(String,CharsetEncoder)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 907-934
Documentation:
/** Outputs a js string, using the optimal (single/double) quote character */
Method code:
907 |   static String jsString(String s, CharsetEncoder outputCharsetEncoder) {
908 |     int singleq = 0, doubleq = 0;
909 | 
910 |     // could count the quotes and pick the optimal quote character
911 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
912 |       switch (s.charAt(i)) {
913 |         case '"': doubleq++; break;
914 |         case '\'': singleq++; break;
915 |       }
916 |     }
917 | 
918 |     String doublequote, singlequote;
919 |     char quote;
920 |     if (singleq < doubleq) {
921 |       // more double quotes so escape the single quotes
922 |       quote = '\'';
923 |       doublequote = "\"";
924 |       singlequote = "\\\'";
925 |     } else {
926 |       // more single quotes so escape the doubles
927 |       quote = '\"';
928 |       doublequote = "\\\"";
929 |       singlequote = "\'";
930 |     }
931 | 
932 |     return strEscape(s, quote, doublequote, singlequote, "\\\\",
933 |         outputCharsetEncoder);
934 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)

426 |     @Override
427 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
428 |       attachLiteralTypes(n);
429 | 
430 |       switch (n.getType()) {
431 |         case Token.CALL:
432 |           checkForClassDefiningCalls(t, n, parent);
433 |           break;
434 | 
435 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
436 |           if (t.getInput() == null || !t.getInput().isExtern()) {
437 |             nonExternFunctions.add(n);
438 |           }
439 | 
440 |           // VARs and ASSIGNs are handled in different branches of this
441 |           // switch statement.
442 |           if (parent.getType() != Token.ASSIGN &&
443 |               parent.getType() != Token.NAME) {
444 |             defineDeclaredFunction(n, parent);
445 |           }
446 | 
447 |           break;
448 | 
449 |         case Token.ASSIGN:
450 |           // Handle constructor and enum definitions.
451 |           defineNamedTypeAssign(n, parent);
452 | 
453 |           // Handle initialization of properties.
454 |           Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();
455 |           if (firstChild.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
456 |               firstChild.isQualifiedName()) {
457 |             maybeDeclareQualifiedName(t, n.getJSDocInfo(),
458 |                 firstChild, n, firstChild.getNext());
459 |           }
460 |           break;
461 | 
462 |         case Token.CATCH:
463 |           defineCatch(n, parent);
464 |           break;
465 | 
466 |         case Token.VAR:
467 |           defineVar(n, parent);
468 |           break;
469 | 
470 |         case Token.GETPROP:
471 |           // Handle stubbed properties.
472 |           if (parent.getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT &&
473 |               n.isQualifiedName()) {
474 |             maybeDeclareQualifiedName(t, n.getJSDocInfo(), n, parent, null);
475 |           }
476 |           break;
477 |       }
478 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)

Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1146 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1147 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1148 |       return -1;
1149 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1150 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1151 |     } else {
1152 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1153 |     }
1154 |   }

/**
   * Tokenizes JSDoc comments.
   */
Method code:
59 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
60 |   final JsDocToken getJsDocToken() {
61 |     int c;
62 |     stringBufferTop = 0;
63 |     for (;;) {
64 |       // eat white spaces
65 |       for (;;) {
66 |         charno = -1;
67 |         c = getChar();
68 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
69 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
70 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
71 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
72 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
73 |           break;
74 |         }
75 |       }
76 | 
77 |       switch (c) {
78 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
79 |         case '@':
80 |           do {
81 |             c = getChar();
82 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
83 |               addToString(c);
84 |             } else {
85 |               ungetChar(c);
86 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
87 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
88 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
89 |             }
90 |           } while (true);
91 | 
92 |         case '*':
93 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
94 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
95 |           } else {
96 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
97 |           }
98 | 
99 |         case ',':
100 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
101 | 
102 |         case '>':
103 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
104 | 
105 |         case '(':
106 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
107 | 
108 |         case ')':
109 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
110 | 
111 |         case '{':
112 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
113 | 
114 |         case '}':
115 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
116 | 
117 |         case '[':
118 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
119 | 
120 |         case ']':
121 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
122 | 
123 |         case '?':
124 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
125 | 
126 |         case '!':
127 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
128 | 
129 |         case ':':
130 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
131 | 
132 |         case '=':
133 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
134 | 
135 |         case '|':
136 |           matchChar('|');
137 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
138 | 
139 |         case '.':
140 |           c = getChar();
141 |           if (c == '<') {
142 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
143 |           } else {
144 |             if (c == '.') {
145 |               c = getChar();
146 |               if (c == '.') {
147 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
148 |               } else {
149 |                 addToString('.');
150 |               }
151 |             }
152 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
153 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
154 |             c = '.';
155 |           }
156 |           // fall through
157 | 
158 |         default: {
159 |           // recognize a jsdoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
160 |           // a non-jsdoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
161 |           int c1 = c;
162 |           addToString(c);
163 |           int c2 = getChar();
164 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
165 |             ungetChar(c2);
166 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
167 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
168 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
169 |           } else {
170 |             do {
171 |               c1 = c2;
172 |               c2 = getChar();
173 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
174 |                 ungetChar(c2);
175 |                 ungetChar(c1);
176 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
177 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
178 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
179 |               } else {
180 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
181 |                   addToString(c1);
182 |                 } else {
183 |                   ungetChar(c2);
184 |                   addToString(c1);
185 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
186 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
187 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
188 |                 }
189 |               }
190 |             } while (true);
191 |           }
192 |         }
193 |       }
194 |     }
195 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1200-1304

/**
     * Declare the symbol for a qualified name in the global scope.
     *
     * @param info The doc info for this property.
     * @param n A top-level GETPROP node (it should not be contained inside
     *     another GETPROP).
     * @param parent The parent of {@code n}.
     * @param rhsValue The node that {@code n} is being initialized to,
     *     or {@code null} if this is a stub declaration.
     */
Method code:
1200 |     void maybeDeclareQualifiedName(NodeTraversal t, JSDocInfo info,
1201 |         Node n, Node parent, Node rhsValue) {
1202 |       Node ownerNode = n.getFirstChild();
1203 |       String ownerName = ownerNode.getQualifiedName();
1204 |       String qName = n.getQualifiedName();
1205 |       String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
1206 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(qName != null && ownerName != null);
1207 | 
1208 |       // Function prototypes are special.
1209 |       // It's a common JS idiom to do:
1210 |       // F.prototype = { ... };
1211 |       // So if F does not have an explicitly declared super type,
1212 |       // allow F.prototype to be redefined arbitrarily.
1213 |       if ("prototype".equals(propName)) {
1214 |         Var qVar = scope.getVar(qName);
1215 |         if (qVar != null) {
1216 |           if (!qVar.isTypeInferred()) {
1217 |             // Just ignore assigns to declared prototypes.
1218 |             return;
1219 |           }
1220 |           if (qVar.getScope() == scope) {
1221 |             scope.undeclare(qVar);
1222 |           }
1223 |         }
1224 |       }
1225 | 
1226 |       // Precedence of type information on GETPROPs:
1227 |       // 1) @type annnotation / @enum annotation
1228 |       // 2) ASSIGN to FUNCTION literal
1229 |       // 3) @param/@return annotation (with no function literal)
1230 |       // 4) ASSIGN to anything else
1231 |       //
1232 |       // 1 and 3 are declarations, 4 is inferred, and 2 is a declaration iff
1233 |       // the function has not been declared before.
1234 |       //
1235 |       // FUNCTION literals are special because TypedScopeCreator is very smart
1236 |       // about getting as much type information as possible for them.
1237 | 
1238 |       // Determining type for #1 + #2 + #3
1239 |       JSType valueType = getDeclaredGetPropType(t, info, n, rhsValue);
1240 |       if (valueType == null && rhsValue != null) {
1241 |         // Determining type for #4
1242 |         valueType = rhsValue.getJSType();
1243 |       }
1244 | 
1245 |       if (valueType == null) {
1246 |         if (parent.getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT) {
1247 |           stubDeclarations.add(new StubDeclaration(
1248 |               n,
1249 |               t.getInput() != null && t.getInput().isExtern(),
1250 |               ownerName));
1251 |         }
1252 | 
1253 |         return;
1254 |       }
1255 | 
1256 |       boolean inferred = true;
1257 |       if (info != null) {
1258 |         // Determining declaration for #1 + #3
1259 |         inferred = !(info.hasType() || info.hasEnumParameterType() ||
1260 |             FunctionTypeBuilder.isFunctionTypeDeclaration(info));
1261 |       }
1262 | 
1263 |       if (inferred) {
1264 |         // Determining declaration for #2
1265 |         inferred = !(rhsValue != null &&
1266 |             rhsValue.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
1267 |             !scope.isDeclared(qName, false));
1268 |       }
1269 | 
1270 |       if (!inferred) {
1271 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1272 |         if (ownerType != null) {
1273 |           // Only declare this as an official property if it has not been
1274 |           // declared yet.
1275 |           boolean isExtern = t.getInput() != null && t.getInput().isExtern();
1276 |           if ((!ownerType.hasOwnProperty(propName) ||
1277 |                ownerType.isPropertyTypeInferred(propName)) &&
1278 |               ((isExtern && !ownerType.isNativeObjectType()) ||
1279 |                !ownerType.isInstanceType())) {
1280 |             // If the property is undeclared or inferred, declare it now.
1281 |             ownerType.defineDeclaredProperty(propName, valueType, isExtern);
1282 |           }
1283 |         }
1284 | 
1285 |         // If the property is already declared, the error will be
1286 |         // caught when we try to declare it in the current scope.
1287 |         defineSlot(n, parent, valueType, inferred);
1288 |       } else if (rhsValue != null &&
1289 |           rhsValue.getType() == Token.TRUE) {
1290 |         // We declare these for delegate proxy method properties.
1291 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1292 |         if (ownerType instanceof FunctionType) {
1293 |           JSType ownerTypeOfThis = ((FunctionType) ownerType).getTypeOfThis();
1294 |           String delegateName = codingConvention.getDelegateSuperclassName();
1295 |           JSType delegateType = delegateName == null ?
1296 |               null : typeRegistry.getType(delegateName);
1297 |           if (delegateType != null &&
1298 |               ownerTypeOfThis.isSubtype(delegateType)) {
1299 |             defineSlot(n, parent, getNativeType(BOOLEAN_TYPE),
1300 |                 true);
1301 |           }
1302 |         }
1303 |       }
1304 |     }

===== M0940 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 842-869
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
842 |   static boolean isSubtype(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
843 |     // unknown
844 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
845 |       return true;
846 |     }
847 |     // equality
848 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
849 |       return true;
850 |     }
851 |     // all type
852 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
853 |       return true;
854 |     }
855 |     // unions
856 |     if (thatType instanceof UnionType) {
857 |       UnionType union = (UnionType)thatType;
858 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
859 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
860 |           return true;
861 |         }
862 |       }
863 |     }
864 |     // named types
865 |     if (thatType instanceof NamedType) {
866 |       return thisType.isSubtype(((NamedType)thatType).getReferencedType());
867 |     }
868 |     return false;
869 |   }

===== M0948 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.ensurePropertyDefined(Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 548-590
Documentation:
/**
   * Defines a property if the property has not been defined yet.
   */
Method code:
548 |   private void ensurePropertyDefined(Node getprop, JSType rightType) {
549 |     String propName = getprop.getLastChild().getString();
550 |     JSType nodeType = getJSType(getprop.getFirstChild());
551 |     ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(
552 |         nodeType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
553 |     if (objectType == null) {
554 |       registry.registerPropertyOnType(propName, nodeType);
555 |     } else {
556 |       if (ensurePropertyDeclaredHelper(getprop, objectType)) {
557 |         return;
558 |       }
559 | 
560 |       if (!objectType.isPropertyTypeDeclared(propName)) {
561 |         // We do not want a "stray" assign to define an inferred property
562 |         // for every object of this type in the program. So we use a heuristic
563 |         // approach to determine whether to infer the propery.
564 |         //
565 |         // 1) If the property is already defined, join it with the previously
566 |         //    inferred type.
567 |         // 2) If this isn't an instance object, define it.
568 |         // 3) If the property of an object is being assigned in the constructor,
569 |         //    define it.
570 |         // 4) If this is a stub, define it.
571 |         // 5) Otherwise, do not define the type, but declare it in the registry
572 |         //    so that we can use it for missing property checks.
573 |         if (objectType.hasProperty(propName) ||
574 |             !objectType.isInstanceType()) {
575 |           if ("prototype".equals(propName)) {
576 |             objectType.defineDeclaredProperty(propName, rightType, false);
577 |           } else {
578 |             objectType.defineInferredProperty(propName, rightType, false);
579 |           }
580 |         } else {
581 |           if (getprop.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.THIS &&
582 |               getJSType(syntacticScope.getRootNode()).isConstructor()) {
583 |             objectType.defineInferredProperty(propName, rightType, false);
584 |           } else {
585 |             registry.registerPropertyOnType(propName, objectType);
586 |           }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1317-1354
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a {@link Token#FUNCTION} node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1317 |   private void visitFunction(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1318 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1319 | 
1320 |     FunctionType functionType = (FunctionType) n.getJSType();
1321 |     String functionPrivateName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
1322 |     if (functionType.isInterface() || functionType.isConstructor()) {
1323 |       FunctionType baseConstructor = functionType.
1324 |           getPrototype().getImplicitPrototype().getConstructor();
1325 |       if (baseConstructor != null &&
1326 |           baseConstructor != getNativeType(OBJECT_FUNCTION_TYPE) &&
1327 |           (baseConstructor.isConstructor() && functionType.isInterface() ||
1328 |            baseConstructor.isInterface() && functionType.isConstructor())) {
1329 |         compiler.report(
1330 |             t.makeError(n, CONFLICTING_EXTENDED_TYPE, functionPrivateName));
1331 |       }
1332 | 
1333 |       for (JSType baseInterface : functionType.getImplementedInterfaces()) {
1334 |         boolean badImplementedType = false;
1335 |         ObjectType baseInterfaceObj = ObjectType.cast(baseInterface);
1336 |         if (baseInterfaceObj != null) {
1337 |           FunctionType interfaceConstructor =
1338 |               baseInterfaceObj.getConstructor();
1339 |           if (interfaceConstructor != null &&
1340 |               !interfaceConstructor.isInterface()) {
1341 |             badImplementedType = true;
1342 |           }
1343 |         } else {
1344 |           badImplementedType = true;
1345 |         }
1346 |         if (badImplementedType) {
1347 |           report(t, n, BAD_IMPLEMENTED_TYPE, functionPrivateName);
1348 |         }
1349 |       }
1350 |       if (functionType.isConstructor()) {
1351 |         validator.expectAllInterfacePropertiesImplemented(functionType);
1352 |       }
1353 |     }
1354 |   }

/**
   * Expect that the first type can be assigned to a symbol of the second
   * type.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal.
   * @param n The node to issue warnings on.
   * @param rightType The type on the RHS of the assign.
   * @param leftType The type of the symbol on the LHS of the assign.
   * @param msg An extra message for the mismatch warning, if necessary.
   * @return True if the types matched, false otherwise.
   */
Method code:
327 |   boolean expectCanAssignTo(NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType rightType,
328 |       JSType leftType, String msg) {
329 |     if (!rightType.canAssignTo(leftType)) {
330 |       if (bothIntrinsics(rightType, leftType)) {
331 |         // We have a superior warning for this mistake, which gives you
332 |         // the line numbers of both types.
333 |         registerMismatch(rightType, leftType);
334 |       } else {
335 |         mismatch(t, n, msg, rightType, leftType);
336 |       }
337 |       return false;
338 |     }
339 |     return true;
340 |   }

Original range: 1088-1147
Documentation:
/**
     * Look for class-defining calls.
     * Because JS has no 'native' syntax for defining classes,
     * this is often very coding-convention dependent and business-logic heavy.
     */
Method code:
1088 |     private void checkForClassDefiningCalls(
1089 |         NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1090 |       SubclassRelationship relationship =
1091 |           codingConvention.getClassesDefinedByCall(n);
1092 |       if (relationship != null) {
1093 |         ObjectType superClass = ObjectType.cast(
1094 |             typeRegistry.getType(relationship.superclassName));
1095 |         ObjectType subClass = ObjectType.cast(
1096 |             typeRegistry.getType(relationship.subclassName));
1097 |         if (superClass != null && subClass != null) {
1098 |           FunctionType superCtor = superClass.getConstructor();
1099 |           FunctionType subCtor = subClass.getConstructor();
1100 | 
1101 |           if (relationship.type == SubclassType.INHERITS) {
1102 |             validator.expectSuperType(t, n, superClass, subClass);
1103 |           }
1104 | 
1105 |           if (superCtor != null && subCtor != null) {
1106 |             codingConvention.applySubclassRelationship(
1107 |                 superCtor, subCtor, relationship.type);
1108 |           }
1109 |         }
1110 |       }
1111 | 
1112 |       String singletonGetterClassName =
1113 |           codingConvention.getSingletonGetterClassName(n);
1114 |       if (singletonGetterClassName != null) {
1115 |         ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(
1116 |             typeRegistry.getType(singletonGetterClassName));
1117 |         if (objectType != null) {
1118 |           FunctionType functionType = objectType.getConstructor();
1119 | 
1120 |           if (functionType != null) {
1121 |             FunctionType getterType =
1122 |                 typeRegistry.createFunctionType(objectType);
1123 |             codingConvention.applySingletonGetter(functionType, getterType,
1124 |                 objectType);
1125 |           }
1126 |         }
1127 |       }
1128 | 
1129 |       DelegateRelationship delegateRelationship =
1130 |           codingConvention.getDelegateRelationship(n);
1131 |       if (delegateRelationship != null) {
1132 |         applyDelegateRelationship(delegateRelationship);
1133 |       }
1134 | 
1135 |       ObjectLiteralCast objectLiteralCast =
1136 |           codingConvention.getObjectLiteralCast(t, n);
1137 |       if (objectLiteralCast != null) {
1138 |         ObjectType type = ObjectType.cast(
1139 |             typeRegistry.getType(objectLiteralCast.typeName));
1140 |         if (type != null && type.getConstructor() != null) {
1141 |           setDeferredType(objectLiteralCast.objectNode, type);
1142 |         } else {

1144 |                   CONSTRUCTOR_EXPECTED));

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionParamBuilder.addVarArgs(JSType)

Documentation:
/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.
103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;
109 |   }
