92 |     } catch (IOException e) {
93 |       compiler.report(JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceName));
94 |     }
95 | 
96 |     if (root == null || compiler.hasHaltingErrors()) {
97 |       // There was a parse error or IOException, so use a dummy block.
98 |       root = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
99 |     } else {
100 |       compiler.prepareAst(root);
101 |     }
102 | 
103 |     // Set the source name so that the compiler passes can track
104 |     // the source file and module.
105 |     root.putProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP, sourceName);
106 |   }

===== M0992 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.ensureTyped(NodeTraversal,Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1677-1701
Documentation:
/**
   * Enforces type casts, and ensures the node is typed.
   *
   * A cast in the way that we use it in JSDoc annotations never
   * alters the generated code and therefore never can induce any runtime
   * operation. What this means is that a 'cast' is really just a compile
   * time constraint on the underlying value. In the future, we may add
   * support for run-time casts for compiled tests.
   *
   * To ensure some shred of sanity, we enforce the notion that the
   * type you are casting to may only meaningfully be a narrower type
   * than the underlying declared type. We also invalidate optimizations
   * on bad type casts.
   *
   * @param t The traversal object needed to report errors.
   * @param n The node getting a type assigned to it.
   * @param type The type to be assigned.
   */
Method code:
1677 |   private void ensureTyped(NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType type) {
1678 |     // Make sure FUNCTION nodes always get function type.
1679 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() != Token.FUNCTION ||
1680 |             type instanceof FunctionType ||
1681 |             type.isUnknownType());
1682 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1683 |     if (info != null) {
1684 |       if (info.hasType()) {
1685 |         JSType infoType = info.getType().evaluate(t.getScope(), typeRegistry);
1686 |         validator.expectCanCast(t, n, infoType, type);
1687 |         type = infoType;
1688 |       }
1689 | 
1690 |       if (info.isImplicitCast() && !inExterns) {
1691 |         String propName = n.getType() == Token.GETPROP ?
1692 |             n.getLastChild().getString() : "(missing)";
1693 |         compiler.report(
1694 |             t.makeError(n, ILLEGAL_IMPLICIT_CAST, propName));
1695 |       }
1696 |     }
1697 | 
1698 |     if (n.getJSType() == null) {
1699 |       n.setJSType(type);
1700 |     }
1701 |   }

===== M1003 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferParameterTypes(Node,JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 457-526
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the parameter types from the list of argument names and
   * the doc info.
   */
Method code:
457 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferParameterTypes(@Nullable Node argsParent,
458 |       @Nullable JSDocInfo info) {
459 |     if (argsParent == null) {
460 |       if (info == null) {
461 |         return this;
462 |       } else {
463 |         return inferParameterTypes(info);
464 |       }
465 |     }
466 | 
467 |     // arguments
468 |     Node oldParameterType = null;
469 |     if (parametersNode != null) {
470 |       oldParameterType = parametersNode.getFirstChild();
471 |     }
472 | 
473 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry);
474 |     boolean warnedAboutArgList = false;
475 |     Set<String> allJsDocParams = (info == null) ?
476 |         Sets.<String>newHashSet() :
477 |         Sets.newHashSet(info.getParameterNames());
478 |     boolean foundTemplateType = false;
479 |     for (Node arg : argsParent.children()) {
480 |       String argumentName = arg.getString();
481 |       allJsDocParams.remove(argumentName);
482 | 
483 |       // type from JSDocInfo
484 |       JSType parameterType = null;
485 |       boolean isOptionalParam = isOptionalParameter(arg, info);
486 |       boolean isVarArgs = isVarArgsParameter(arg, info);
487 |       if (info != null && info.hasParameterType(argumentName)) {
488 |         parameterType =
489 |             info.getParameterType(argumentName).evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
490 |       } else if (oldParameterType != null &&
491 |           oldParameterType.getJSType() != null) {
492 |         parameterType = oldParameterType.getJSType();
493 |         isOptionalParam = oldParameterType.isOptionalArg();
494 |         isVarArgs = oldParameterType.isVarArgs();
495 |       } else {
496 |         parameterType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
497 |       }
498 | 
499 |       if (templateTypeName != null &&
500 |           parameterType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined().isTemplateType()) {
501 |         if (foundTemplateType) {
502 |           reportError(TEMPLATE_TYPE_DUPLICATED, fnName);
503 |         }
504 |         foundTemplateType = true;
505 |       }
506 |       warnedAboutArgList |= addParameter(
507 |           builder, parameterType, warnedAboutArgList,
508 |           isOptionalParam,
509 |           isVarArgs);
510 | 
511 |       if (oldParameterType != null) {
512 |         oldParameterType = oldParameterType.getNext();
513 |       }
514 |     }
515 | 
516 |     if (templateTypeName != null && !foundTemplateType) {
517 |       reportError(TEMPLATE_TYPE_EXPECTED, fnName);
518 |     }
519 | 
520 |     for (String inexistentName : allJsDocParams) {
521 |       reportWarning(INEXISTANT_PARAM, inexistentName, fnName);
522 |     }
523 | 
524 |     parametersNode = builder.build();
525 |     return this;
526 |   }

===== M1006 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.getGreatestSubtype(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 612-634
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common
   * getGreatestSubtype implementations.
   */
Method code:
612 |   static JSType getGreatestSubtype(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
613 |     if (thatType.isEmptyType() || thatType.isAllType()) {
614 |       // Defer to the implementations of the end lattice elements when
615 |       // possible.
616 |       return thatType.getGreatestSubtype(thisType);
617 |     } else if (thisType.isUnknownType() || thatType.isUnknownType()) {
618 |       // The greatest subtype with any unknown type is the universal
619 |       // unknown type, unless the two types are equal.
620 |       return thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType) ? thisType :
621 |           thisType.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.UNKNOWN_TYPE);
622 |     } else if (thisType.isSubtype(thatType)) {
623 |       return thisType;
624 |     } else if (thatType.isSubtype(thisType)) {
625 |       return thatType;
626 |     } else if (thisType.isUnionType()) {
627 |       return ((UnionType) thisType).meet(thatType);
628 |     } else if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
629 |       return ((UnionType) thatType).meet(thisType);
630 |     } else if (thisType.isObject() && thatType.isObject()) {
631 |       return thisType.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_OBJECT_TYPE);
632 |     }
633 |     return thisType.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_TYPE);
634 |   }

===== M1016 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.analyze(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 201-229
Documentation:
/**
   * Finds a fixed-point solution. The function has the side effect of replacing
   * the existing node annotations with the computed solutions using {@link
   * com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.GraphNode#setAnnotation(Annotation)}.
   *
   * <p>Initially, each node's input and output flow state contains the value
   * given by {@link #createInitialEstimateLattice()} (with the exception of the
   * entry node of the graph which takes on the {@link #createEntryLattice()}
   * value. Each node will use the output state of its predecessor and compute a
   * output state according to the instruction. At that time, any nodes that
   * depends on the node's newly modified output value will need to recompute
   * their output state again. Each step will perform a computation at one node
   * until no extra computation will modify any existing output state anymore.
   *
   * @param maxSteps Max number of iterations before the method stops and throw
   *        a {@link MaxIterationsExceededException}. This will prevent the
   *        analysis from going into a infinite loop.
   */
Method code:
201 |   final void analyze(int maxSteps) {
202 |     initialize();
203 |     int step = 0;
204 |     while (!orderedWorkSet.isEmpty()) {
205 |       if (step > maxSteps) {
206 |         throw new MaxIterationsExceededException(
207 |           "Analysis did not terminate after " + maxSteps + " iterations");
208 |       }
209 |       DiGraphNode<N, Branch> curNode = orderedWorkSet.iterator().next();
210 |       orderedWorkSet.remove(curNode);
211 |       joinInputs(curNode);
212 |       if (flow(curNode)) {
213 |         // If there is a change in the current node, we want to grab the list
214 |         // of nodes that this node affects.
215 |         List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> nextNodes = isForward() ?
216 |             cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(curNode) :
217 |             cfg.getDirectedPredNodes(curNode);
218 |         for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> nextNode : nextNodes) {
219 |           if (nextNode != cfg.getImplicitReturn()) {
220 |             orderedWorkSet.add(nextNode);
221 |           }
222 |         }
223 |       }
224 |       step++;
225 |     }
226 |     if (isForward()) {
227 |       joinInputs(getCfg().getImplicitReturn());
228 |     }
229 |   }

Method code:
565 |     @Override
566 |     Node processInfixExpression(InfixExpression exprNode) {
567 |       Node n =  newNode(
568 |           transformTokenType(exprNode.getType()),
569 |           transform(exprNode.getLeft()),
570 |           transform(exprNode.getRight()));
571 |       // Set the line number here so we can fine-tune it in ways transform
572 |       // doesn't do.
573 |       n.setLineno(exprNode.getLineno());
574 |       // Position in new ASTNode is to start of expression, but old-fashioned
575 |       // line numbers from Node reference the operator token.  Add the offset
576 |       // to the operator to get the correct character number.
577 |       n.setCharno(position2charno(exprNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
578 |           exprNode.getOperatorPosition()));
579 |       return n;
580 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.CollectProperties.maybeCollectMember(NodeTraversal,Node,Node,Node)

Original range: 1384-1407
Documentation:

Method code:
1384 |       private void maybeCollectMember(NodeTraversal t,
1385 |           Node member, Node nodeWithJsDocInfo, @Nullable Node value) {
1386 |         JSDocInfo info = nodeWithJsDocInfo.getJSDocInfo();
1387 | 
1388 |         // Do nothing if there is no JSDoc type info, or
1389 |         // if the node is not a member expression, or
1390 |         // if the member expression is not of the form: this.someProperty.
1391 |         if (info == null ||
1392 |             member.getType() != Token.GETPROP ||
1393 |             member.getFirstChild().getType() != Token.THIS) {
1394 |           return;
1395 |         }
1396 | 
1397 |         member.getFirstChild().setJSType(thisType);
1398 |         JSType jsType = getDeclaredGetPropType(t, info, member, value);
1399 |         Node name = member.getLastChild();
1400 |         if (jsType != null &&
1401 |             (name.getType() == Token.NAME || name.getType() == Token.STRING)) {
1402 |           thisType.defineDeclaredProperty(
1403 |               name.getString(),
1404 |               jsType,
1405 |               false /* functions with implementations are not in externs */);
1406 |         }
1407 |       }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototype(FunctionPrototypeType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 286-304
Documentation:
/**
   * Sets the prototype.
   * @param prototype the prototype. If this value is {@code null} it will
   *        silently be discarded.
   */
Method code:
286 |   public boolean setPrototype(FunctionPrototypeType prototype) {
287 |     if (prototype == null) {
288 |       return false;
289 |     }
290 |     // getInstanceType fails if the function is not a constructor
291 |     if (isConstructor() && prototype == getInstanceType()) {
292 |       return false;
293 |     }
294 | 
295 |     this.prototype = prototype;
296 | 
297 |     if (isConstructor() || isInterface()) {
298 |       FunctionType superClass = getSuperClassConstructor();
299 |       if (superClass != null) {
300 |         superClass.addSubType(this);
301 |       }
302 |     }
303 |     return true;
304 |   }

===== M1038 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 470-505
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
470 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
471 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
472 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
473 | 
474 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
475 | 
476 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
477 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
478 | 
479 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
480 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
481 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
482 |     }
483 | 
484 |     curNode = n;
485 |     pushScope(n);
486 | 
487 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
488 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
489 |       // scope.
490 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
491 |     }
492 | 
493 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
494 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
495 | 
496 |     // Args
497 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
498 | 
499 |     // Body
500 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
501 |             body.getType() == Token.BLOCK);
502 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
503 | 
504 |     popScope();
505 |   }

===== M1039 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeValidator.expectIndexMatch(NodeTraversal,Node,JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeValidator.java
Original range: 267-283
Documentation:
/**
   * Expect that the first type can be addressed with GETELEM syntax,
   * and that the second type is the right type for an index into the
   * first type.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal.
   * @param n The node to issue warnings on.
   * @param objType The type of the left side of the GETELEM.
   * @param indexType The type inside the brackets of the GETELEM.
   */
Method code:
267 |   void expectIndexMatch(NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType objType,
268 |       JSType indexType) {
269 |     if (objType.isUnknownType()) {
270 |       expectStringOrNumber(t, n, indexType, "property access");
271 |     } else if (objType.toObjectType() != null &&
272 |         objType.toObjectType().getIndexType() != null) {
273 |       expectCanAssignTo(t, n, indexType, objType.toObjectType().getIndexType(),
274 |           "restricted index type");
275 |     } else if (objType.isArrayType()) {
276 |       expectNumber(t, n, indexType, "array access");
277 |     } else if (objType.matchesObjectContext()) {
278 |       expectString(t, n, indexType, "property access");
279 |     } else {
280 |       mismatch(t, n, "only arrays or objects can be accessed",
281 |           objType, typeRegistry.createUnionType(ARRAY_TYPE, OBJECT_TYPE));
282 |     }
283 |   }

489 |     Node processFunctionCall(FunctionCall callNode) {

===== M1045 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 361-376
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
361 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
362 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
363 |       Set<String> directives = null;
364 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
365 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
366 |         if (directives == null) {
367 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
368 |         } else {
369 |           directives.add(directive);
370 |         }
371 |       }
372 | 
373 |       if (directives != null) {
374 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
375 |       }
376 |     }

/**
   * Looks for a type expression at the current token and if found,
   * returns it. Note that this method consumes input.
   *
   * Parameter type expressions are special for two reasons:
   * <ol>
   *   <li>They must begin with '{', to distinguish type names from param names.
   *   <li>They may end in '=', to denote optionality.
   * </ol>
   *
   * @param token The current token.
   * @return The type expression found or null if none.
   */
Method code:

1040 | 
1041 |     Node typeNode = parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(token);
1042 |     int endCharno = stream.getCharno();
1043 |

1045 |         true);

555 |   public Scope getScope() {
556 |     Scope scope = scopes.isEmpty() ? null : scopes.peek();

===== M1075 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 437-465
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
437 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
438 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
439 |     int type = n.getType();
440 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
441 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
442 |     }
443 | 
444 |     curNode = n;
445 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
446 | 
447 |     switch (type) {
448 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
449 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
450 |         break;
451 | 
452 |       default:
453 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
454 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
455 |           // would no longer point to the true next
456 |           Node next = child.getNext();
457 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
458 |           child = next;
459 |         }
460 |         break;
461 |     }
462 | 
463 |     curNode = n;
464 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
465 |   }

517 |   JSType resolveInternal(ErrorReporter t, StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
518 |     setResolvedTypeInternal(this); // for circularly defined types.
519 | 
520 |     boolean changed = false;
521 |     ImmutableList.Builder<JSType> resolvedTypes = ImmutableList.builder();
522 |     for (JSType alternate : alternates) {

===== M1083 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1965-1975
Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
1965 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
1966 |     char LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN = 0x7f;
1967 |     int len = s.length();
1968 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
1969 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
1970 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
1971 |         return false;
1972 |       }
1973 |     }
1974 |     return true;
1975 |   }

   * Determines if the subtree might throw an exception.

893 |   public static boolean mayThrowException(Node n) {
894 |     switch (n.getType()) {
895 |       case Token.CALL:

===== M1100 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SimpleDefinitionFinder.getSimplifiedName(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SimpleDefinitionFinder.java
Original range: 137-149
Documentation:
/**
   * Extract a name from a node.  In the case of GETPROP nodes,
   * replace the namespace or object expression with "this" for
   * simplicity and correctness at the expense of inefficiencies due
   * to higher chances of name collisions.
   *
   * TODO(user) revisit.  it would be helpful to at least use fully
   * qualified names in the case of namespaces.  Might not matter as
   * much if this pass runs after "collapsing properties".
   */
Method code:
137 |   private static String getSimplifiedName(Node node) {
138 |     if (NodeUtil.isName(node)) {
139 |       String name = node.getString();
140 |       if (name != null && !name.isEmpty()) {
141 |         return name;
142 |       } else {
143 |         return null;
144 |       }
145 |     } else if (NodeUtil.isGetProp(node)) {
146 |       return "this." + node.getLastChild().getString();
147 |     }
148 |     return null;
149 |   }

===== M1102 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1570-1609
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeExpression := BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '?' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '!' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '?'
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '!'
   *     | '?'
   */
Method code:
1570 |   private Node parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1571 |     if (token == JsDocToken.QMARK) {
1572 |       // A QMARK could mean that a type is nullable, or that it's unknown.
1573 |       // We use look-ahead 1 to determine whether it's unknown. Otherwise,
1574 |       // we assume it means nullable. There are 5 cases:
1575 |       // {?} - right curly
1576 |       // {?=} - equals
1577 |       // {function(?, number)} - comma
1578 |       // {function(number, ?)} - right paren
1579 |       // {function(): ?|number} - pipe
1580 |       // I'm not a big fan of using look-ahead for this, but it makes
1581 |       // the type language a lot nicer.
1582 |       token = next();
1583 |       if (token == JsDocToken.COMMA ||
1584 |           token == JsDocToken.EQUALS ||
1585 |           token == JsDocToken.RC ||
1586 |           token == JsDocToken.RP ||
1587 |           token == JsDocToken.PIPE) {
1588 |         restoreLookAhead(token);
1589 |         return newNode(Token.QMARK);
1590 |       }
1591 | 
1592 |       return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, parseBasicTypeExpression(token));
1593 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.BANG) {
1594 |       return wrapNode(Token.BANG, parseBasicTypeExpression(next()));
1595 |     } else {
1596 |       Node basicTypeExpr = parseBasicTypeExpression(token);
1597 |       if (basicTypeExpr != null) {
1598 |         if (match(JsDocToken.QMARK)) {
1599 |           next();
1600 |           return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, basicTypeExpr);
1601 |         } else if (match(JsDocToken.BANG)) {
1602 |           next();
1603 |           return wrapNode(Token.BANG, basicTypeExpr);
1604 |         }
1605 |       }
1606 | 
1607 |       return basicTypeExpr;
1608 |     }
1609 |   }

===== M1119 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 157-178
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
157 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
158 |     if (isAllType) {
159 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
160 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
161 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
162 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
163 |       } else {
164 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
165 |       }
166 |     } else {
167 |       int size = alternates.size();
168 |       if (size > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
169 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
170 |       } else if (size > 1) {
171 |         return null;
172 |       } else if (size == 1) {
173 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
174 |       } else {
175 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
176 |       }
177 |     }
178 |   }
